# API action catalog (`vfo-admin-api`)

All actions dispatched by `vfo-admin-api` (PUBLIC/AUTH split + the authoritative **LIVE COUNT (496 in code as of 2026-09-11 — 6 logins + 490 dispatched)**. _2026-09-11 (SpecRev ERT share leg, branch `claude/vfo-session-setup-68295f`): **action count 495 -> 496** — one action ADDED, none renamed or removed. **`specialist_revenue_ert_transfer`** (`actions/specialist-revenue/ert-transfer.ts`) is **AUTH**, in **`SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`**, and deliberately in **NO `TAB_ACTIONS` list** — per #338 the accounting grant is the whole boundary for that tab, so nothing superadmin-only may live there. It is the one-off “Send funds to ERT” catch-up transfer and it is **DB-free**: no row is written, no email drafted, no bell raised — Stripe’s own transfer list is the record. Its row is in the VFO Specialist Revenue table below._ _2026-09-11 (second Stripe account, v829): **action count 494 -> 495** — one action ADDED, none renamed or removed. **`membership_stripe_remap`** (`actions/membership/stripe-remap.ts`) is **AUTH**, in **`SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`**, and deliberately in **NO `TAB_ACTIONS` list** — per #338 the accounting grant is the whole boundary for that tab, so nothing superadmin-only may live there. It is the only member of the `membership_*` family that is not tab-gated, and the only writer allowed to re-stamp `member_payment_plans.stripe_account`. Its row is in the Membership Fees table below._ _2026-09-09 (recurring Growth Credit start date, v820): **action count 493 -> 494** — one action ADDED, none renamed or removed. **`gc_update_subscription_date`** (`actions/gc/update-subscription-date.ts`) is **AUTH**, in **`ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`** and deliberately **NOT** in `MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS`: it is addressed by `subscription_id`, so there is no `member_number` for that list to rewrite, and the admin-only gate is the whole boundary (#455). Its row is in the Growth Credits table below._ _2026-09-04/08 (advisor + accountant onboarding rework, v811-v814): **action count 481 -> 493** — TWELVE actions ADDED, none renamed or removed. Per pipeline (`<P>` = `ADVISOR` | `ACCOUNTANT`): **AUTH**, in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` — `automation_<P>_meetingreminder` (`actions/<p>/meeting-reminder.ts`), `automation_<P>_depositemail` (`deposit-email.ts`), `automation_<P>_depositrefund` (`deposit-refund.ts`); **PUBLIC** — `automation_<P>_depositconfirmation` (`deposit-confirmation.ts`), `automation_<P>_chargebalance` (`charge-balance.ts`). Plus two shared **PUBLIC** actions: `automation_ONBOARDING_MEETING_sweep` (`actions/onboarding/meeting-reminder-sweep.ts`, service-role bearer, pg_cron jobid 18 every 5 min) and `automation_ONBOARDING_meetingresponse` (`actions/onboarding/meeting-response.ts`, token page `/onboarding-meeting`, idempotent on `meeting_response`). #167 checked: the three AUTH actions per pipeline sit on the same admin-only surface as the onboarding steps beside them. Full flow: [../flows/advisor-accountant-onboarding.md](../flows/advisor-accountant-onboarding.md)._ _2026-09-04 (failed-installment retry link, v808): **action count 480 -> 481** — one action ADDED. **`accounting_redraft_installment_link`** is **AUTH**, in **`TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** only (NOT `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, NOT `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`) — the same gate as the two neighbours it sits between on the Outstanding Payment Links panel. #167 checked: it IS behind a tab-gated surface and IS in `TAB_ACTIONS`. Nothing renamed or removed._ _2026-08-27 (recurring Growth Credit services, v797): **action count 477 → 480** — three actions ADDED. **`gc_load_subscriptions`** and **`gc_cancel_subscription`** are **AUTH**, both in **`MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS`**; that list rewrites only `body.member_number`, so cancel's `.eq("member_number", …)` on the UPDATE **is** its ownership guard (#455) and is commented as such at the site. **`automation_GC_recurring_sweep`** is **PUBLIC**, behind the same service-role `Authorization` check as `automation_GROWTH_overdue_sweep`. No `TAB_ACTIONS` mirroring for any of the three (#167 checked, not applicable). Nothing renamed or removed._ _2026-08-27 (corporate-member lead link + the member client Vault tab, v796): **action count 473 → 477** — four actions ADDED, all **AUTH**. **`member_stripe_link_lead`** is in **`ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`**. The other three — **`member_client_vault_list` / `member_client_vault_download` / `member_client_vault_upload_url`** — are deliberately in **NO gate list at all**, which is correct here and not the #309 hole it usually is: `ADMIN_ONLY` would 403 the member surface they exist for and `MEMBER_SCOPED` only rewrites a `body.member_number` none of them reads (#455). Their confinement is the in-handler `denyIfNotOwnClient`, first statement of each. No `TAB_ACTIONS` mirroring needed for any of the four (#167 checked, not applicable). Nothing renamed or removed. Same session, **no count effect**: `msm_load_clients` + `msm_load_training_progress` gained `denyIfNotOwnEnrollment` (an IDOR fix, #455) and `member_payments_load` / `member_stripe_connect_request` / `connect_setup_link` gained borrowed-account guards (#454)._ _2026-08-26 (first-class "Stopped" state + the tax two-slot allocation, v793): **action count 470 → 473** — three actions ADDED, all **AUTH**, all named in **`ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`** and **none** tab-gated (so `ADMIN_ONLY` is the whole boundary and no `TAB_ACTIONS` entry needs mirroring — #167 checked, not applicable), all registered in `router/dispatch.ts`: **`map1_update_status`** (`actions/pipeline/map1-update-status.ts`, body `{pipeline_id, status:'live'|'stopped'}`) — the MAP 1 track-header Live/Stopped toggle; it mirrors `msm_update_tax_status`'s gate **minus the planner allowance**, because MAP 1 has no planner portal. **`advisor_update_status`** (`actions/advisor/update-status.ts`) and **`accountant_update_status`** (`actions/accountant/update-status.ts`), body `{onboarding_id, status:'active'|'stopped'}`, returning the refreshed row. Each writes ONE column and 400s any other value — a deliberate positive allowlist, since these are bare `text` with no CHECK (#431). Nothing renamed or removed. Same session, **no count effect**: `onboarding/update.ts` (the specialist toggle's existing action) gained a `{active,stopped,completed}` status allowlist; `tax_allocate_planner` was reworked to write **one of two slots chosen by BODY KEY** (`tax_planner_id` **or** `tax_team_member_id`, never both — 400; a missing key still reads as the planner slot, so every pre-existing caller is unchanged) with role/slot enforcement, a state-based bell chain and a planner-slot-only withheld-share release; `tax_planner_portal_clients` gained `team_member_id`/`team_member_name` and an `.or()` over both slots. See the "Advisor / Accountant onboarding" section below. **2026-07-10 (Growth Credits / Notifications page / notes visibility, v580–v584): +9 actions** — `gc_load_accounting` (ADMIN_ONLY + TAB_ACTIONS.accounting; Accounting > Members > Growth Credits totals/ledger/balances); `load_notifications_page` + `mark_notifications_read` (ADMIN_ONLY, every admin — the full-page bell view: scope current|archive, kind all|action|fyi|reminder, paginated; bulk clear is dismissible+recipient-scoped); `reminder_create`/`reminder_load`/`reminder_delete` (ADMIN_ONLY, scoped to session email — personal reminders, `fire_at` via `utils/timezone.ts zonedTimeToUtc`); PUBLIC service-role sweeps `automation_REMINDER_sweep` (5-min cron jobid 14; DIRECT `notifications` insert — the documented #176 exception) + `automation_NOTIFICATIONS_purge` (daily cron jobid 15; deletes READ rows >90d); `client_notes_load_shared` (member-facing, `denyIfNotOwnClient` — shared client notes only). Same session, changed contracts (no new actions): `gc_create_checkout` (now MEMBER_SCOPED; server-side `GC_PACKAGES` pricing; `payment_method` ach|card; sandbox-aware via the `GROWTH_CREDITS` config row; origin-derived return URLs), `gc_redeem` (MEMBER_SCOPED; inserts `status='pending'`; spend bell), `gc_load_services` (admin-only sandbox_config + include_inactive), `gc_add_credits`/`gc_manage_service`/`gc_update_redemption`/`gc_load_all_redemptions` (previously UNGATED → ADMIN_ONLY; the queue pair also TAB_ACTIONS.growth_credits), and the client-notes/program-notes/meeting/coaching handlers gained `visibility` with role-filtered/redacting loaders (gotchas #210–#214). count 386→395. **2026-07-09 (Recurring Specialist Revenue, v569): +6 actions** in `actions/specialist-revenue/` — `specialist_revenue_recurring_create` (SUPERADMIN) + `specialist_revenue_recurring_load` (SUPERADMIN) + `specialist_revenue_recurring_cancel` (SUPERADMIN) + PUBLIC `specialist_revenue_recurring_pay_load` + `specialist_revenue_recurring_checkout` (`/specialist-revenue-pay?kind=recurring` subscription setup) + chained PUBLIC `specialist_revenue_recurring_confirmationemail`. Two new deny-all tables `specialist_revenue_recurring_plans` (split snapshot + `charge_day` 1-20 + `stripe_customer_id`/`subscription_id` + `setup_token` + `status` + `last_invoice_id` idempotency cursor) + `specialist_revenue_recurring_lines`; `specialist_revenue_requests` gained nullable `recurring_plan_id` + `stripe_invoice_id` (unique partial index); new SECURITY-DEFINER RPC `claim_specrev_recurring_invoice(plan_id, invoice_id, paid_at)` (service_role-only atomic per-invoice claim, mirror of `claim_specialist_license_invoice`); migration `20260708150000_specialist_revenue_recurring.sql`. count 375→381. **2026-07-08 (Growth Plan 4-Ways/hub + accountant variant, v560): +1 action** — `growth_plan_save_four_ways` (AUTH, **ADMIN_ONLY**, `actions/growth/save-four-ways.ts`) merge-upserts the member-keyed `growth_plan_four_ways` setup row (Get Started MSM + 4-Ways answers) and syncs the MSM to the current score row (gotcha #191); `growth_plan_load` now also returns `four_ways`. No other new actions — `notify.ts`/`overdue-sweep.ts` gained the Growth-notification deep-link (`?member=&feature=gp_onepage`) + `notifyPlanCompleted`→`GROWTH_plan_completed` rule (`save-accountability.ts`), and the entire **accountant Growth Plan variant is FRONTEND-only** (generic storage — no backend/DB change). **2026-06-29 (Strategic Members + member payout setup): +3 actions** — `strategic_groups_load` + `strategic_group_add` (ADMIN_ONLY, new `actions/strategic/`; live v505) and `member_stripe_connect_request` (ADMIN_ONLY, `actions/members/stripe-connect-request.ts`; built, ships v506). New deny-all table `strategic_member_groups`; `members_member_category_check` widened for `strategic_member` (gotcha #155); `email_templates` row `MEMBER_PAYOUT/member_connect_setup`. count 341→344. **2026-06-23 (Payment-Continuation migration tool, v503): +6 actions** in new `actions/migration/` — `migration_stripe_lookup` (read-only saved-card lookup), `migration_backfill_map1` + `migration_backfill_tax` (preview + idempotent upsert of an in-flight plan: reflect-paid-inert + schedule-remaining; dedupe MAP1 by `client_id`, tax by `client_id`+`program_id`), `migration_send_setup_link` (create Stripe customer + draft `/connect-card` Gmail) — **all SUPERADMIN_ONLY** — plus PUBLIC `migration_connect_load` + `migration_connect_checkout` (the `/connect-card` token page; mints a `mode:setup` session stamped `payment_kind=card_update`+`pipeline`+`row_id` so the EXISTING card_update webhook activates the dormant row). New deny-all table `migration_setup_tokens`; marker cols `legacy_migrated_at`/`legacy_source` on `pmap1`+`client_tax_plans`. Reuses the chargescheduled sweep, revshare, charge-implementation, and the card_update webhook. **2026-07-15 (v608): `card_fee_waived`** — both backfills stamp it in `setup_link` mode; the sweep / charge-implementation / card_update-webhook fee recompute / Payments-tab display all charge+show base (no card gross-up) when set; download re-checks the `document_shares` grant row owner + `revoked_at` before a 300s signed URL). The prior session added `save_advisor_team_member` (AUTH/admin) + `save_accountant_team_member` (AUTH/admin) — the Stage-1 "Team Member Responsible" step — deployed **v462**. The catalog cites file paths (not line numbers).

> **The nine token DECISION actions are unchanged, but their pages no longer call them on load (2026-07-27, gotcha #290).** `automation_PCADMIN_finaldecision`, `automation_TAX_finaldecision`, `automation_TAX_postreviewclientdecision`, `automation_TAX_implementfinaldecision`, `automation_ADVISOR_clientdecision`, `automation_ACCOUNTANT_clientdecision`, `automation_PFT_ftresponse`, `automation_PFT_undecided_response` and `automation_SPECIALIST_revsharefinal` keep identical names, request bodies, tokens and responses. What changed is purely frontend: each `/…-decide` page now renders a confirmation card and POSTs only when the recipient clicks it, because corporate email link-scanners were executing the old on-mount POST. Existing emailed links keep working. See [02-frontend-shell.md](02-frontend-shell.md#token-link-ux-pages).

Format: action · `file` · tables read / written · chains / external. Table prefix `pipeline_map1` is shortened to `pmap1` and `pipeline_sandbox_config` to `psbx_cfg` for brevity. All file references are relative to `C:\vfo-edge-functions\supabase\functions\vfo-admin-api\`.

> **Pre-refactor count.** v194 had 130 dispatch references. Phase 6 mechanical removed two: the duplicate `msm_update_client` registration (always unreachable) and the dead `automation_CONTRACT_stripewebhook` handler (doubly unreachable). After that the total was 128. Subsequent feature/refactor work added the remaining handlers (revshare sweep, sandbox-config save, chargescheduled sweep, check-payment flow, the entire `actions/tax/` module, the Tax 4/5 redesign and Tax Planning program_id=4 alignment, 22 handlers across the new `actions/advisor/` module for Advisor Onboarding Phases 1-6 + login setup + admin automation loader + team-member step, 14 handlers for the PIP Meetings purchase chain, and 23 handlers across the new `actions/accountant/` module for Accountant Onboarding + Partnership? + team-member steps). **Do not trust a hard-coded total on this page — derive it.** The parity gate is `logins + dispatch registrations`, where dispatch = the count of `"<action>": (c) =>` registrations in `router/dispatch.ts`. As of **v748 (2026-08-16) that is 6 logins + 457 dispatched = 463** (the stale figure previously written here, "5 + 405 = 410", was the v601 snapshot).

## Webhooks (header / body-shape gated, no `action` field)

| Trigger | File | R | W | Chains |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Stripe webhook (`stripe-signature` header) — `checkout.session.completed` (GC) | `router/webhooks.ts` | `gc_balances` | `gc_balances`, `gc_transactions` | — |
| Stripe webhook — `checkout.session.completed` (MAP1 first payment) | `router/webhooks.ts` | `pmap1`, `psbx_cfg` | `pmap1` (pay1_status, payment_method_type, acct_last4, card_processing_fee, pay1-4_date, confirmation_status) | `automation_CONTRACT_confirmationemail` (BOTH methods — the card gate is inside that handler, which also owns the PF bell / ERT copy / Tracy email; a card gets no client email and `confirmation_status='Skipped - Card (Receipt Only)'`), `automation_CONTRACT_invoicereceipt` (card only) · Stripe `GET /v1/payment_intents/{id}` |
| Stripe webhook — `payment_intent.succeeded` | `router/webhooks.ts` | `pmap1`, `client_tax_plans` (fallback if MAP1 miss) | `pmap1` (`payN_status`) OR `client_tax_plans` (`retainer_status='succeeded'` if ACH cleared) | `automation_CONTRACT_invoicereceipt` OR `automation_TAX_invoicereceipt` |
| Stripe webhook — `checkout.session.completed` (Tax retainer, after MAP1 miss) | `router/webhooks.ts` | `client_tax_plans`, `psbx_cfg`(pipeline=`TAX`) | `client_tax_plans` (retainer_status, payment_method_type, acct_last4, card_processing_fee, retainer_date, retainer_payment_intent_id, retainer_confirmation_status='Confirmation Needed') | `automation_TAX_confirmationemail` (BOTH methods — the card gate is inside that handler, which also owns the PF bell + ERT vault copy; a card gets no client email and `retainer_confirmation_status='Skipped - Card (Receipt Only)'`) + `automation_TAX_invoicereceipt` (card only) · Stripe `GET /v1/payment_intents/{id}` |
| BoldSign webhook (embedded — `body.event.eventType`) | `router/webhooks.ts` | `pmap1`, `client_tax_plans`, `advisor_onboarding`, `accountant_onboarding` (fallback chain — MAP1 → tax → advisor → accountant) | `pmap1` (c17/c18) OR `client_tax_plans` (client_signed, ceo_signed) OR `advisor_onboarding` (agreement_signed_by_advisor_at, agreement_signed_by_ceo_at) OR `accountant_onboarding` (agreement_signed_by_accountant_at, agreement_signed_by_ceo_at) | **Tax branch chains** `automation_TAX_ceocountersign` (client signed) + `automation_TAX_stripecustomer` (Completed). **Advisor branch chains** `automation_ADVISOR_ceocountersign` (advisor signed) + `automation_ADVISOR_stripecustomer` (Completed). **Accountant branch chains** `automation_ACCOUNTANT_ceocountersign` (accountant signed) + `automation_ACCOUNTANT_stripecustomer` (Completed). MAP1 branch unchanged. Returns `{ok, branch:'<tax\|advisor\|accountant>'}`. |
| BoldSign webhook (standalone — `boldsign-webhook` function, separate URL) | `boldsign-webhook/index.ts` | same four tables | same as above | same chain pattern incl. advisor + accountant branches. Live URL per BoldSign dashboard config. **MUST deploy with `--no-verify-jwt`.** |
| Stripe webhook — `checkout.session.completed` (Advisor onboarding, after MAP1 + Tax miss) | `router/webhooks.ts` | `advisor_onboarding`, `psbx_cfg`(pipeline=`ADVISOR_ONBOARDING`) | `advisor_onboarding` (payment_status, payment_method_type, acct_last4, card_processing_fee, stripe_payment_intent_id, payment_completed_at, renewal_date) | ONE action selected by method: card → `automation_ADVISOR_invoicereceipt` (already cleared — documents now, no confirmation); ACH → `automation_ADVISOR_confirmationemail` (documents follow at `payment_intent.succeeded`). Routes by `metadata.pipeline='ADVISOR_ONBOARDING'` + `metadata.payment_kind='onboarding'`. Stripe `GET /v1/payment_intents/{id}` for method+last4. |
| Stripe webhook — `checkout.session.completed` (Accountant onboarding, after MAP1 + Tax + Advisor + PIP miss) | `router/webhooks.ts` | `accountant_onboarding`, `psbx_cfg`(pipeline=`ACCOUNTANT_ONBOARDING`) | `accountant_onboarding` (payment_status, payment_method_type, acct_last4, card_processing_fee, stripe_payment_intent_id, payment_completed_at, renewal_date) | ONE action selected by method: card → `automation_ACCOUNTANT_invoicereceipt`; ACH → `automation_ACCOUNTANT_confirmationemail`. Routes by `metadata.pipeline='ACCOUNTANT_ONBOARDING'` + `metadata.payment_kind='onboarding'`. Same shape as advisor branch — uses `computeAccountantRenewalDate` (clone of advisor's algorithm). |
| Stripe webhook — `payment_intent.succeeded` (Accountant ACH cleared) | `router/webhooks.ts` | `accountant_onboarding` | `accountant_onboarding` (payment_status='succeeded', payment_completed_at, card_processing_fee, renewal_date) | `automation_ACCOUNTANT_invoicereceipt` **directly** — the funds have settled, so this is where the documents go out; the ACH confirmation already went out at checkout time and the confirmation handler no longer chains the receipt (gotcha #289). Branched by `pi.metadata.pipeline === 'ACCOUNTANT_ONBOARDING'`. |
| Stripe webhook — `checkout.session.completed` + `mode==='setup'` + `metadata.payment_kind==='card_update'` (Phase D admin-initiated card/bank change) | `router/webhooks.ts` | session `metadata` (`pipeline`, `row_id`) + the new default PM off the completed SetupIntent | `pipeline_map1.default_payment_method_id` OR `client_tax_plans.default_payment_method_id` OR `specialist_onboarding.lic_default_payment_method_id` (saves the newly-entered method as the engagement's default for its next off-session charge) | — Token-page flow minted by `payments_cardupdate_checkout`. No charge — `mode:'setup'` only. |
| Daily pg_cron — `advisor-sweep-daily` at 05:00 UTC | `pg_cron` + `pg_net.http_post` → POST `vfo-admin-api` action `automation_ADVISOR_sweep` | (see action row in Advisor Phase 6 section below) | (see action row) | Reminder ladder (3 stalls × 2+4 **business** days) + 14-**calendar**-day implicit-No on advisor onboarding. Setup: `supabase/cron/advisor-sweep.sql`. |
| Daily pg_cron — `accountant-sweep-daily` at 06:00 UTC | `pg_cron` + `pg_net.http_post` → POST `vfo-admin-api` action `automation_ACCOUNTANT_sweep` | (see Accountant Onboarding section below) | (see action row) | Same 3-stall reminder ladder (business days) + 14-**calendar**-day implicit-No pattern as advisor, against `accountant_onboarding`. Setup: `supabase/cron/accountant-sweep.sql`. |

## Public-token automation (`PUBLIC_HANDLERS` in `router/dispatch.ts`)

These sit BEFORE the auth gate. Triggered either by user-facing token links (`/decide`, `/pay`) or by server-to-server chain calls authenticated via `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`.

| Action | File | R | W | Chains / external |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `connect_setup_link` (2026-07-23) | `actions/payouts/connect-setup-link.ts` | `connect_setup_tokens`(by token), `members`/`experts`/`strategic_member_groups`/`tax_planning_groups`(by entity → `stripe_account_id`), `psbx_cfg`(MAP 1) | `connect_setup_tokens.last_used_at` (fire-and-forget) | Backs the public `/payout-setup?token=` page for the 4 "Set Up Payment Details" emails. Mints a **fresh** Stripe `POST /v1/account_links` (`type=account_onboarding`) per click; `refresh_url` loops back to the same page, `return_url` = `/payout-setup?done=1`. **Since v693 it also sends `collection_options[fields]=eventually_due`** so the hosted page collects the full set-for-life requirements (SSN/DOB) rather than only what is currently blocking — without it a half-onboarded account is shown a complete page and later caps at $3,000 lifetime payouts (gotcha #317). Friendly generic errors (404 invalid token/entity, 410 on a Stripe read failure); real Stripe error `console.log`ged WITHOUT the token. Replaces the single-use raw `connect.stripe.com` links the creators used to embed. Gotcha #268. **GUARDED 2026-08-27 / v796 (gotcha #454):** for a `member` entity whose `stripe_account_id` is BORROWED from their lead (`members.stripe_account_linked_from` set), it returns the **generic 404** (reason `console.log`ged, never leaked). **This is the half that actually matters:** the token is durable and already sits in the member's inbox, so guarding only the admin setup-email button would have left a live route into the LEAD's Stripe account open. |
| `automation_PCADMIN_finaldecision` | `actions/pipeline/pcadmin-final-decision.ts` | `pmap1`(by c15_token), `clients`, `members`, `psbx_cfg`, `email_templates` | `pmap1`(c15_final_decision, c15_service_level), `notifications` | Gmail draft (decline email if "No") |
| `automation_CONTRACT_ceocountersign` | `actions/pipeline/contract-ceo-countersign.ts` | `pmap1`, `clients`, `psbx_cfg`, `email_templates` | — | BoldSign `GET /v1/document/getEmbeddedSignLink` (3 retries) · Gmail draft to CEO |
| `automation_CONTRACT_stripecustomer` | `actions/pipeline/contract-stripe-customer.ts` | `pmap1`, `clients`, `psbx_cfg` | `pmap1`(stripe_customer_id, checkout_token) | Stripe `POST /v1/customers` · `automation_CONTRACT_paymentemail` |

### Advisor Onboarding payment chain (Phase 4 — added 2026-05-26)

All 6 handlers PUBLIC. Trigger graph: BoldSign Completed (advisor branch in embedded + standalone webhooks) → `_stripecustomer` → `_paymentemail` → client clicks link → `/advisor-pay` page → `_loadpayment` (on mount) → `_stripecheckout` (on Pay click) → Stripe Checkout → Stripe webhook routes to advisor branch → `_confirmationemail` → `_invoicereceipt`. Stripe metadata convention: `pipeline='ADVISOR_ONBOARDING'`, `payment_kind='onboarding'`. Payment amount is DYNAMIC ($4,000 / $4,600 / $8,000 / $8,600) based on which BoldSign checkboxes the advisor ticked (vfo_ft / pft / corporate); computed in Phase 3's `ceo-countersign.ts` and persisted to `advisor_onboarding.payment_amount`. `setup_future_usage=off_session` on the PaymentIntent so the card is saved for the 6-month renewal review (no auto-renew cron yet).

**Deposit + balance leg added 2026-09-04 (v811).** `automation_ADVISOR_depositconfirmation` and `automation_ADVISOR_chargebalance` are PUBLIC and join this chain; `automation_ADVISOR_meetingreminder`, `_depositemail` and `_depositrefund` are **AUTH / `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`** and are listed with the Stage-1 admin actions. `_stripecustomer` no longer early-returns on an existing customer — it FORKS on `deposit_status` (`succeeded` → `_chargebalance`; `processing` → park `balance_charge_status='awaiting_deposit'`; otherwise the original link route). `_loadpayment` / `_stripecheckout` resolve `checkout_token` first and then `deposit_checkout_token`, and every fee base nets a settled deposit off `payment_amount`.

|---|---|---|---|---|

| `automation_ADVISOR_paymentemail` | `actions/advisor/payment-email.ts` | `advisor_onboarding`, `psbx_cfg`, `email_templates`(`ADVISOR_payment_link`) | `advisor_onboarding.payment_link_sent_at` | Gmail draft to advisor with `/advisor-pay?token=<>` link + plan list + total amount + `Engagement Term: 6 months`. |
| `automation_ADVISOR_loadpayment` | `actions/advisor/load-payment.ts` | `advisor_onboarding`(by checkout_token) | — | Returns `advisor_name`, `payment_amount`, `selected_plans` for the `/advisor-pay` page header. |
| `automation_ADVISOR_stripecheckout` | `actions/advisor/stripe-checkout.ts` | `advisor_onboarding`(by checkout_token), `psbx_cfg` | `advisor_onboarding`(payment_method_type) | Stripe `POST /v1/checkout/sessions` for `payment_amount` (one-time, mode=payment, `setup_future_usage=off_session`). Product name + PI description "Advisor Onboarding - <name>". Metadata: `pipeline='ADVISOR_ONBOARDING'`, `payment_kind='onboarding'`, `onboarding_id`, `checkout_token`, `payment_method_type`. Returns Stripe URL. |

The `\|card` template still exists but is no longer sent automatically. **Chains nothing** — as of 2026-07-26 it no longer chains `automation_ADVISOR_invoicereceipt`; the webhook owns receipt sequencing on every path (card at checkout, ACH at `payment_intent.succeeded`). Numbering: `INV-ADV{onboarding_id}-{seq:0004}` / `REC-ADV{onboarding_id}-{seq:0004}` where seq is the GLOBAL `document_numbers` counter shared with MAP1/Tax. html2pdf.app (×2) · Gmail draft with both PDFs attached. |

### Advisor Onboarding member-portal login setup (added 2026-05-28)

3 PUBLIC handlers chained from Stage 3 Create Advisor: after `automation_ADVISOR_createmember` writes the `members` row, it generates a 14-day single-use `login_setup_token` on `advisor_onboarding` and chains `automation_ADVISOR_loginsetupemail`. The advisor receives a Gmail draft with a `/member-setup?token=<>` button, lands on `MemberSetupPage` (calls `_loadloginsetup` on mount), picks a passcode (`_submitloginsetup` on submit), then redirects to `/member/login` with email pre-filled. Email pipeline `ADVISOR_ONBOARDING`; template `ADVISOR_login_setup` (12th advisor template). New columns on `advisor_onboarding`: `login_setup_token`, `login_setup_token_expires_at`, `login_setup_email_sent_at`, `login_setup_completed_at` (all nullable). UNIQUE partial index on `login_setup_token` where not null.

Idempotent on `login_setup_email_sent_at`. Subject: "Elite Resource Team / VFO Services - Set Up Your Member Portal Login - [Advisor Name]". |
| `automation_ADVISOR_loadloginsetup` | `actions/advisor/load-login-setup.ts` | `advisor_onboarding`(by login_setup_token), `member_logins`(by email) | — | Returns `{state: 'ready'\|'already_setup'\|'invalid', advisor_name, email, member_number}`. `already_setup` if a `member_logins` row exists for this email OR `login_setup_completed_at` is set. `invalid` on token-not-found OR token-expired. |
| `automation_ADVISOR_submitloginsetup` | `actions/advisor/submit-login-setup.ts` | `advisor_onboarding`(by login_setup_token), `member_logins`(by email for race-check) | `member_logins`(insert with salted PBKDF2 `passcode_hash`, `member_number` from onboarding row), `advisor_onboarding.login_setup_completed_at` | Validates token + expiry + passcode (min 6 chars, matches confirm). Returns `{success: true, email}` so the frontend redirects to `/member/login` with email pre-filled. |

### Accountant Onboarding chain (added 2026-05-28 — cloned from Advisor + Partnership? step)

25 handlers in `actions/accountant/*.ts` (cloned from advisor with `save-partnership.ts` + `save-team-member.ts` + `load-advisors.ts` + `save-cc-advisor.ts` added). Token-swap convention: every advisor identifier → accountant equivalent (e.g. `automation_ADVISOR_decision` → `automation_ACCOUNTANT_decision`, table `advisor_onboarding` → `accountant_onboarding`, column `agreement_signed_by_advisor_at` → `agreement_signed_by_accountant_at`). 14 PUBLIC chain-callable + 10 AUTH (admin-triggered) + 1 token-page chain (`_clientdecision`). Trigger graph mirrors advisor exactly with an inserted "Direct or Advisor Partnership" step (`save_accountant_partnership`) — plus a conditional "CC Connected Advisor" step on the Advisor branch (`accountant_load_advisors` + `save_accountant_cc_advisor`) — between Stage 1 Prelim Meeting and Stage 1 Decision. Stripe metadata convention: `pipeline='ACCOUNTANT_ONBOARDING'`, `payment_kind='onboarding'`. Payment amount is DYNAMIC ($2,000 / $2,600 / $4,000 / $4,600) based on the partnership choice + corporate add-on; computed in `ceo-countersign.ts` and persisted to `accountant_onboarding.payment_amount`. Same `setup_future_usage=off_session` for 6-month renewal review. RLS now enabled on both `advisor_onboarding` and `accountant_onboarding`.

Diffs from advisor:

- **`save_accountant_partnership`** (AUTH) — new handler with no advisor analog. Body `{ onboarding_id, partnership: 'No accountant partnership' | 'Accountant Partnership' | null }`. Writes `accountant_partnership` + `accountant_partnership_at` on the row. Frontend gates Stage 1 Decision buttons until the partnership choice is saved. **UI relabel (2026-07-13, gotcha #219):** the step is displayed as "Direct or Advisor Partnership" with options **Direct** (= `'No accountant partnership'`) / **Advisor** (= `'Accountant Partnership'`) — the stored values are unchanged. Switching off Advisor also clears the `cc_advisor_*` columns.
- **`accountant_load_advisors`** (AUTH, 2026-07-13) — returns `[{member_number, name, email, elite_status}]` for every `members` row with `member_category='advisor'` and a non-empty email, for the "CC Connected Advisor" searchable picker (Advisor path only).
- **`save_accountant_cc_advisor`** (AUTH, 2026-07-13) — body `{ onboarding_id, member_number|null }`. Looks the advisor up server-side and writes the denormalized `accountant_onboarding.cc_advisor_member_number/name/email/at` (or clears them on null). That email is auto-CC'd on every accountant-facing onboarding email via the new optional `extraCc` param on `utils/email-recipients.ts resolveTemplateRecipients` (wired at decision / decline-email / send-agreement / payment-email / confirmation-email / invoice-receipt / login-setup / sweep; `ceo-countersign` excluded; suppressed in sandbox).
- **`automation_ACCOUNTANT_sendagreement`** — reads `accountant_partnership` from row (returns 400 if NULL) and loads `agreement_templates WHERE pipeline='ACCOUNTANT_ONBOARDING' AND payment_plan = <partnership_value>`. Two `agreement_templates` rows (one per partnership branch). Drops `pft_checkbox` entirely (not in accountant flow); `vfo_ft_checkbox` becomes a standalone required checkbox (no FormGroups validation); `corporate_checkbox` stays as the optional add-on.
- **`automation_ACCOUNTANT_ceocountersign`** — reads `accountant_partnership` from row to compute pricing: `vfo_ft_checkbox` = $4,000 (No partnership) OR $2,000 (Accountant Partnership); `corporate_checkbox` = +$600 either way. Also has a hardened BoldSign field-read parser (multi-key signer lookup with fallback to `signerDetails[0]`, multi-key field-id match, multi-value truthy check, soft-fail with admin notification + diagnostic shape dump on read failure — Anton's countersign email always sends). Same parser fix was retrofitted to `actions/advisor/ceo-countersign.ts` since the advisor handler had a latent bug from launch (`s.order === 1` never matched).
- **`automation_ACCOUNTANT_createmember`** — **`member_type` is PARTNERSHIP-BRANCHED as of 2026-08-04 / v698 (gotcha #329):** `ob.accountant_partnership === "Accountant Partnership"` → `"Implementation - VFO FT (Advisor)"`, anything else → `"Implementation - VFO FT (Direct)"`. It previously hardcoded plain `"Implementation"` — an **advisor-only** value inherited when the accountant pipeline was cloned from the advisor pipeline, and not a value any accountant admin can pick — which typed the first automation-created accountant (Anil Grandhi, member **30006**) wrong; that row was data-fixed by superadmin with a `member_type_history` audit row. **VFO Associate accountants are deliberately unmapped** (the option does not exist yet; a comment in the handler marks the spot). The branch reads the SAME field that picks the BoldSign template and the agreement PDF (#58). **Writes `revenue_decision='Money Mapping'` on the inserted `members` row AND on the `accountant_onboarding` update, as of 2026-08-12 / v730** (gotcha #375) — mirroring the advisor pipeline. It previously wrote nothing there on the theory that accountants have no revenue decision; `accountant_onboarding` already had the column, so no DDL was needed. Member number via category-relative `nextMemberNumber('accountant','New Model')` (gotcha #48 — the old `[30000,90000)` / `[90000,∞)` ranges + the `ACCOUNTANT_TYPES` accountant-only filter are GONE). Writes `members.accountant_onboarding_id` FK + `advisor_model='New Model'` + `member_category='accountant'`. Like the advisor handler, also accepts the 7 `sale_*` body fields (persisted on the onboarding row), fires `sendNewModelSaleEmail()` in both the happy + idempotent paths, and clears the "Ready to create" notification.
- **`automation_ACCOUNTANT_invoicereceipt`** — invoice/receipt format `INV-ACC{onboarding_id}-{seq:0004}` / `REC-ACC{onboarding_id}-{seq:0004}`. The `document_numbers` table got a new `accountant_onboarding_id` FK column for tracking; without it, the original advisor-cloned insert was silently failing in supabase-js.
- **`automation_ACCOUNTANT_sweep`** — same 3-stall (Undecided / signing / payment) × 2-tier (2-business-day reminder + 4-business-day PF notification, all six cutoffs `businessDelayCutoffIso` as of 2026-08-14) + 14-**calendar**-day implicit-No pattern as advisor. Cron at 06:00 UTC. Notifications use `pipeline='ACCOUNTANT_ONBOARDING'` + `link='/admin?tab=accountants&section=accountant_onboarding'`.
- **`automation_load_accountant_pipelines`** (AUTH) — loader for `AccountantAutomationPanel`. Returns rows of `accountant_onboarding` for the admin pipeline view.

Plus 14 more PUBLIC handlers that are direct functional clones (different token names + tables, same shape): `_declineemail`, `_clientdecision`, `_stripecustomer`, `_paymentemail`, `_loadpayment`, `_stripecheckout`, `_confirmationemail`, `_loginsetupemail`, `_loadloginsetup`, `_submitloginsetup`. `automation_ACCOUNTANT_confirmationemail` matches the advisor twin exactly, including the 2026-07-26 policy change: **ACH only**, and it **no longer chains `automation_ACCOUNTANT_invoicereceipt`** (gotcha #289). Plus 9 AUTH handlers: `load_accountant_onboardings`, `load_accountant_onboarding`, `create_accountant_onboarding`, `save_accountant_prelim_meeting`, `automation_ACCOUNTANT_decision`, `save_accountant_partnership`, `save_accountant_team_member`, `accountant_load_advisors`, `save_accountant_cc_advisor`.

### Advisor / Accountant onboarding — meeting reminder, deposit + balance (added 2026-09-04, v811-v814)

Twelve actions, ten of them a strict advisor/accountant mirror (`<P>` = `ADVISOR` | `ACCOUNTANT`, `<p>` = `advisor` | `accountant`, table = `<p>_onboarding`). Full flow: [../flows/advisor-accountant-onboarding.md](../flows/advisor-accountant-onboarding.md).

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `automation_<P>_meetingreminder` | **AUTH**, `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` | `actions/<p>/meeting-reminder.ts` | `<p>_onboarding` | `meeting_date/_time/_timezone`, `meeting_at`, `meeting_reminder_due_at` (one BUSINESS day earlier, `utils/onboarding-meeting.ts reminderDueAt`), `meeting_reminder_token`, `meeting_reminder_scheduled_at` \| `_skipped_at`; a **reschedule additionally NULLs** `meeting_reminder_sent_at` / `_60m_sent_at` / `_10m_sent_at` / `meeting_response` / `meeting_response_at` (#472) | 400 without a Team Member Responsible — the bells route to that person |
| `automation_ONBOARDING_MEETING_sweep` | **PUBLIC**, service-role bearer | `actions/onboarding/meeting-reminder-sweep.ts` | BOTH tables, `status='active'` only | the three `*_sent_at` guards, each only after a successful send | pg_cron **jobid 18** `*/5 * * * *`; templates `<P>_meeting_reminder` / `_60m` / `_10m`, **all six `send_mode=true`** (#325) |
| `automation_ONBOARDING_meetingresponse` | **PUBLIC**, token | `actions/onboarding/meeting-response.ts` | `<p>_onboarding` by `meeting_reminder_token` | `meeting_response` (`confirm`\|`cancel`\|`reschedule`), `meeting_response_at` — **idempotent**, later clicks return `existing_response` | page `/onboarding-meeting`; FYI bells `<P>_meeting_confirmed` / `_cancelled` / `_reschedule` → `TEAM_MEMBER` |
| `automation_<P>_depositemail` | **AUTH**, `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` | `actions/<p>/deposit-email.ts` | `<p>_onboarding`, `psbx_cfg`, `email_templates`(`<P>_deposit_payment_link`) | `deposit_amount` (**$500-$4,000**, 400 outside), `deposit_checkout_token`, `deposit_email_sent_at`, `stripe_customer_id` | **creates the Stripe customer EARLY if missing**; Gmail draft |
| `automation_<P>_depositconfirmation` | **PUBLIC** | `actions/<p>/deposit-confirmation.ts` | `<p>_onboarding`, `email_templates`(`<P>_deposit_received`) | `deposit_confirmation_email_sent_at` | chained from `checkout.session.completed` on **BOTH card and ACH** — a documented exception to #287 |
| `automation_<P>_chargebalance` | **PUBLIC** | `actions/<p>/charge-balance.ts` | `<p>_onboarding` | `balance_payment_intent_id`, `balance_charge_status`, `balance_card_fee`, `balance_charge_date`; on decline `payment_status='declined'` + a fresh link | off-session PI on the deposit's saved method, card grossed up `(base + 0.30) / 0.971`, metadata `payment_kind='onboarding_balance'`, idempotency `adv-balance-<id>-<pm>-<date>` / `acct-balance-…` (#228). `amountDue <= 0` → paid-in-full, no PI. Failure bell `<P>_balance_charge_failed` (action-required) |
| `automation_<P>_depositrefund` | **AUTH**, `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` | `actions/<p>/deposit-refund.ts` | `<p>_onboarding` | `deposit_refund_id/_amount/_date/_status`, `deposit_status='refunded'`, **`status='stopped'`**, `deposit_refund_email_sent_at` | Stripe refund **in full**; drafts `<P>_deposit_refund` with a required `[Refund Reason]`; bell `<P>_deposit_refunded`. Refuses once `payment_status` is `succeeded`/`processing` |

### PIP Meetings purchase chain (added 2026-05-27 — Phases 1-6 + Automation Panel)

All 7 PUBLIC. Trigger graph: Admin submits Phase 3 "Purchase Additional Services" form (auth context) → kicks off `_stripecustomer` → `_paymentemail` → client clicks `/pip-pay` link → `_loadpayment` (on mount) → `_stripecheckout` (on Pay click) → Stripe Checkout → Stripe webhook routes by `metadata.pipeline='PIP'` → `_confirmationemail` → `_invoicereceipt` → `_revshare`. Stripe metadata convention: `pipeline='PIP'`, `payment_kind='purchase'`. All payments are 1-time only (no quarterly, no `setup_future_usage`). State lives on `client_priority_tracks` rows where `track_type='pip'` and `pip_purchase_kind IS NOT NULL`. See [../flows/pip-meetings.md](../flows/pip-meetings.md).

| Action | File | R | W | Chains / external |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `automation_PIP_stripecustomer` | `actions/msm/pip-stripe-customer.ts` | `client_priority_tracks` (by id, track_type='pip'), `clients`, `psbx_cfg`(MAP 1) | `client_priority_tracks`(pip_stripe_customer_id, pip_checkout_token, pip_payment_status='pending') | Stripe `POST /v1/customers` (metadata.pipeline='PIP', metadata.priority_track_id) · chains `automation_PIP_paymentemail`. Idempotent on `pip_stripe_customer_id`. |
| `automation_PIP_paymentemail` | `actions/msm/pip-payment-email.ts` | `client_priority_tracks`, `clients`, `psbx_cfg`, `email_templates`(pipeline='PIP', name='PIP_payment') | `client_priority_tracks.pip_payment_email_sent_at` | Gmail draft to client with `/pip-pay?token=...` link + `[Purchase Description]` ("Tax Planning" or "N additional PIP meetings") + `[Payment Amount]`. |
| `automation_PIP_loadpayment` | `actions/msm/pip-load-payment.ts` | `client_priority_tracks`(by pip_checkout_token), `clients` | — | Returns `client_name`, `service_level` (= purchase description), `payment_amount`, `payment_x='1'`, `payment_y='1'`. |
| `automation_PIP_stripecheckout` | `actions/msm/pip-stripe-checkout.ts` | `client_priority_tracks`(by pip_checkout_token), `psbx_cfg` | — | Stripe `POST /v1/checkout/sessions` for `pip_purchase_amount` (one-time, mode=payment, NO `setup_future_usage`). Card path grosses up to absorb 2.9% + $0.30 fee. Metadata: `pipeline='PIP'`, `payment_kind='purchase'`, `priority_track_id`, `client_id`, `checkout_token`. Returns Stripe URL. |
| `automation_PIP_confirmationemail` | `actions/msm/pip-confirmation-email.ts` | `client_priority_tracks`, `clients`, `psbx_cfg`, `email_templates`(name='PIP_confirmation') | `client_priority_tracks.pip_confirmation_email_sent_at` | Gmail draft to client — **ACH ONLY** (the webhook's `isCardP` branch chains it only on the ACH path; the gate is at the call site because this handler owns nothing but the email). A card purchase leaves `pip_confirmation_email_sent_at` NULL forever and the admin panels render that step as "skipped". Substitutes `[CARD_FEE_TEXT]` and `[PROCESSING_TIME]`. Idempotent. |
| `automation_PIP_invoicereceipt` | `actions/msm/pip-invoice-receipt.ts` | `client_priority_tracks`, `clients`, `psbx_cfg`, `email_templates`(name='PIP_invoicereceipt_email'), `document_numbers` | `client_priority_tracks`(pip_invoice_number, pip_receipt_number, pip_invoice_drive_id, pip_receipt_drive_id, pip_invoice_receipt_email_sent_at), `document_numbers`(insert) | `utils/pip-html-templates.ts` (`generatePipInvoiceHTML` + `generatePipReceiptHTML` — single-row Payment Schedule, no Service Level, "Purchase Details" box). html2pdf.app (×2) · Google Drive (find/create folder, upload ×2) · Gmail draft with both PDFs attached. Numbering: `INV-PIP-{clientRef}-{seq:0004}` / `REC-PIP-{clientRef}-{seq:0004}` from shared global counter. Idempotent. |
| `automation_PIP_revshare` | `actions/msm/pip-revshare.ts` | `client_priority_tracks`, `clients`, `members`, `psbx_cfg` | `client_priority_tracks`(pip_rev_share_status, pip_rev_share_transfer_id, pip_rev_share_amount, pip_rev_share_completed_at, pip_rev_member_email_sent_at), and on success of additional_pip purchases: `client_priority_tracks` UPDATE `pip_paid=true` WHERE `pip_purchased_from_track_id=<source>` (unlocks locked child meetings). | Stripe `POST /v1/transfers` to `members.stripe_account_id` for Revenue Share members (skipped for Money Mapping). Gmail draft to member with inline-HTML revenue-share-or-Money-Mapping banner. **No Tracy's-sheet verification** (uses form-supplied splits). Idempotent on `pip_rev_share_status LIKE 'Completed%'` — which is why the 2026-08-24 `Held - Member …` statuses re-run. **2026-08-24 — member-standing hold:** a suspended/paused member parks the leg held with **no transfer id and no `pip_rev_share_completed_at`** and no member email, but the additional-PIP child meetings still unlock (the client paid). Branch chain made exhaustive in the same change. Released nightly by the 02:00 MAP 1 sweep's new held pass, or instantly by `releaseHeldMemberPayouts`. |

Plus stripe-webhook integration in `router/webhooks.ts`: PIP routing added as a cascade fallback (MAP1 → Tax → Advisor → PIP) on `checkout.session.completed`, AND a metadata-gated branch on `payment_intent.succeeded` (`pi.metadata.pipeline === "PIP"`) for ACH-clearing.

### Partnership Fast Track (PFT) engagement chain (added 2026-06-05)

Files in `actions/pft/` (shared helpers in `_shared.ts`: `PF_EMAILS`, `notifyAssignedPf`/`notifyPf`, `ftButtons`, template/sandbox/`savePftProgress` helpers — as of 2026-07-14 `notifyPfAndRachael` was renamed `notifyPf` and no longer CCs Rachael; the `RACHAEL_EMAIL` export was removed; **as of 2026-08-03 `savePftProgress` also calls `activateClientIfPending` (`utils/client-status.ts`) at the end**, flipping `clients.status` `pending`→`active` for the PFT decision/email-button steps that do not route through `msm_save_client_task` — gotcha #320). Pipeline label `PARTNERSHIP_FAST_TRACK`; per-accountant state in `pft_engagement`. All emails are Gmail drafts (sandbox row `PARTNERSHIP_FAST_TRACK`). See [../flows/partnership-fast-track.md](../flows/partnership-fast-track.md).

| Action | Gate | File | R | W | Chains / external |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `automation_PFT_meetingemail` | AUTH | `actions/pft/meeting-email.ts` | `clients`, `pft_engagement`, `email_templates`(PFT_meeting_confirm / PFT_meeting_declined / PFT_meeting1_member_declined / PFT_meeting_ert_declined), `psbx_cfg`(PFT) | `client_progress`(status/date/notes), `pft_engagement`(discovery_token/_email_sent_at on Meeting 2) | Gmail draft. `meeting`(1/2/3) + `decision`(confirm_date/confirm_no_date/declined/**us_declined**); `declined`=client declines us (`PFT_meeting_declined`), **`us_declined`=we decline them** (requires `decline_reason`, injects `[DECLINE_REASON]`; Meeting 1→`PFT_meeting1_member_declined` status `Declined by Member`, Meeting 2/3→`PFT_meeting_ert_declined` status `Declined by ERT/VFOS`). Fills `[PRIOR_MEETING]`/`[NEXT_MEETING]`/`[NEXT_MEETING_TITLE]`/`[FORM_SECTION]`/`[CLOSING]`. Discovery `[FORM_SECTION]` attaches only on confirm decisions. (gotcha #247) **2026-08-16 (v746): accepts `reschedule`** — a resend keeps the same `discovery_token` but **skips the `discovery_email_sent_at` / `discovery_pf_notified_at` writes**, so the discovery reminder ladder in `actions/pft/sweep.ts` does NOT restart (#404). **2026-08-20 (v766): ctx gained `MEMBER`** (`memberEmailForClient` in `pft/_shared.ts`, `clients.member_number`→`members.email`), so the `MEMBER` token the meeting templates have always listed in `cc_list` finally resolves — before this the member was silently dropped from every PFT email (#424). |
| `automation_PFT_decisionemail` | AUTH | `actions/pft/decision-email.ts` | `clients`, `pft_engagement`, `email_templates`(PFT_decision_*), `psbx_cfg` | `client_progress`, `pft_engagement`, `accountant_onboarding`(insert for FT/Associate; copies the client's `assigned_pf`→`onboarding_team_member`+`onboarding_team_member_at`), `clients.status='lost'`(No) | Gmail draft. FT **and (2026-07-13) Associate** → 2-button confirm/another-meeting email (shared `/pft-ft-decide` flow) + immediate handoff (`selected_vfo_ft`/`accountant_type='VFO FT'` resp. `selected_pft`/`'VFO Associate'`), stamps `ft_response_token`+`ft_email_sent_at`; No → decline + stop. **2026-08-20 (v766): the shared ctx gained `MEMBER`** (`memberEmailForClient`), so the decision templates' long-listed `cc_list` `MEMBER` token resolves for the first time (#424). |
| `automation_PFT_undecided` | AUTH | `actions/pft/undecided-email.ts` | `clients`, `pft_engagement`, `email_templates`(`PFT_decision_undecided`), `psbx_cfg` | `pft_engagement`(decision_token/_email_sent_at/_task_id), `client_progress`("Undecided - awaiting client") | Added 2026-07-13. Drafts the client a 3-button email (VFO FT/Associate/No → `/pft-decide?token=&choice=`); defers the decision to the client (mirrors MAP 1 `/decide`). |
| `automation_PFT_undecided_response` | PUBLIC token | `actions/pft/undecided-response.ts` | `pft_engagement`(by decision_token) | `pft_engagement`(decision_response/_at) then delegates | Added 2026-07-13. Idempotent on `decision_response`; records the client's choice then **delegates in-process to `automation_PFT_decisionemail`** (rolls back on delegate failure). |
| `pft_load_engagement` | AUTH | `actions/pft/load-engagement.ts` | `pft_engagement`, `accountant_onboarding` | — | Returns the engagement row + linked onboarding `{id, stage, stage_label}` (associate = always Stage 3 "Add New Accountant"). |
| `automation_load_pft_pipelines` | AUTH | `actions/pft/automation-load-pipelines.ts` | `program_client_tasks`(meeting/decision/owner task ids by name), `client_progress`, `pft_engagement`, `clients`, `accountant_onboarding`, `psbx_cfg`(PFT) | — | Added 2026-07-20 (gotcha #247). Loader for the NEW `PFTAutomationPanel` (Automation & Config → Partnership Fast Track). Returns `{rows, sandbox_config}` — one row per client with ANY PFT email activity (meeting `client_progress` OR `pft_engagement`), bundling meeting outcomes + discovery/decision/FT sends & client button-clicks + onboarding handoff. **Meeting 1 only surfaced when the Initial-Contact "Who is completing the tracking for this accountant?" step = `Member`** (returns `tracking_owner`; nulls Meeting 1 + drops Meeting-1-only clients otherwise). |
| `automation_PFT_ftresponse` | PUBLIC token | `actions/pft/ft-response.ts` | `pft_engagement`(by ft_response_token), `clients` | `pft_engagement`(ft_response/_at), `accountant_onboarding`(create if missing + `onboarding_team_member` copied from `assigned_pf` + `prelim_meeting_status='Request no meeting'` on confirm), `notifications` | Idempotent on `ft_response`. confirm/another_meeting both notify the assigned PF only (`notifyPf`; no longer CCs Rachael, as of 2026-07-14). |
| `automation_PFT_loaddiscovery` | PUBLIC token | `actions/pft/discovery.ts` | `pft_engagement`(by discovery_token) | — | Returns `{state, data, submitted}`. |
| `automation_PFT_submitdiscovery` | PUBLIC token | `actions/pft/discovery.ts` | `pft_engagement`(by discovery_token), `clients` | `pft_engagement`(discovery_data/_submitted_at), `notifications` | Notifies assigned PF on submit. |
| `automation_PFT_sweep` | PUBLIC svc-role | `actions/pft/sweep.ts` | `pft_engagement`, `clients`, `email_templates`, `psbx_cfg` | `pft_engagement`(reminder/notified guards), `notifications` | Cron `pft-sweep-daily` 08:00 UTC. **All six cutoffs count BUSINESS days via `businessDelayCutoffIso` (2026-08-14).** Discovery 2-business-day reminder email + 4-business-day PF notice; FT 2-business-day reminder email + 4-business-day PF notice; **undecided-decision 2-business-day reminder email (re-sends `PFT_decision_undecided`) + 4-business-day PF notice** (rules `PFT_undecided_reminder_email`/`PFT_undecided_stall_bell`, guards `decision_reminder_sent_at`/`decision_pf_notified_at`). **2026-08-20 (v766): `sendDiscoveryReminder` + `sendFtReminder` ctx gained `MEMBER`** (`memberEmailForClient`) so their templates' `cc_list` `MEMBER` token resolves (#424); **`sendUndecidedReminder` deliberately NOT changed** — `PFT_decision_undecided` carries no `MEMBER` token (empty `to_list`, `cc_list` = two literal staff addresses). |

**Also this session:** `create_accountant_onboarding` + `automation_ACCOUNTANT_createmember` accept/honor `accountant_onboarding.accountant_type` (`'VFO Associate'` skips Stages 1-2 and the payment gate); `save_accountant_prelim_meeting` accepts the new `'Request no meeting'` status; all accountant/advisor onboarding notification links now append `&onboarding=<id>` to deep-link to the record.

### Advisor / Accountant onboarding — Team Member Responsible + New Model Sale email (added this session, v462)

Two AUTH/admin handlers record which team member "owns" an onboarding (the Stage-1 "Team Member Responsible" step), so its stall/decision/ready notifications route to that person's bell via `teamMemberRecipient()` (`constants/onboarding-team.ts`; 7 names → `@elitert.com` logins — `Evan Anderson`/`Bridger Silvester` added 2026-07-14). All are in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`.

| Action | File | R | W | Chains |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `save_advisor_team_member` | `actions/advisor/save-team-member.ts` | — | `advisor_onboarding` (`onboarding_team_member`, `onboarding_team_member_at`) | AUTH/admin. Validates the name against `TEAM_MEMBER_NAMES` (400 if unknown); clearing the field nulls both columns. |
| `save_advisor_implementation_value` | `actions/advisor/save-implementation-value.ts` | `advisor_onboarding` | `advisor_onboarding` (`implementation_value_vfo_ft`, `implementation_value_pft`, `implementation_value_at`) | AUTH/admin, `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` (added 2026-07-30, live v679 — gotcha #307). The Stage-1 **"Implementation Value"** step. Body `{ onboarding_id, vfo_ft_value?, pft_value? }` is a **PARTIAL update**: an absent key is left untouched, `null`/`""` clears it, anything else must parse finite and `> 0` (rounded 2 dp) or that key 400s on its own — so the two FE inputs can commit independently without clobbering each other. `implementation_value_at` = `existing ?? now()` when BOTH final values are `> 0`, else `null` (the `existing ??` preserves a date hand-corrected via `save_onboarding_step_date`). Returns `{ onboarding }`. |
| `save_accountant_team_member` | `actions/accountant/save-team-member.ts` | — | `accountant_onboarding` (`onboarding_team_member`, `onboarding_team_member_at`) | AUTH/admin (VFO FT accountants). Same shape as the advisor handler against `accountant_onboarding`. |
| `save_onboarding_step_date` | `actions/onboarding/step-date.ts` | — | `advisor_onboarding` / `accountant_onboarding` (one allow-listed `*_at` column) | AUTH/admin, ADMIN_ONLY (added 2026-07-20). Body `{ pipeline, onboarding_id, field, date }`. Inline edit of a MANUAL step's completion date (matches the tracking-track `StepDate`). `pipeline`∈{ADVISOR,ACCOUNTANT}; `field` validated against a per-pipeline allow-list (advisor: `prelim_meeting_status_at`/`onboarding_team_member_at`/**`implementation_value_at`** (added 2026-07-30)/`prelim_meeting_decision_at`; accountant: + `accountant_partnership_at`/`cc_advisor_at`); `date` `YYYY-MM-DD` stored UTC-midnight ISO. Returns `{ onboarding }`. Automated-event `*_at` columns are NOT allow-listed (stay read-only). |
| `automation_ADVISOR_extrameeting` | `actions/advisor/extra-meeting.ts` | `advisor_onboarding` | `advisor_onboarding` (`final_decision`/`via_extra_meeting`/`extra_meeting_completed_at`), `notifications`(clear) | AUTH/admin (added 2026-07-14). Body `{ onboarding_id, outcome }`. Decision stage: `'Yes'`→`final_decision='Yes'`+`via_extra_meeting=true`+chains `automation_ADVISOR_sendagreement` (service-role HTTP), `'No'`→`final_decision='No'`+`via_extra_meeting`+chains `automation_ADVISOR_declineemail`; signing/payment stage: `'Completed'`→stamps `extra_meeting_completed_at` only. All paths clear the action-required bell (by pipeline, `dismissible=false`, `read=false`, `link LIKE '%onboarding=<id>%'`). |
| `automation_ACCOUNTANT_extrameeting` | `actions/accountant/extra-meeting.ts` | `accountant_onboarding` | same shape on `accountant_onboarding` | AUTH/admin (added 2026-07-14). Same shape as the advisor handler; chains `automation_ACCOUNTANT_sendagreement` / `automation_ACCOUNTANT_declineemail`. |

**Extra-meeting mechanism (added 2026-07-14, clones MAP 1's):** the Undecided decision email AND all three sweep reminder emails (per pipeline) carry a blue **"Request Additional Meeting"** button — the `/advisor-decide` | `/accountant-decide` token URL with `decision=ExtraMeeting`; the sweeps call `ensureDecisionToken()` to mint+save a `decision_token` for straight-Yes rows lacking one (button omitted if minting fails). The public `automation_ADVISOR_clientdecision` / `automation_ACCOUNTANT_clientdecision` handlers now accept `decision='ExtraMeeting'`: they derive the stage from row state (`payment_link_sent_at`→`'payment'`, else `agreement_sent_at`→`'signing'`, else `'decision'`), write `extra_meeting_requested_at`/`extra_meeting_stage` (+ null `extra_meeting_completed_at` on repeat requests), park `final_decision='ExtraMeeting'` at the decision stage (a new possible value that stops the decision-stall reminders and the 14-day auto-decline), then fire an action-required bell (`dismissible:false`) via new rules `ADVISOR_extra_meeting_requested` / `ACCOUNTANT_extra_meeting_requested` to `TEAM_MEMBER`, linking to the onboarding record. The admin then records the outcome via the two `*_extrameeting` handlers above. DB: migration `20260714230000_onboarding_extra_meeting.sql` added 4 columns to BOTH `advisor_onboarding` + `accountant_onboarding` (`extra_meeting_requested_at`, `extra_meeting_stage` text `'decision'|'signing'|'payment'`, `extra_meeting_completed_at`, `via_extra_meeting` boolean default false) + the 2 `notification_rules` seed rows. Frontend: shared `src/components/admin/OnboardingExtraMeetingCard.jsx` + tracker badges/cards in `Advisor/AccountantOnboarding.jsx` + `Advisor/AccountantAutomationPanel.jsx` + a "Meeting Requested" confirmation in `Advisor/AccountantDecidePage.jsx`.

**Notification-routing changes (same session, no new actions):**

- `automation_ADVISOR_invoicereceipt` / `automation_ACCOUNTANT_invoicereceipt` — after stamping `invoice_sent_at`, now also INSERT a non-dismissible (`dismissible:false`) action-required `notifications` row titled `Ready to create <name> — onboarding complete`, routed to `teamMemberRecipient(onboarding_team_member)`; cleared when `create-member` runs.
- `automation_ADVISOR_sweep` / `automation_ACCOUNTANT_sweep` — the second-tier (4-business-day) stall PF notification now routes to `teamMemberRecipient(onboarding_team_member)` (was hardcoded `recipient:'admin'`).
- `automation_ADVISOR_clientdecision` / `automation_ACCOUNTANT_clientdecision` — the clicked-Yes notification routes to `teamMemberRecipient()`; the advisor "No" notification link is fixed from `/admin` to the `/admin?tab=advisors&section=advisor_onboarding&onboarding=<id>` deep-link. As of 2026-07-14 they also accept `decision='ExtraMeeting'` (client "Request Additional Meeting" button) — see the extra-meeting mechanism above.
- `automation_ADVISOR_createmember` / `automation_ACCOUNTANT_createmember` (Members table below) — now accept 8 `sale_*` body fields (`sale_closer`, `sale_setter`, `sale_introduced_by`, `sale_introduced_member_number`, `sale_introduced_member_name`, `sale_company_name`, `sale_website`, `sale_msm`), persist them on the onboarding row, fire `sendNewModelSaleEmail()` in BOTH the happy + idempotent-retry paths, and clear the "Ready to create" action-required notification. (`sale_msm` = the Member Success Manager pick; the email renders only its first name.)

**New Model Sale team email** (`utils/new-model-sale-email.ts`, no action of its own): drafts the internal "New Model Sale" Gmail when an advisor/accountant is created. Reads the `email_templates` row `pipeline='TEAM'`/`template_name='new_model_sale'` and sends TO every address on that row's new `to_list` jsonb (no Cc/Bcc; sandbox → `pipeline_sandbox_config.sandbox_email`). Atomic single-send claim on `<table>.new_model_sale_email_sent_at` (rolled back if the draft fails); never throws. The `to_list` column is persisted by `automation_save_email_template` (see Pipeline section) and editable via the Team Notification section of the Email Templates tab.

These three sit in `PUBLIC_HANDLERS` (pre-auth). Each is gated by a single-use/long-lived token on the `clients` row.

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `vault_tax_upload_url` | `actions/vault/tax-upload-url.ts` | `clients` (by `tax_upload_token`) | storage `client-tax-returns` (private bucket) | Mints a signed upload URL into the private `client-tax-returns` bucket. Used by the `/tax-upload` page. |
| `load_client_setup` | `actions/auth/client-setup-load.ts` | `clients` (by `client_setup_token`) | — | Validates `clients.client_setup_token`; returns `ready`/`already_setup`/`invalid` + client name/email. **ORPHANED as of 2026-08-26** — see below. |
| `submit_client_setup` | `actions/auth/client-setup-submit.ts` | `clients` (by `client_setup_token`) | `client_logins` (insert, salted passcode), `clients.client_setup_completed_at` | Creates the `client_logins` row + stamps `clients.client_setup_completed_at`. **ORPHANED as of 2026-08-26** — see below. |

> **`clients.client_setup_token` has NO WRITER as of 2026-08-26.** The `[PORTAL_SETUP]` button was removed from all four first-payment emails (`automation_CONTRACT_invoicereceipt` ids 10/115, `automation_TAX_invoicereceipt` ids 25/137) and both handlers stopped minting the token, so **nothing mints a fresh one** — `grep client_setup_token` over the whole function now returns only the two readers above. The pair is **still registered and still works**, deliberately: every `/client-setup?token=` link already in a client's inbox keeps resolving against the tokens minted before that date. Going forward the only route into a client login is the manual `send_login_setup_email` → `/set-password` chain (`login_setup_tokens`, a different table). Do not delete these two on the assumption they are dead code.

## Authed handlers (`AUTH_HANDLERS` in `router/dispatch.ts`)

All dispatched AFTER `middleware/auth.ts::authenticate()` validates body.token. Some take `auth: AuthContext` as 4th param.

### Bootstrap

| Action | File | R | W | Chains |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `load_data` | `actions/data/load-data.ts` | `experts`, `vfo_ecosystem_assignments`, `member_plugin_settings`, `member_exclusions`, `members`, `pipeline_sandbox_config` (MAP 1 row), `member_connections` (`member_a, member_b`) | — | Response shape: `{experts, ecosystems, members, exclusions, member_connections, sandbox_config}` (the `ciq` array was **removed 2026-07-01** — specialist CIQ topics gone, gotcha #168). Each `members` object includes `member_category` (`advisor`/`accountant`/`strategic_member`/null), `accountant_onboarding_id` (gotcha #48), `engagement_level` (added 2026-07-01), and `introduced_by_member_number` (added 2026-07-31 beside `connected_member_number`/`connection_type`). **`member_connections` is TOP-LEVEL, not per-member** (added 2026-07-31, gotcha #312) — the pairs are symmetric, so folding them into each member would duplicate every row and invite one side to drift. **The two halves of this payload are built by OPPOSITE rules.** `members` is re-assembled field-by-field as an **ADDITIVE whitelist** — a new `members` column is invisible to the portal until it is listed there (gotcha **#207**), which is why `members.personal_email` is private by simply never being added. **Two whitelisted fields are additionally ADMIN-GATED inside that assembly** — `notes` since it was added, and **`engagement_level` since 2026-09-09 (v820)**, both returned as `isAdmin ? value : null`: they are internal ratings OF the member sitting in a blob every logged-in caller receives, and both of `engagement_level`'s readers (the Member Overview editor, the member-profile hero pill) are admin surfaces. `experts` is `select("*")` and then **SUBTRACTIVELY redacted per caller**: admins get the rows untouched, non-admins get them filtered to `status='Active'` and destructured through `const { email, personal_email, ...rest }`. **So a new `experts` column is PUBLIC by default** — `personal_email` joined the stripped set 2026-08-26, and any future private `experts` column must be added here **and** to the two showroom loaders (`specialist-showroom-load.ts`, `client-showroom-load.ts`) or it leaks to every non-admin caller. |

See gotcha #278. |

| `member_stripe_connect_request` (uses auth) | `actions/members/stripe-connect-request.ts` | `members`, `pipeline_sandbox_config (MAP 1)`, `email_templates (member_connect_setup)`, `connect_setup_tokens` | `members.stripe_account_id` (Stripe Connect **Express** account, reused if present); `connect_setup_tokens` (durable token via `ensureConnectSetupToken`); drafts a Gmail **which AUTO-SENDS as of 2026-08-03** — `member_connect_setup` (id 159) was flipped `send_mode=true`, and this handler passes `sendMode: { pipeline: "MEMBER_PAYOUT", templateName: "member_connect_setup" }` to `draftGmail`, so the delivery layer dispatches it immediately (gotcha #325) | ADMIN_ONLY. "Set Up Payment Details" / "Resend setup email" — **as of 2026-08-03 these buttons live in the member profile's Settings tab** (a new always-visible "Stripe Connect" section between Member Login and the Danger Zone); they were removed from the Edit Profile tab, whose "Revenue &amp; Stripe" card is now just "Revenue" (gotcha #318). Creates the Connect account on the same platform/sandbox toggle the revshare transfer reads, then drafts the payout-setup email whose `[SETUP_LINK]` is the **durable** `${PORTAL_BASE}/payout-setup?token=` page (NOT a raw single-use Stripe link — 2026-07-23, gotcha #268); the per-click `connect_setup_link` action mints the fresh Stripe account link — which since v693 collects `eventually_due` (gotcha #317). Placeholders `[First Name]`/`[SETUP_LINK]`. **GUARDED as of 2026-08-21 / v774 (gotcha #428):** because the send is real, a set `members.connect_setup_email_sent_at` makes the handler return **`200 {already_sent_at, to_email}` with NO side effects at all** — no Connect account, no token, no email — unless the body carries `force: true`, which proceeds fully and re-stamps. A successful send stamps the column (`{error}` destructured, logged, never fatal — the mail is already out). The FE shows a `window.confirm` naming the prior date and re-calls with `force: true` on OK. **SECOND GUARD as of 2026-08-27 / v796 (gotcha #454):** a member whose `stripe_account_id` is BORROWED from their lead (`members.stripe_account_linked_from` set) gets a **400 naming the lead**, before any Stripe call or mail side effect — without it this email hands the corporate member an onboarding link **into the LEAD's Stripe account**. It sits above the re-send guard, and `force:true` does **not** override it. **v506; durable-link rework v645; guard v774; borrowed-account refusal v796.** |
| `member_connect_status` (uses auth) | `actions/members/connect-status.ts` | `members` (`stripe_account_id`), `pipeline_sandbox_config (MAP 1)`, Stripe `GET /v1/accounts/{id}` | **NOTHING — read-only** (no DB write, no Stripe object created) | ADMIN_ONLY. Added 2026-08-03 (**+1 → 450**); gotcha #318. Body `{member_number}`. Backs the live status dot on the member profile **Details** view (fetched fresh on every profile open + a Refresh button — no DB cache, no cron, no stored status column). Resolves the platform via the shared "MAP 1" `pipeline_sandbox_config` toggle, i.e. the platform that would actually pay the member out. Returns `status` ∈ **`none`** (no `stripe_account_id`) / **`complete`** (`payouts_enabled` AND `capabilities.transfers==='active'` AND `currently_due`+`eventually_due` both empty) / **`eligible_capped`** (payable now but requirements outstanding — the "$3,000 lifetime cap" state of #317) / **`pending`** (payouts or transfers not enabled) / **`mode_mismatch`** (404s under the resolved mode but resolves under the OTHER key — a deliberate cross-mode retry so a sandbox/live account-creation split renders NEUTRAL, never a false red/green; also returns `found_in_sandbox`) / **`unavailable`** (Stripe unreachable, or 404 under both keys). Also returns `payouts_enabled`, `transfers_active`, `details_submitted`, `currently_due`, `eventually_due`, `disabled_reason`. 400 without `member_number`, 404 on unknown member. **v692.** |
| `member_stripe_link_lead` (uses auth) | `actions/members/stripe-link-lead.ts` | `members` (the member row + the resolved lead row) | `members.stripe_account_id` (**the LEAD's id, COPIED**) + `members.stripe_account_linked_from` (the marker); `unlink` NULLs both | ADMIN_ONLY. Added 2026-08-27 (**+1 → 474**); migration `20260827140000_member_stripe_linked_from.sql`; gotcha **#454**. Body `{member_number, unlink?:true}`. Points a **Corporate Member**'s rev-share at their **LEAD** member's Connect account. **The copy IS the routing** — all four payout engines read `stripe_account_id` directly as the transfer `destination` and **none changed**; `stripe_account_linked_from` is a MARKER only. **The lead is resolved SERVER-SIDE** (`connected_member_number` when non-empty, else the member-number prefix `58147-C1`→`58147`) — a body-supplied lead would let a caller point any member's payouts at any account. **Refuses (400):** a non-corporate `member_type`; an unresolvable lead; a lead with no Stripe account; an `unlink` when nothing is linked; and a member who already has an account of their **own** (`stripe_account_id` set with NO marker) — it will not clobber a real id. 404 on an unknown member or lead. Re-linking to the same lead is idempotent. **It gates three other surfaces — see `member_stripe_connect_request`, `connect_setup_link` and `member_payments_load`.** No Stripe call at all. **v796.** |
| `automation_ADVISOR_createmember` | `actions/advisor/create-member.ts` | `advisor_onboarding`, `members` + `member_number_baselines` (via `nextMemberNumber`) | `member_plugin_settings`, `members` (`member_type='Implementation'`, `elite_status='Active'`, `advisor_model='New Model'`, `member_category='advisor'`, `revenue_decision='Money Mapping'`, `onboarding_id=<advisor_onboarding.id>` FK), `advisor_onboarding` (`member_number`, `member_created_at`, `revenue_decision='Money Mapping'`, the 7 `sale_*` fields, `login_setup_token`, `login_setup_token_expires_at`), `notifications` (clears "Ready to create") | Fired from Advisor Onboarding Stage 3 "Create Advisor & Send Setup Link" button. Accepts the 7 `sale_*` body fields (closer/setter/introduced_by/introduced_member_number/introduced_member_name/company_name/website). Member number via category-relative `nextMemberNumber('advisor','New Model')` (gotcha #48 — no fixed 60100 start). Idempotent on `advisor_onboarding.member_number` already set. No Stripe Connect — Money Mapping only. After member create succeeds (or on idempotent retry), generates a 14-day single-use `login_setup_token` and chains `automation_ADVISOR_loginsetupemail` via service-role, fires `sendNewModelSaleEmail()` (TEAM/new_model_sale), and clears the "Ready to create" notification. |
| `member_profile_load` | `actions/members/profile-load.ts` | `members` | — | — |
| `member_profile_save` (uses auth) | `actions/members/profile-save.ts` | `members` (old type for history) | `members` (full passthrough upsert of the `profile` payload — incl. `trading_name` and, since 2026-07-31, `introduced_by_member_number` + `connection_type`, and since 2026-08-20 `personal_email`), **`member_plugin_settings.name`** (2026-08-20), `member_type_history` (on type change) | Because the upsert is a whole-row spread of `profile`, a NEW `members` column needs no handler edit here — only a `load_data` whitelist entry (#207), **which `personal_email` deliberately does NOT get: it is record-keeping only and must never reach an email payload**. **NEW 2026-08-20 — the display-name sync:** after the `members` upsert, when `first_name` or `last_name` is present the handler also updates `member_plugin_settings.name` (the column the header, members list, avatar and member-login greeting actually read) to the collapsed/trimmed `"first last"`, skipping an empty result. Before this, that name was written once at creation and never resynced, so renames only took on the profile form. **NEW 2026-08-24 — the hold-release trigger:** the pre-upsert read now also selects `suspended` / `paused` / `membership_suspended`, and when a save takes `memberHoldReason` from non-null to null (**re-read from the row after the upsert, never merged from the payload — partial saves like the CIQ toggles send three fields**) it calls `releaseHeldMemberPayouts`, HTTP-chaining every revenue-share leg parked by the standing hold. The response gains an **additive** `payout_release: {fired, results}`; without a transition the shape is the old bare `{success:true}`. A failed re-read logs and skips the release rather than guessing. |
| `member_connection_add` (ADMIN) | `actions/members/connection-add.ts` | `members` (existence check on both numbers) | `member_connections` (INSERT) | Added 2026-07-31 (**+1 → 448**); gotcha #312. Body `{member_a, member_b}` in **either order** — the handler sorts them to the table's canonical `member_a < member_b` before writing. 400 on empty / equal / non-existent member numbers (lookup is `{data, error}`-destructured per #187 so a DB failure never reads as "not found"). **Idempotent:** a `23505` unique violation returns `{success:true}`, so double-clicks and retries are safe. Connections are MUTUAL and untyped — introductions are the separate directional `members.introduced_by_member_number` slot and are never touched here. |
| `member_connection_remove` (ADMIN) | `actions/members/connection-remove.ts` | — | `member_connections` (DELETE) | Added 2026-07-31 (**+1 → 449**); gotcha #312. Mirror of the add: same body, same canonical-order normalization, same 400s. **Deleting a pair that does not exist is a success** — the desired end state (not connected) is reached either way. |
| `member_overview_relations` (ADMIN + tab `member_overview`) | `actions/members/overview-relations.ts` | `member_enrollments`+`programs`, `clients`, `client_enrollments` | — | Added 2026-07-01. One bulk call for the Member Overview tab → `{programsByMember: {member_number: [name]}, clientsByMember: {member_number: [{id, ref, name, status, pf, joined, programs:[{id,name}]}]}}`. Avoids N+1 across all members. |
| `member_save_engagement` (ADMIN + tab `member_overview`) | `actions/members/save-engagement.ts` | — | `members.engagement_level` (validates highly/reasonably/somewhat_engaged·disengaged; empty clears) | Added 2026-07-01. |
| `client_overview_load` (ADMIN + tab `client_overview`) | `actions/clients/overview.ts` + engines `overview-engine/-map1/-regular/-tax/-pft.ts` | Per `section`: `pipeline_map1`, `client_priority_tracks`+`priority_progress`, `client_tax_plans`+`client_tax_progress`+`client_tax_specialists`, `pft_engagement`+`accountant_onboarding`, `client_progress`, templates `program_client_phases`/`program_client_tasks`, plus `clients`+`members`+`member_enrollments`+`client_enrollments`+`programs` enrichment | — | Added 2026-07-09. One bulk read-only call per Client Overview sub-tab (`section: map1\|regular\|holistic_tax\|tax_planning\|pft`) → `{section, clients:[{id,client_ref,name,status,assigned_pf,member_number,member_name,programs,tracks:[{id,title,state,last_action,last_action_at,next_action,next_actions,closed_reason,program_label,service_level,payment_plan,specialists,onboarding_stage_label,warnings,link}]}]}`. Step lists walk the LIVE task templates (template edits flow through automatically; special tasks keyed by `status_options` code); terminal branches → `closed` + reason. Regular section mirrors the tab's UNSORTED task order (gotcha #202). **REDESIGNED 2026-08-18 (v755–v758) — three payload changes, all additive.** (1) **`next_actions: [{label, owner}]` is now the real answer and `next_action` is legacy-compat** (gotcha #416): the array substitutes a step's `groupLabel` where one is set (the per-specialist Tax 5 / Tax 6 rows and Regular's MAP 5 collapse to their PHASE rather than naming whichever parallel row sorts first), it carries the **owner**, and it is the only field that can hold MORE THAN ONE entry — `computeTrack`'s `opts.parallelNext` lets an engine declare a genuine fork (two people owed work simultaneously), which a strictly-positional single string cannot express. `closed` and `complete` tracks return `[]`. (2) **Owners are a display string, except the PF, which is a TOKEN** (gotcha #415): every engine sets `Step.owner` — a real name where known (allocated planner, assigned PF) or a role (`Tray`, `Tracy / Tray`, `Admin`, `Client`, `Anton (CEO)`, `System`) — but the engines run BEFORE `enrichClients` and never hold the `clients` row, so a PF-owned step emits `PF_OWNER` (the literal `"__PF__"`, exported from `overview-engine.ts`) and **`overview.ts` is the one and only site that resolves it** to `assigned_pf || "PF"`. An engine emitting a raw name instead silently bypasses `assigned_pf` and is wrong for every client whose PF differs. The resolver walks `next_actions` only. (3) **`program_label`** (`"Tax Planning"` / `"Holistic Tax Priorities"`, null on non-tax tracks) tells merged tax rows apart. **`section: "tax_planning"` now MERGES both tax programs** — `mergeResults(runTax(1), runTax(4))`, client order first-seen across the two passes, a client on both keeping both programs' tracks. **`holistic_tax` is retained server-side unchanged** (program 1 alone) purely for the deploy window: the backend ships before the frontend, so a stale bundle still asking for it must keep getting exactly what it got before — it is no longer requested by any live caller. **`warnings` is still COMPUTED and still SHIPPED but is no longer RENDERED** — the 2026-08-18 frontend deleted the whole warnings UI, leaving the second grant key `client_overview_warnings` (EMPTY `TAB_ACTIONS` list, plus its `AdminEditor.jsx` checkbox) **orphaned**; retire the grant, the checkbox and the computation together or keep all three, and see the hub's OPEN/OWED. **The `SECTION_LINK` client-side mirror is GONE (2026-08-18):** `ClientOverviewPanel.jsx` navigates per-row off the payload's own `track.link`, so changing a section's emitted `link` no longer requires a matching frontend edit. Frontend pills are now **four** — `map1` / `regular` / `tax_planning` / `pft`. **(2026-08-03) The MEMBER-name column in the same table is now separately clickable** via the shared `MemberNameLink` (`components/shared/personLinks.jsx`, gotcha #322), navigating to `/admin?member=<member_number>` off the payload's existing `member_number` — a different destination from the client-name link and independent of `SECTION_LINK`. **Tax sections, 2026-07-31 / v683 (gotcha #311):** `overview-tax.ts` now emits `Tax 6 - Implementation` as **one step per allocated specialist** (label `<task> — <specialist>`, read from the per-specialist progress map, `applicable: implReach`) instead of one plan-level step, and tags each with the **same `spec-<id>` scope as the Tax 5 steps** so a specialist's Tax 5 + Tax 6 steps form ONE ordered sequence for the skipped-step warning scan (a separate scope would false-warn across different specialists on every multi-specialist plan). *(That code moved VERBATIM to `utils/tax-plan-steps.ts` on 2026-08-17 — `overview-tax.ts` is a thin caller — and on 2026-08-18 those same per-specialist rows gained `groupLabel`, so they still emit one step each but collapse to a single phase name in `next_actions`.)* |

NOT admin-only — the member portal uploads through it. Gotcha #309. |
