#### Client vault — admin side (added this session, all in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`)

Admin-facing handlers over the private client buckets. The `vault_tax_*` set guards downloads/uploads/deletes behind `isTaxAdmin`; the `vault_gen_*` set is the General Documentation section open to any admin. **`isTaxAdmin` reads one constant — `TAX_VIEWERS` in `constants/tax-access.ts`, currently Jake / Tray / Paul / Tracy Miller (`tnmiller@elitert.com`, added 2026-07-30 v680).** There is no frontend mirror of the allowlist: the UI is driven by the server-returned `can_view` flag on `vault_tax_list`, so a backend deploy is the whole change. **VIEW is wider than the allowlist since 2026-08-18:** `vault_tax_list`'s `can_view` and `vault_tax_download` both call `canViewClientTaxVault` (same file) = `isTaxAdmin` **OR** the admin who is that one client's assigned PF (`taxPfLoginEmail(clients.assigned_pf)`, resolved server-side). Upload / delete / share stay on bare `isTaxAdmin`, and `vault_tax_list` reports that narrower answer as a SECOND flag `can_manage` so the UI can offer View without Add/Delete/Share.

| Action | File | R | W | Chains |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `vault_tax_list` | `actions/vault/tax-list.ts` | storage `client-tax-returns` (list by `client_id`) | — | Any admin. Lists tax-return titles for a `client_id` + returns TWO flags: `can_view` (= `canViewClientTaxVault`: `isTaxAdmin` OR this client's assigned PF; planners short-circuit true) and the narrower `can_manage` (= `isTaxAdmin` OR planner — the pre-carve-out `can_view` expression). The Vault tab keys View on the first and Add/Delete/Share on the second. |
| `vault_tax_download` | `actions/vault/tax-download.ts` | `clients` (`assigned_pf`, via `canViewClientTaxVault`) · storage `client-tax-returns` | — | `canViewClientTaxVault` ONLY (= `isTaxAdmin` OR this client's assigned PF) — the SAME predicate `vault_tax_list` reports as `can_view`. 300s signed view URL. |
| `vault_tax_admin_upload_url` | `actions/vault/tax-admin-upload-url.ts` | storage `client-tax-returns` | — | Signed upload URL. **Side effect added 2026-08-14 (#400), REORDERED 2026-09-09 (v820) to run BEFORE `notifyPlannerVaultDrop`: calls `maybeStampAssessVaultUpload` — for a vault-assess group planner only, it stamps `client_tax_plans.assess_vault_uploaded_at/_by`, clears the planner's assess bell and raises the assigned PF's `TAX_assess_vault_uploaded_pf` ask. It does NOT complete the assess step (the form still does) and the AUTH SHAPE IS UNCHANGED. It now RETURNS whether it raised that PF ask, and that boolean is passed to `notifyPlannerVaultDrop` as `suppressPf` so the same PF does not also get the dismissible FYI about the same file (#481).** `isTaxAdmin` for admin callers; **also in `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` (2026-08-13, v735)** — a `tax_planner` caller branches to `denyIfNotPlannerClient` group scope instead of the allowlist, mirroring `vault_tax_download`. Path built server-side from `client_id`. | **Also fires `VAULT_planner_document_added`** (FYI bell → Tracy **+ the assigned PF, unless `suppressPf`**) via `notifyPlannerVaultDrop` when the caller resolves as a planner / Team Member; admin callers do NOT fire it. Fires at MINT, not on the PUT (#393).

Sections are separate buckets, so this is a cross-bucket **copy → verify → delete** (never delete-first, #389); falls back to download+upload where `.copy()` has no `destinationBucket`. `ADMIN_ONLY` **and** narrowed in-handler to `isErtManager` (Jake/Tray). Source and destination paths are both built from the same server-side `key`, so it can never cross people. Same-section = 400. |

#### Client vault — MEMBER side (added 2026-08-27, v796; in NO gate list — confined by an in-handler guard)

The member portal's **Vault** tab on one of the member's OWN clients (member portal → program → client). Permissions, chosen by the user: **General VIEW+ADD · Sensitive/tax-returns VIEW+ADD · ERT/VFOS VIEW ONLY.** No delete, no share, no request-documentation, no drag-to-move.

**Read the gating carefully — all three are deliberately in NO `role-gates.ts` list, and that is correct here rather than the #309 hole it usually is.** `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` would 403 the surface they exist for; `MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS` only rewrites a `body.member_number` that none of them read, so it would guard **nothing** (gotcha **#455**). Their confinement is the in-handler **`denyIfNotOwnClient`** (`utils/client-ownership.ts`), the **first statement of every one of the three**, before any storage call, scoped on `auth.callerMemberNumber` and never a body value — members are deny-by-**OMISSION** in `middleware/auth.ts`, so this is the NAMED confinement #309 demands of the upload-url minter and the ownership check #142 demands of the other two. `constants/role-gates.ts` carries an 11-line comment naming all three so the omission is not "fixed" later.

**Bucket safety (`actions/vault/member-client-vault-shared.ts`):** TWO maps — **READ** (`general`→`client-documents`, `sensitive`→`client-tax-returns`, `ert`→`client-ert-docs`) and **WRITE** (general + sensitive only). **`'ert'` is ABSENT from the write map rather than present-and-rejected, so widening READ can never widen WRITE.** `bucketFrom()` is a `hasOwnProperty` **positive-allowlist** lookup with **no fallback** — deliberately unlike the older `memberBucketFor`, which silently defaults an unknown section to the general bucket (#204).

| Action | File | R | W | Chains |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `member_client_vault_list` | `actions/vault/member-client-vault-list.ts` | storage `client-documents` + `client-tax-returns` + `client-ert-docs` (list by `client_id`) | — | `denyIfNotOwnClient` first. Returns all three sections in the usual `{path,name,size,created_at}` shape, the 16-char upload prefix stripped from `name`. |
| `member_client_vault_download` | `actions/vault/member-client-vault-download.ts` | storage (300s signed URL) | — | `denyIfNotOwnClient` first, **plus** `path.startsWith(`${client_id}/`)` — a signed read is minted only for a path inside the caller's own client folder. Works for all three sections incl. `ert`. |
| `member_client_vault_upload_url` | `actions/vault/member-client-vault-upload-url.ts` | — | storage `client-documents` / `client-tax-returns` (signed upload url) | `denyIfNotOwnClient` first. **Bucket from the WRITE allowlist (403 *"ERT/VFOS Documentation is read-only here"* on `ert` or any unknown section); path built HERE as `${client_id}/${token32().slice(0,16)}_${safeName}` — the body selects neither bucket nor folder.** Fires the `VAULT_planner_document_added` bell via `notifyMemberVaultDrop` (see below). **DELIBERATELY does NOT call `maybeStampAssessVaultUpload`**, which the admin/planner minters do — that stamp satisfies the TAX PLANNER's assess step for vault-assess groups, and a member dropping a file must never green a planner's step on their behalf. |

**The bell:** `notifyMemberVaultDrop` in `member-client-vault-shared.ts` is a **sibling** of `utils/vault-planner-notify.ts notifyPlannerVaultDrop`, not a branch inside it — that helper resolves a TAX PLANNER session first and would silently no-op for a member caller. Same rule key `VAULT_planner_document_added`, same title shape, same recipients (Tracy + the client's assigned PF via `taxPfLoginEmail`, deduped), same `dynamic: { ASSIGNED_PF }` (#324/#413), same **fires-at-MINT-time** caveat (#393 — a browser PUT that then fails leaves one early bell), whole body try/catch so a notify failure never fails the mint. **The rule row's own prose still says "planner"** — see [flows/notifications.md](../flows/notifications.md).

Frontend: `ClientDetail.jsx` (member tab strip gains Vault; `validTabsForProgram`'s member array becomes `['home','vault']`) and `ClientVaultTab.jsx` (new `memberMode` prop, `MEMBER_SECTIONS` beside the renamed `ADMIN_SECTIONS`; **every added condition is `&& !memberMode`**, so admin/planner rendering is unchanged).

#### Request documentation — the vault-wide secure-upload request (added 2026-07-30, v681; gotcha #310)

Program/pipeline-agnostic: the SAME three actions serve the Sensitive + General section of a **client**, a **member** and a **specialist** vault (6 admin placements). The admin action is `ADMIN_ONLY`; the two upload-side actions are **PUBLIC and deliberately in NO `role-gates.ts` list** — the `vault_upload_tokens` row is the entire credential and the destination is derived from it server-side, never from the body (this is how #309 is satisfied without a gate).

| Action | File | R | W | Chains |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `vault_request_docs` | `actions/vault/request-docs.ts` | `clients`(by id) / `members`(by `member_number`) / `experts`(by id) via the exported `resolveVaultPerson`; `members`(by the client's `member_number`, CLIENT requests only) · `email_templates`(pipeline `VAULT`, name `VAULT_request_documentation`, `active=true`) · `vault_upload_tokens`(by entity+section) | `vault_upload_tokens` (INSERT if absent — `token32()`; else REUSED) + `last_requested_at` · ONE Gmail **DRAFT** via `draftGmail` (`sendMode {pipeline:'VAULT'}`, `VFO_SIGNATURE` appended) | **NEW 2026-07-30 (v681), +1 action.** AUTH, **ADMIN_ONLY**. Body `{entity_type: client\|member\|specialist, entity_key, section: sensitive\|general, requested_info}`. Substitutes `[Recipient Name]` / `[Recipient First]` / `[REQUESTED_INFO]` / `[UPLOAD_BUTTON]` (the button links to `${PORTAL_BASE}/vault-upload?token=`, label "Upload Documents"). Recipients resolve through `resolveTemplateRecipients` with `RECIPIENT` = the person and **`MEMBER` = the client's member for CLIENT requests ONLY** — member/specialist requests pass no `MEMBER`, so the template's `cc_list ["MEMBER"]` role token drops and there is no Cc (silently, by omission — #310). 400 on a bad `entity_type`/`section`/blank `requested_info`, 404 unknown recipient, 400 when the person has no email. **The admin's typed text is stored NOWHERE.** _Note: the `MEMBER` ctx postdates the v681 deploy — live v681 sends every draft with no Cc until v682 ships._ |
| `vault_request_upload_url` (PUBLIC) | `actions/vault/request-upload-url.ts` | `vault_upload_tokens` (by `token`) | storage — signed UPLOAD url into the bucket from `VAULT_REQUEST_BUCKETS` (client → `client-tax-returns`/`client-documents`, member → `member-tax-returns`/`member-vault`, specialist → `specialist-tax-returns`/`specialist-documents`), path `` `${entity_key}/${rand16}_${safeName}` `` | **NEW 2026-07-30 (v681), +1 action.** PUBLIC — the `/vault-upload?token=` page's minter. Body `{token, filename}` ONLY; **no id, section or bucket is accepted from the caller.** Filename stripped to `[a-zA-Z0-9._-]`, capped 120. 404 "Invalid or expired link" on an unknown token or an unmapped bucket. Same response shape as `vault_tax_upload_url`. |
| `vault_request_upload_notify` (PUBLIC) | `actions/vault/request-upload-notify.ts` | `vault_upload_tokens` (by `token`) · `resolveVaultPerson` (imported from `request-docs.ts`) | `vault_upload_tokens.last_upload_at` (non-fatal, logged on failure) · `notifications` via `notifyByRule` (`VAULT_requested_doc_uploaded`, `defaultRecipients ["tvaldes@elitert.com"]` = Tray) | **NEW 2026-07-30 (v681), +1 action.** PUBLIC. Fired by the `/vault-upload` page AFTER a successful signed-URL PUT (the PUT bypasses the server). Body `{token, file_name}`. 403 "Invalid link" on a token miss. Deep link per entity: client → `/admin/client/<id>?tab=vault`, member → `/admin?member=<n>&feature=vault`, specialist → `/admin?tab=specialists&section=specialist_search&expert=<id>`. The whole notification is wrapped in try/catch so a bell failure never loses the upload. **Fires NO tax stamps** — `tax_returns_received_at` / `additional_info_received_at` belong exclusively to the `/tax-upload` flow. |

The owner never writes here (their portal write handlers reject `section='ert'`); they only see an `ert` array from their `*_vault_list` and download `section='ert'`.

#### Specialist vault — specialist side (added this session, gated by `SPECIALIST_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`)

A `specialist` session may call ONLY the `SPECIALIST_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` allowlist. The four vault actions below are scoped to `auth.callerSpecialistId` — never a body `expert_id`; single "General Documentation" section over the private `specialist-documents` bucket (namespaced by `expert_id`). The allowlist also includes the `specialist_shared_*` doc-sharing actions, `specialist_update_login`, and — new 2026-06-19 — **`specialist_showroom_load`** (`actions/data/specialist-showroom-load.ts`): returns all **Active** experts + ecosystems (no exclusions) for the specialist-portal **Showroom** tab — the same data as the admin Showroom. **The third subtractive `experts` strip site** — `const { email, personal_email, ...rest }`; `personal_email` joined the stripped set 2026-08-26. The `experts` blob is `select("*")` everywhere, so this destructure is the ONLY thing keeping a private column out of a specialist's payload.

#### Payments — read-only aggregation (per-person tabs 2026-06-15 in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`; admin GLOBAL page 2026-06-16 in `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`)

There is no unified ledger — payment data is inline across 6 tables — so each handler fans out and normalizes to one row shape `{date, label, amount, fee, status, method+last4, invoice/receipt#}` via the shared builders in `actions/payments/normalize.ts`. The 3 per-person handlers each have a dormant role branch (client→`callerClientId`, member→`callerMemberNumber`, specialist→`callerSpecialistId`, ignoring any passed id) but stay **admin-only** — the originally-planned Track-3 portal exposure was cancelled 2026-06-16 (gotcha #125). Two pull LIVE from Stripe. The 4th action `all_payments_load` (superadmin-only) is the admin GLOBAL view — DB-only across everyone, with DB-derived rev-share payout rows (gotcha #129). The `fee` field is the card-fee portion of `amount` (base = amount − fee). **v613:** `normalize.ts` `PayRow` now carries an optional `revShare {member, vfo, memberIsPercent?, splitType?, status?}` on every MAP1 installment + tax retainer/implementation row (status: 'Paid to member' / 'No share due' / 'Money Mapping' / 'Transfer failed' / 'Pending', null until the payment has a receipt), rendered as a revshare line in `PaymentsTable.jsx`.

| Action | File | R | W | Chains |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `client_payments_load` | `actions/payments/client-payments-load.ts` | `pipeline_map1` + `client_tax_plans` + `client_priority_tracks` (pip) + `clients`/`members` (on-behalf marker) | — | MAP 1 / Tax / PIP rows for one client; flags member-paid-on-behalf. |
| `member_payments_load` | `actions/payments/member-payments-load.ts` | `advisor_onboarding`/`accountant_onboarding` (own fee) + their clients' `pipeline_map1`/`client_tax_plans` (on-behalf) + **Stripe `GET /v1/transfers?destination=`** (rev-share payouts) | — | 3 sections: own onboarding fee / on-behalf / live rev-share payouts. Graceful `payout_error` on Stripe failure. **GATED 2026-08-27 / v796 (gotcha #454):** the payout listing is by **`destination`**, so for a member whose `stripe_account_id` is BORROWED (`members.stripe_account_linked_from` set) it would return the LEAD's entire payout history *and every sibling corporate member's*. Stripe is now **not called at all** for such a member; the response carries an additive **`payouts_linked_to: {member_number, name}`** and `MemberPaymentsTab.jsx` renders a note in place of the empty table. |
| `specialist_payments_load` | `actions/payments/specialist-payments-load.ts` | `specialist_onboarding` (bg `bg_*`) + **Stripe `GET /v1/invoices?subscription=`** (full monthly-license history) | — | bg-check + live license history; sandbox key via `pipeline_sandbox_config[SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING]`. Graceful `license_error`. |
| `all_payments_load` | `actions/payments/all-payments-load.ts` | ALL `pipeline_map1` + `client_tax_plans` + `client_priority_tracks (pip)` + `advisor_onboarding` + `accountant_onboarding` + `specialist_onboarding (bg)` + `clients`/`members` (names) | — | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** — it is in `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`, NOT `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, and **its in-handler `isSuperadmin` 403 was REMOVED 2026-08-07 (v707)** under the Accounting-tab full-parity decision (#338); the tab grant is the whole gate. (The stale "superadmin-only" header comment in the handler was corrected in the same change.) GLOBAL admin Payments page — every DB-resident payment across everyone, tagged `person`+`personType`. **DB-only** (no per-person live-Stripe fan-out) but DERIVES rev-share PAYOUT rows from `rec{N}_rev_paid='Yes'`. Added 2026-06-16 (gotcha #129). **v613:** `GlobalRow` now also carries optional `clientId`/`memberNumber`/`memberName` (client rows all three incl. connected member; member-payout + onboarding rows carry `memberNumber`) — `PaymentsTable.jsx` links the client name → `/admin/client/<clientId>` and a "Member: <name>" sub-line → `/admin?member=<memberNumber>` on the global page. |

#### Payments — cancel remaining (added 2026-08-26, v789)

The "Cancel all remaining payments" button at the bottom of an expanded MAP 1 / Tax group on the admin client Payments tab. **Superadmin-only** (`SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, plus `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` as a fallback) because it permanently closes uncollected money and the UI offers no un-cancel.

| Action | File | R | W | Chains |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `payments_cancel_remaining` | `actions/payments/cancel-remaining.ts` | `pipeline_map1` (`payment_plan`, `pay2-4_status/_date`) **or** `client_tax_plans` (`retainer_status`, `final_retainer_status`, `implementation_charge_status`, `implementation_amount`, `refund_status`, `fee_process_version`) | the literal status `'cancelled'` + a `*_cancelled_at` stamp on every still-uncollected slot in ONE engagement group | **NONE — and that is the point.** No Stripe call, no email, no notification, and **no revenue-share column is ever touched** (`rec{N}_*` / `*_rev_share` / `*_rev_paid` are left alone — a payment that was never collected has no payout leg, and writing a terminal value onto one is how money gets silently swallowed, #303/#377). Body `{client_id, program:'map1'\|'tax', row_id}`; returns `{ok, cancelled[], count}` and a repeat click returns `count: 0`. Cancellable is a **positive allowlist** — `NULL`/`''`, `declined`, `auth_required`, `manual_required` (#431) — so `succeeded`/`processing`/`pending`/`check_pending` can never be closed. **MAP 1 payment 1 is DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED**: it is the engagement's OPENING payment, not an installment, so a NULL `pay1_status` means the client has not paid anything yet and "cancel the remaining payments" there would really mean cancelling the whole engagement — the button only ever touches 2/3/4, and a Pay-in-Full row returns `count: 0`. On Tax it re-evaluates `normalize.ts buildTaxRows`' own predicates (shared `isThreePaymentPlan` for the final retainer; `retainer_status` set + `implementation_amount > 0` + no succeeded refund for implementation), so **what the tab shows is exactly what the button closes**. Every downstream reader was taught `'cancelled'` means CLOSED — the authoritative list is the file's header comment. |

#### Payments — admin-initiated card/bank update (Phase D, added 2026-06-16, v483)

The "Send Email to Change Payment Method" button on every per-person Payments tab. `payments_send_card_update` (AUTH, **superadmin-only** — in `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`) drafts ONE Gmail to the payer with a secure `/update-card?token=` link; the link covers ALL the person's reusable engagements (the token is keyed to the PERSON, not one engagement). The two PUBLIC `/update-card` token-page actions back that page and bypass the auth gate (registered in `PUBLIC_HANDLERS` — listed here next to their sibling for locality; they sit BEFORE the auth gate like the other `/pay` token pages). Payer enumeration + per-engagement Stripe-customer/method resolution is shared via `actions/payments/card-update-shared.ts` (`loadCardUpdatePerson`, not an action). The webhook closes the loop (`checkout.session.completed` + `mode='setup'` + `payment_kind='card_update'` → saves the new default PM; see Webhooks table).

| Action | Gate | File | R | W | Chains / external |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `payments_send_card_update` | AUTH, superadmin-only | `actions/payments/card-update-send.ts` | reusable engagements via `loadCardUpdatePerson` (`pipeline_map1` / `client_tax_plans` / `specialist_onboarding` etc.), `email_templates`(`PAYMENTS`/`card_update`, built-in fallback if missing), `psbx_cfg` (per-pipeline sandbox routing) | `card_update_tokens` (insert, 7-day, person-keyed) | Gmail draft to payer with `/update-card?token=` button. Body `{person_type:'client'\|'member'\|'specialist', person_ref}`. Returns `{sent, count, to_email, sandbox}` or `{sent:false, reason:'none'\|'no_email'}`. |
| `payments_loadcardupdate` *(PUBLIC token)* | — | `actions/payments/card-update-load.ts` | `card_update_tokens`(by token, expiry-checked) + engagements via `loadCardUpdatePerson` | — | Backs the `/update-card` page. Body `{token}`. Returns `{person_name, engagements:[{id, pipeline, row_id, label, for_client_name, current_method, current_last4}]}`. Stripe customer id NOT exposed. |
| `payments_cardupdate_checkout` *(PUBLIC token)* | — | `actions/payments/card-update-checkout.ts` | `card_update_tokens`(by token, expiry-checked) + engagements via `loadCardUpdatePerson`, `psbx_cfg` (Stripe key) | — | Body `{token, pipeline, row_id, method:'card'\|'ach'}`. **Re-verifies the (pipeline,row_id) belongs to the token's person**, then Stripe `POST /v1/checkout/sessions` `mode:'setup'` for that engagement's existing customer (metadata `payment_kind='card_update'`, `pipeline`, `row_id`). Returns `{url}`. |

| Action | File | R | W | Chains |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `gc_load_services` | `actions/gc/load-services.ts` | `gc_services`, `allowed_admins` (admin only), `pipeline_sandbox_config` (admin only) | — | **Payload splits by caller role.** MEMBER: active services with `allocated_admin_email` + `scheduling_link` STRIPPED, `sandbox_config: null`. ADMIN: full rows plus `allocated_admin_name` per service (email → `allowed_admins.name`) and a top-level `admins: [{name,email}]` for the allocation dropdown. In NO gate list — unmapped actions are not tab-gated, so a `growth_credits`-only admin may call it. |
| `gc_load_balance` | `actions/gc/load-balance.ts` | `gc_balances` | — | — |
| `gc_load_transactions` | `actions/gc/load-transactions.ts` | `gc_transactions` | — | — |
| `gc_load_redemptions` | `actions/gc/load-redemptions.ts` | `gc_redemptions`, `gc_services` | — | — |
| `gc_load_all_redemptions` | `actions/gc/load-all-redemptions.ts` | `gc_redemptions`, `gc_services`, `member_plugin_settings` | — | — |
| `gc_redeem` | `actions/gc/redeem.ts` | `gc_balances`, `gc_services` (incl. `billing_interval`), `gc_subscriptions` (duplicate check) | `gc_redemptions`, `gc_balances`, `gc_transactions`, **`gc_subscriptions` (recurring services only)** | **Recurring (2026-08-27):** a `monthly`/`yearly` service first 400s a duplicate — an existing `active`/`on_hold` subscription for this member+service returns *"You already have this recurring service"* (this is what a stale tab hits). After the ledger writes it opens the subscription with `next_charge_date = addInterval(today, interval)` (`utils/gc-recurring.ts`, UTC + month-end clamped) and returns `recurring: {billing_interval, next_charge_date}`; that insert is **best-effort like the two below** — logged on failure, never returned as an error, because the credits are already spent. The one-time path is byte-for-byte unchanged. Then two best-effort side effects, each in its own try/catch (neither may fail the redemption): the `GC_credits_spent` bell — to `gc_services.allocated_admin_email` when set, else the historical assigned-MSM / MSM-team fallback — and `utils/gc-redemption-email.ts` (`GROWTH_CREDITS` / `GC_redemption_confirmation`, draft-mode, member To + allocated team member Cc). |
| `gc_add_credits` | `actions/gc/add-credits.ts` | `gc_balances` | `gc_balances`, `gc_transactions` | — |
| `gc_update_redemption` | `actions/gc/update-redemption.ts` | `gc_redemptions` (incl. `service_id`), `gc_balances` | `gc_redemptions.status`; on **reject** of a still-`pending` row also `gc_balances` + a `gc_transactions` `refunded` row, **and `gc_subscriptions` → `cancelled`** for that member+service | `status` must be `fulfilled`/`rejected` or 400. The subscription cancel (2026-08-27) stops the sweep charging for something just declined; renewals file no redemption row, so a rejectable pending one is always the INITIAL redemption. |
| `gc_manage_service` | `actions/gc/manage-service.ts` | `allowed_admins` (validating `allocated_admin_email`) | `gc_services` (insert/update/**delete**, incl. `category`, `allocated_admin_email`, `scheduling_link`, **`billing_interval`**) | Flat body, not `{mode, service}`. **`billing_interval`** (2026-08-27, the **Frequency** dropdown) is `one_time` when absent/null/empty and 400s on anything but `one_time`/`monthly`/`yearly`; the DB carries the same CHECK. `{service_id, mode:'delete'}` deletes and short-circuits BEFORE the name/cost validation; a service with `gc_redemptions` history hits FK `23503` and returns **400** "set it Inactive instead" (never cascades). Otherwise `name` required + `credit_cost` finite `>= 0` (**0 is valid** — a free service). A non-null `allocated_admin_email` must exist in `allowed_admins` or 400. |
| `gc_load_accounting` | `actions/gc/load-accounting.ts` | `gc_balances`, `gc_transactions` (incl. `amount_usd`), `members` (names) | — | ADMIN_ONLY + TAB_ACTIONS.accounting. Totals: credits purchased/spent/refunded, revenue_usd, outstanding. |
| `gc_create_checkout` (Stripe-helper adopter) | `actions/gc/create-checkout.ts` | — | — | Stripe `POST /v1/checkout/sessions` (metadata: member_number, credits, `pipeline: GROWTH_CREDITS`). **Prices SERVER-SIDE from `GC_PACKAGES` — 1/`$100`, 10/`$950`, 20/`$1,800`, 50/`$4,000` (the 50-credit / 20%-off package ADDED 2026-09-01, v804); the client-sent `amount` must be one of those keys and any client-sent price is ignored.** **Webhook fulfills via `router/webhooks.ts` `fulfillGrowthCredits()`, deduped on `stripe_session_id`.** |
| `gc_load_subscriptions` | `actions/gc/load-subscriptions.ts` | `gc_subscriptions` + embedded `gc_services(name, billing_interval)` | — | `MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS`; reads by `member_number` alone, exactly like `gc_load_redemptions`. Newest-first; returns every status, the frontend picks the first `active`/`on_hold` per service. |
| `gc_cancel_subscription` | `actions/gc/cancel-subscription.ts` | — | `gc_subscriptions` → `status='cancelled'` + `cancelled_at` | `MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS`. Body is `{member_number, subscription_id}` — and since that list rewrites **only `member_number`**, the `.eq("member_number", …)` on the UPDATE **is** the ownership guard (**#455**), not decoration; `.select()` must return exactly 1 row or it 404s. Matches `active`/`on_hold` only. **No refund, no proration** — the paid period stands, only the next charge stops. |
| `gc_update_subscription_date` | `actions/gc/update-subscription-date.ts` | — | `gc_subscriptions.next_charge_date` | **NEW 2026-09-09 (v820).** AUTH, in **`ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`** and **deliberately NOT in `MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS`** — it is addressed by `subscription_id` and reads the owning member off the row, so there is no `member_number` in the body for that list to rewrite and a member cannot reach it at all (#455: name the field the handler filters on, never reason from a list's name). Body `{subscription_id, start_date}` with `start_date` as `YYYY-MM-DD`. **`start_date` is DAY 1 OF THE CURRENT PERIOD, NOT the next charge date** (user decision, "Option B"): the handler stores `next_charge_date = addInterval(start_date, gc_services.billing_interval)` using the same `utils/gc-recurring.ts addInterval` the redeem path and the nightly sweep use, because `gc_redeem` already deducted the credits for the period the member is in — see **#478**. **No credits move.** 400s on a non-real date, a cancelled subscription, a non-recurring service, and a start whose computed renewal is already before today UTC; the UPDATE carries an optimistic `.eq("next_charge_date", <the value just read>)` and 409s if the sweep moved the row underneath the edit. |
| `automation_GC_recurring_sweep` (**PUBLIC**) | `actions/gc/recurring-sweep.ts` | `gc_subscriptions` (due: status `active`/`on_hold`, `next_charge_date <= today`), `gc_services`, `gc_balances` | `gc_subscriptions` (claim/advance, or `on_hold` + `on_hold_notified_at`), `gc_balances`, `gc_transactions` (`"<name> (renewal)"`) — **never `gc_redemptions`** | Service-role Bearer gate. Cron `gc-recurring-sweep-daily` @12:30 UTC; **no weekend skip** (deliberate). Optimistic period claim before the deduction; **re-anchors from TODAY, not the stored due date (#456)**. Drafts `GC_recurring_renewal` (244) or `GC_recurring_out_of_credits` (245) via `utils/gc-recurring-email.ts`, fire-and-forget. Returns `{ok, charged, held, skipped}`. |

> **Caller-ownership (2026-06-18, C2):** `msm_update_client`, `msm_load_client_home`, `msm_load_client_detail`, `msm_save_client_task`, `msm_load_client_progress`, and `msm_delete_client_contact` now each call `denyIfNotOwnClient` (`utils/client-ownership.ts`) — a member caller's `body.client_id` must map to a `clients` row whose `member_number` is theirs, else 403 (admins unrestricted). Additionally `msm_update_client` now **ignores `status` + `assigned_pf` from member callers** (admin-controlled fields; members are view-only on them). Closes a member→other-member client IDOR.

> **Removed in Phase 6 mechanical:** the duplicate `msm_update_client` handler (formerly `update-client-dup.ts`) was deleted. The original second `if (action === "msm_update_client")` block at baseline line 2427 was always unreachable because the first dispatch returned. Map dedupe in `router/dispatch.ts` accomplishes the same effect — only one registration of `msm_update_client` (pointing at `update-client.ts`).

| `automation_TAX_amend_fee` | `actions/tax/amend-fee.ts` | `client_tax_plans` | `client_tax_plans` (every re-derived fee amount + every revenue-share leg), `client_tax_fee_amendments` (one audit row) | **NEW 2026-08-25 (v782–v787); WIDENED TO TAX PLANNERS 2026-09-03 (v807).** AUTH, **dual-listed in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` AND `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`** (the #273 pattern). It was admin-only until 2026-09-03, when both portal roles gained it — **and because the omission had BEEN the confinement, the widening had to add the guard this handler never carried: `denyIfNotPlannerPlan`, placed ahead of the plan read AND the `keep` short-circuit** (an allowlist entry is not an ownership check, #257/#455; without it a planner could re-price any plan in the system off a body `tax_plan_id`). Re-pricing still moves the final retainer, the implementation charge and every revenue-share leg — including the planner's own `tax_planner_share` — which is accepted and made auditable by `client_tax_fee_amendments.created_by` rather than prevented. Backs the Tax 4 **"Amend fee"** and Tax 5b **"Amend implementation fee"** steps; both task names are also on the two `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` sets, because the step row is written by a SECOND `tax_save_task` call (#262/#467). Body is either `{tax_plan_id, stage:'tax4'\|'tax5', keep:true}` (confirm the current total unchanged) or `{tax_plan_id, stage, new_total}`. **Server-authoritative — the caller sends only the new total**; every amount and every revenue-share leg is re-derived server-side in whole cents and none is ever accepted from the body. Writes one audit row to the NEW `client_tax_fee_amendments` table, `created_by` = the caller's session email. **AMENDED 2026-09-01 (v802): the `isNewFeeProcess` 400 is DELETED — LEGACY plans (`fee_process_version` NULL) can amend too**, landing the whole movement on `implementation_amount` via the pre-existing fallback arm; a legacy plan that was never PRICED is refused instead (*"This plan's fee has not been set yet"*), and a **`cancelled`** final retainer / implementation charge now freezes its stage alongside `processing`/`succeeded`. Which legacy plans HAVE the step is decided by `utils/tax-plan-steps.ts`, not here. No gate-list, count or body-shape change. **AMENDED 2026-09-01 (v805, the buffer band):** the Tax 4 arm's re-derivation test moved from `newTotalCents > THREE_PAYMENT_THRESHOLD * 100` to `>= THREE_PAYMENT_MIN * 100`, so an amendment BELOW `$31,000` — whether at or under `$30,000` or inside the `$30,000.01–$30,999.99` band — takes the pre-existing CONVERSION arm, which IS the band's storage shape; nothing else in the handler changed. **AMENDED 2026-09-09 (v819): the implementation-only arm's floor moved `<= 0` → `< 0`, so a new total EQUAL to the retainer already paid is accepted and `implementation_amount` stores `$0.00`** (*"Total cannot be less than the retainer already paid"* replaces *"…must be more than…"*); only a NEGATIVE implementation fee is refused. **The two 3-payment Tax 4 arms keep their own floors — a `$0.00` FINAL retainer is still refused.** That floor had been silently guaranteeing *"a payment exists"* to three consumers outside this handler — see **#477** and the `automation_TAX_implementfinaldecision` row below. No gate-list, count or body-shape change. See [../flows/tax-fee-process.md](../flows/tax-fee-process.md). |

> **`automation_TAX_save_meeting_date` is now orphaned** (no frontend caller) — superseded by `automation_TAX_highlevelmeeting_confirm` above. Still registered in `router/dispatch.ts`, so it counts toward the action total.

> **Member-pays branching (this session).** 14 tax handlers now branch on `client_tax_plans.member_paying_on_behalf` (decision, send-agreement, stripe-customer, payment-email, confirmation-email, invoice-receipt, paidbycheck, final-decision, extra-meeting, postreview-decision, refund, implement-decision, implementation-receipt, charge-implementation; plus `revshare-sweep.ts` `sendReminderEmailUnified`). When true: emails flip To member / Cc client + use the member `email_templates` variant (suffix ` (member signing/paying on clients behalf)`, ids 126–147), member is BoldSign signer 1 + Stripe payer, invoice/receipt "Bill To" = member. `send-agreement.ts` filters `agreement_templates` by `payer_type` (client id 8 / member id 20). NOT changed: `ceo-countersign`, `revshare`, Setup `deposit-refund`. See [../flows/tax-planning.md](../flows/tax-planning.md) Step 2.

> **`automation_TAX_revshare_sweep` (PUBLIC svc-role) — Tax 4 reminder.** Its old `sendMeetingNudgeEmail` (a daily Gmail) is DELETED; the sweep raises ONE persistent action-required in-app `notifications` row (`dismissible:false`, rule `TAX_tax4_decision_needed`), title `Client decision 1 needed — <client>`, when `tax4_meeting_date < today` AND `post_review_decision IS NULL` (guarded by `tax4_meeting_reminder_last_sent_at`). **Recipients since 2026-07-27 (v664): the assigned PF + the allocated tax planner + Tracy** (`tnmiller@elitert.com`, always present); the planner's row is link-overridden to `/tax-planner/client/<id>?program=<n||1>` via the `links` map (gotchas #291/#292). Cleared by `actions/tax/postreview-decision.ts`. All tax notification links now append `&program=<program_id>` (program-context fix).

> **The revised tax fee process (2026-08-25, v782–v787) is three actions.** `automation_TAX_amend_fee` above, plus the PUBLIC pair `automation_TAX_charge_final_retainer` / `automation_TAX_final_retainer_receipt` in the Pipeline / automation group below. One engagement total goes in and every amount, the 3-payment split at **$31,000 and above** (initial retainer / final retainer / implementation) and every revenue-share leg are derived server-side; legacy plans (`fee_process_version IS NULL`) are untouched. **AMENDED 2026-09-01 (v805): three bands, not two** — `THREE_PAYMENT_THRESHOLD = 30000` ends the plain 50:50 split and the NEW `THREE_PAYMENT_MIN = 31000` begins the 3-payment schedule; the `$30,000.01–$30,999.99` **buffer band** between them is 2 payments with the flat `$15,000` as the WHOLE retainer, stored exactly like a converted plan (final NULL, initial kept). `splitFeeCents()` is the one non-throwing core every consumer imports. Full walkthrough: [../flows/tax-fee-process.md](../flows/tax-fee-process.md).

#### Specialist Stage-5 Go-Live + create-specialist + portal login setup (added this session)

The go-live tail: after the license invoice/receipt clears, admin sends the VFO Skool invite (Stage 5), then builds the `experts` row from the SIF (which copies the DD-checklist files into the specialist vault and chains the login-setup email). The specialist receives a `/member-setup?token=<>` link, sets a passcode, and gets a `specialist_logins` row.

Admin-triggered from the **"ROI Meeting booked - Send Confirmation Email"** task (renamed from "Tax 3 Confirmation Email", `task_order` 3 → 0 so it is now FIRST in Tax 2 - Deeper Dive). **The `No - Declined email to client` button renders for Holistic (program 1) only — a FRONTEND-ONLY gate; this handler still accepts `declined` on a program-4 plan** (guardrail, not a boundary; same shape as #353). | `automation_TAX_revshare_sweep` | `actions/tax/revshare-sweep.ts` | `client_tax_plans` (filtered by `*_receipt_number IS NOT NULL` AND `*_rev_paid NOT IN ('Yes','Money Mapping','N/A — No Share Due')` for both retainer and implementation kinds; ALSO drives the 2-business-day / 4-business-day reminder + PF-notification timers for Tax 3 Undecided, Tax 3 signing, Tax 3 payment, Tax 4 post-review Continue **and** Undecided, and Tax 5 implementation Proceed **and** Undecided flows — **14 cutoffs, every one of them `businessDelayCutoffIso` as of 2026-08-14**; AND the Tax 4 "Client decision 1 needed" reminder when `tax4_meeting_date < current_date AND post_review_decision IS NULL`, which is a **CALENDAR** comparison against a real meeting date and stays that way) | `client_tax_plans` (`*_reminder_sent_at`, `*_pf_notified_at` per flow; `tax4_meeting_reminder_last_sent_at` for the Tax 4 reminder); `notifications` (PF notif at the 4-business-day tier; the Tax 4 reminder; all tax notification links now append `&program=<program_id>`) | **THIS SWEEP NEVER CHARGES ANYTHING — 2026-08-14.** The old Tax 5 tier "Proceed + 24h with no client click → write `implementation_final_decision='Auto-Locked'` and fire the off-session charge" is **DELETED**. In its place the Proceed branch gets the same ladder every other stall has, on two NEW `notification_rules` rows (already live): **`TAX_impl_proceed_reminder_email`** (default 2 business days → reminder email re-showing the Proceed/Decline buttons) then **`TAX_impl_proceed_stalled`** (default 4 business days → PF bell, wording "Nothing is charged until they confirm"). A silent Proceed plan now waits **indefinitely** for a real click. Both Tax 5 branches share the `implementation_reminder_sent_at` / `implementation_pf_notified_at` guards, which is safe because `implementation_decision` is either `'Proceed'` or `'Undecided'`, never both — the same argument as the Tax 4 Continue/Undecided pair. Chains `automation_TAX_revshare` per revshare candidate and **nothing else**. Drafts reminder emails inline; `sendReminderEmailUnified` flips to the member email-template variant when `member_paying_on_behalf=true`. **The old daily nudge email (`sendMeetingNudgeEmail`) is DELETED** — replaced by ONE persistent action-required in-app notification (`dismissible:false`, title `Client decision 1 needed — <client>`) to the **assigned PF + the allocated tax planner + Tracy** (`tnmiller@elitert.com`; was Tim + Tracy until 2026-07-27 v664 — gotcha #291), fired once per plan (guarded by `tax4_meeting_reminder_last_sent_at`), cleared by `postreview-decision.ts`. Service-role auth required (401 otherwise). Daily pg_cron job `tax-revshare-sweep-daily` at 02:30 UTC — between MAP1's 02:00 sweep and 03:00 chargescheduled sweep. **NEW 2026-08-10 (v717) — a sixth timer block, `TAX_tax3_assess_reminder_email`:** plans with a booked `tax3_meeting_date` between today and `businessDayHorizonDateOnly(today, delayDays)` (**business days BEFORE the meeting — a countdown delay, not an elapsed one**, default 2; the forward weekday walk is the mirror of `businessDelayCutoffIso` and is the ONLY other caller of it besides the check-reminder lookahead), `assess_form_submitted_at` null, `tax3_assess_reminder_sent_at` null and a planner allocated get ONE Gmail **draft** of `email_templates` **217** (`TAX`/`TAX_tax3_assess_reminder`, Draft mode) to the allocated planner, Cc'd their group's Active Team Members (computed by the sweep and passed as `resolveTemplateRecipients`' **extraCc** — no role token names that set) + `ASSIGNED_PF` + Tracy/Tray, sandbox-resolved per client via `loadTaxSandboxConfigForClient`. **`tax3_assess_reminder_sent_at` is stamped ONLY after a successful draft** — a missing planner email or a failed Gmail auth skips WITHOUT stamping and retries the next night (gotcha **#359**). **NEW 2026-08-20 — a SEVENTH block, the EARLY tier of that same reminder (`TAX_tax3_assess_reminder_early_email`, default 5 business days):** identical query, fork and stamp-on-success semantics on its own guard `tax3_assess_reminder_early_sent_at` and its own `TAX_tax3_assess_reminder_early` / `\|vault` templates, but its window is the half-open range **past** the 2-day horizon (`> assessHorizon`, `<= assessEarlyHorizon`) — so the tiers can never draft on the same night, and configuring the early delay at or below 2 empties the block instead of double-drafting. Returns `{ok, swept, fired, post_review_actions, implementation_actions, tax3_actions, meeting_reminder_actions, …, assess_reminder_actions, assess_reminder_early_actions}`. |

Admin-only, fires from Tax 5b "Implementation decision" task buttons. **No charge fires here, and none fires on a timer — 2026-08-14.** The "24h grace / 48h" wording described the deleted auto-lock tier; both branches now feed the reminder ladder in `automation_TAX_revshare_sweep` (2 business days → reminder email, 4 business days → PF bell) and the fee is charged **only** when the client clicks Proceed on `/tax-implement-decide`. |

Stripe `POST /v1/payment_intents` (off-session, confirm=true; card grossed up unless `card_fee_waived`, ACH at base; idempotency key `tax-impl-{plan}-{resolvedpm_or_pi_suffix}-{date}`). It stays PUBLIC/service-role-callable, which means a future caller can reintroduce a silent charge — treat the single call site as the invariant. |
| `automation_TAX_charge_final_retainer` (PUBLIC) | `actions/tax/charge-final-retainer.ts` | `client_tax_plans`, `clients`, `members`, `email_templates`, `psbx_cfg(TAX)` | `client_tax_plans` (`final_retainer_status` / `_payment_intent_id` / `_charge_date`), `notifications` (via `notifyJakeFailure`), Gmail draft on the failure path | **NEW 2026-08-25 (v782–v787).** Off-session charge of the **final retainer** on a 3-payment plan against the client's saved payment method — the sibling of `automation_TAX_charge_implementation` above and registered the same way: **PUBLIC and UNDERSCORED**. The hyphenated spelling is **not** registered and 401s (**#154**). **It has EXACTLY ONE call site: the service-role chain from `actions/tax/postreview-client-decision.ts`, on the client's own green click.** **Nothing may ever chain it from a sweep** — the same rule as `automation_TAX_charge_implementation` (**#398**); treat the single call site as the invariant. | `automation_TAX_implementfinaldecision` (PUBLIC, token) | `actions/tax/implement-final-decision.ts` | `client_tax_plans` (by `implementation_token`) | `client_tax_plans`(`implementation_final_decision` = 'Proceed' / 'Decline' — **`'Auto-Locked'` is HISTORICAL-ONLY as of 2026-08-14: zero writers anywhere in the codebase; `actions/clients/overview-tax.ts` still READS it so old rows keep rendering**); `notifications` (admin) | Token-based handler called from `/tax-implement-decide?token=X&decision=Proceed\|Decline`. Proceed click → **drafts the client acknowledgment email `TAX_implementdecision\|Proceeding` (`email_templates` id 211) FIRST**, then chains `automation_TAX_charge_implementation` via service-role. Decline click → drafts decline email. Inserts admin notification. Legacy Yes/No URL params still accepted (mapped to Proceed/Decline). **Acknowledgment ordering is deliberate (2026-08-03, v694, gotcha #321):** it is drafted BEFORE the charge chain so a charge failure (which falls back to a fresh `/tax-pay` link) can never skip it; the whole block is `try`/`catch` so an email failure never fails the recorded decision, and **both Proceed-branch returns now carry `email_drafted: ackEmailDrafted`**. It fires on a genuine client click ONLY (`Proceed`/`Confirmed`/legacy `Yes`) — and since 2026-08-14 **that click is the whole of the charge path**, the 24h sweep auto-lock it used to be contrasted with having been deleted. Also reads `email_templates`/`members` and the new token `[Payment Amount]` = base `implementation_amount`. **2026-08-28:** stamps `implementation_final_decision_at` in the same write as the decision, and BOTH emails now honour member-pays — the ack picks `TAX_implementdecision|Proceeding (member signing/paying on clients behalf)` (`email_templates` id **246**, seeded 2026-08-28) and the decline picks id **144**, each with the `RECIPIENT`/`CLIENT` swap and Additional Contacts dropped. Every substitution is a replacer FUNCTION (#438) and `[Member First]` is now substituted — it was missing, so the already-live row 144 would have rendered a raw token. **2026-09-09 (v819) — the `$0` early return.** Since `amend-fee.ts` may now leave `implementation_amount` at `$0.00` exactly, the Proceed/Confirmed branch parses the amount FIRST (same parse as `charge-implementation.ts`) and at `<= 0` returns `{ ok:true, decision, no_fee_due:true, email_drafted:false }`: the decision is still recorded, **the `TAX_implementdecision|Proceeding` ack is NOT drafted** (its `[Payment Amount]` would quote *"$0.00"* as the payment now being processed) and **the charge chain is NOT called** — without it `charge-implementation.ts`'s own `implAmount <= 0` guard would have surfaced a 400 as a FAILED CHARGE on a plan where nothing was owed (#468/#477). The `TAX_impl_client_proceed` bell still fires, with the message *"No implementation fee is due — the fee was amended so the retainer already paid covers the whole engagement. Nothing was charged."* |

Needed because the Request Tax Returns email, the Request Additional Information email and the first-payment email all carry the **same** `clients.tax_upload_token` URL, so the page cannot tell the flows apart. ### VFO Specialist Revenue (Accounting tab — added 2026-06-29)

Bill a specialist, split proceeds to members. Tables `specialist_revenue_requests` + `specialist_revenue_lines` (both deny-all RLS). Stripe ops ride the shared `pipeline_sandbox_config` "MAP 1" row. SUPERADMIN actions are gated in `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`; the PUBLIC ones bypass auth (token page + service-role webhook chains). **Recurring model (2026-07-09, v569):** a third billing method — a monthly ACH subscription that re-runs the same split every cycle — adds two deny-all tables `specialist_revenue_recurring_plans` (split snapshot + `charge_day` 1-20 + dedicated `stripe_customer_id`/`subscription_id` + `setup_token` + `status` `setup_pending`/`active`/`canceled` + `last_invoice_id` idempotency cursor + `next_billing_date` + `confirmation_email_sent_at`) and `specialist_revenue_recurring_lines` (recipient snapshot applied every cycle); `specialist_revenue_requests` gained nullable `recurring_plan_id` + `stripe_invoice_id` (unique partial index) so each monthly invoice pre-creates one request row. New SECURITY-DEFINER RPC `claim_specrev_recurring_invoice(plan_id, invoice_id, paid_at)` (service_role-only atomic per-invoice claim, mirror of `claim_specialist_license_invoice`). Migration `20260708150000_specialist_revenue_recurring.sql`. **ERT share leg (2026-09-11, migration `20260911180000_specrev_ert_share.sql`):** a recipient line now carries **THREE** money columns, not two. `specialist_revenue_lines` gained `ert_share` + `ert_payout_status` + `ert_transfer_id` + `ert_payout_sent_at`; `specialist_revenue_requests` gained `total_ert_share`; the recurring pair gained `specialist_revenue_recurring_lines.ert_share` + `specialist_revenue_recurring_plans.total_ert_share`. No new table, no new policy, no new DB function. **`ert_share` and `vfos_share` are mutually exclusive per line** — the house share is booked to **ERT** (Elite Resource Team, the sister company) *or* to VFOS, never both — and that is enforced three deep: the form disables the other box, both handlers 400 with `Enter either an ERT share or a VFOS share on each line, not both.`, and a DB CHECK (`specialist_revenue_lines_ert_xor_vfos` = `not (ert_share > 0 and vfos_share > 0)`, plus its recurring twin). The gross charged to the specialist is `member_share + vfos_share + ert_share` and **still lands on VFO Services** — customer, Checkout, invoice, receipt, emails and webhook routing are all unchanged; only where the money goes at payout time is new. `ert_payout_status` is a bare `text` column with no CHECK. **Three places write it, and only one writes more than the initial value:** `send-request.ts` and the **two** recurring line-copy sites in `router/webhooks.ts` stamp it **`pending` at INSERT** whenever `ert_share > 0` (and NULL otherwise); after that, `utils/specialist-revenue-payout.ts` is the **only** writer of every subsequent value. Its vocabulary: **NULL** = no ERT leg on the line, or an ERT leg never attempted (and NULL on a line that DOES carry an `ert_share` is **payable**, so a line written before the column existed is still picked up); **NON-TERMINAL** (= the exported **`ERT_PAYABLE_STATUSES`**) `pending` · `failed`; **TERMINAL** `sent`. Writers of the new columns: `send-request.ts` (both the link and the bank-transfer insert paths, stamping `ert_payout_status='pending'` when `ert_share > 0`), `recurring-create.ts`, and the **two** recurring line-copy sites in `router/webhooks.ts` — whose explicit `.select()` lists had to gain `ert_share` by hand (the #448 class of bug: an explicit select list silently drops a new column). New bell rule **`SPECREV_ert_transfer_failed`** (migration `20260911180100_specrev_ert_transfer_failed_rule.sql`; area `VFO Specialist Revenue`, kind `bell`, `action_required`, default recipient Jake, no delay, sort **31** — deliberately next to `SPECREV_transfer_failed` at 30, because it is the same kind of event on the other leg).

| Action | File | Role | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `specialist_revenue_send_request` | `actions/specialist-revenue/send-request.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | Persists request+lines. `payment_method:'link'` (default): creates a Stripe customer for the specialist + drafts the payment-request email (`SPECREV_payment_request`) with a `/specialist-revenue-pay?token=` link. **`payment_method:'pending'` (2026-07-07 house-account model):** calls `ensureHouseAccount` (`house-account.ts`) to create-or-reuse the ONE shared VFO Stripe "house" customer for the row's mode, inserts the request `payment_status='pending'`, sends **NO email**, and returns the shared house ACH account/routing (from `specialist_revenue_house_account`) for the admin to hand the specialist |
| `specialist_revenue_pay_load` | `actions/specialist-revenue/pay-load.ts` | PUBLIC | Token → request summary for the pay page |

| `specialist_revenue_invoicereceipt` | `actions/specialist-revenue/invoice-receipt.ts` | PUBLIC (chained) | INV/REC PDFs (`utils/specialist-revenue-invoice.ts`, numbers `INV-SPEC-<id>`/`REC-SPEC-<id>`) → Drive → email `SPECREV_invoice_receipt_email` w/ both attached |
| `specialist_revenue_payout` | `actions/specialist-revenue/payout.ts` | PUBLIC (chained) | Runs `utils/specialist-revenue-payout.ts` for one request |
| `specialist_revenue_payout_sweep` | `actions/specialist-revenue/payout-sweep.ts` | PUBLIC (cron 13) | **FOUR passes (2026-08-11); all FIVE delay cutoffs count BUSINESS days via `businessDelayCutoffIso` as of 2026-08-14:** payout retry (**Pass 1's candidate query is the imported `PAYABLE_STATUSES` since 2026-08-24 — never a literal list — so it carries the two `held_member_*` values by construction and cannot drift from the engine; totals gain `held`**) + one-off 2-business-day reminder / 4-business-day Tracy FYI + **Pass 2b `awaiting_verification` > 5 business days → `SPECREV_awaiting_verification_bell` to Tracy** (deduped on unread, NO stamp column, keyed on `updated_at`; a poll-based backstop so a stalled micro-deposit verification surfaces even if a Stripe event is missed — gotcha #370) + **recurring `setup_pending` 2-business-day reminder (`SPECREV_recurring_setup_reminder`) / 4-business-day Tracy bell**, guarded by `setup_reminder_sent_at` / `setup_pf_notified_at` (gotcha #296 fix). **Pass 1 now UNIONS two candidate queries (2026-09-11):** the member query (`payout_status in PAYABLE_STATUSES`) and a second ERT query (`ert_share > 0` AND `ert_payout_status` NULL **or** in `ERT_PAYABLE_STATUSES`); the two `request_id` sets are de-duped into one list, so a request whose member legs are all terminal is still swept for an open ERT leg. The ERT arm is the one query that **does** carry an `.is.null` arm — NULL there means “owed, never attempted”, including any line written before the column existed — while `sent` stays excluded by the positive list, same construction as the member query |
| `specialist_revenue_load` | `actions/specialist-revenue/load.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | Requests+lines (+ sandbox cfg) for the viewer + automation tracker |
| `specialist_revenue_retry_payout` | `actions/specialist-revenue/retry-payout.ts` | **SUPERADMIN (self-gates `auth.isSuperadmin`) — the ONE action listed in `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting` that KEPT its in-handler check through the 2026-08-07 full-parity pass**, because its only button lives in `SpecialistRevenueAutomationPanel` (**Automation** tab, Jake-only) and there is no Accounting-tab surface for it. If a Retry payout control is ever added inside the Accounting tab, this check must come off with it or a granted admin gets a silent 403 (#338). | Manual "Retry payout" |
| `specialist_revenue_confirm_received` | `actions/specialist-revenue/confirm-received.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** — **CHANGED 2026-08-07 (v707):** its in-handler `auth.isSuperadmin` 403 was its ONLY gate (it was named in NO gate list at all), so under the full-parity decision (#338) the check was removed AND the action was **ADDED to `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** — without that add, deny-by-omission (#309/#167) would have 403'd every granted admin. | "Mark payment received" on a `pending` (house-account) request. Trust-the-click: creates+confirms a `customer_balance` PaymentIntent to draw the amount from the shared house customer's pooled cash balance. **If the draw does not immediately succeed the PI is CANCELED** (else it stays open and silently absorbs a later transfer into the pool) → `{ok:false,not_funded:true}`. On success flips the request to `received` + chains `specialist_revenue_invoicereceipt` + `specialist_revenue_payout`. The confirm PI carries **NO `metadata.pipeline`** (only `request_id` + `payment_kind=specialist_revenue_house`) so `router/webhooks.ts` ignores it (webhooks.ts untouched by this feature). Gotcha #189 |
| `specialist_revenue_delete_request` | `actions/specialist-revenue/delete-request.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** — the ONLY list it is in, and there is **no in-handler check**: the same gate as the `specialist_revenue_send_request` that created the row, under the #338 full-parity decision. | **NEW 2026-08-21 (v777).** "Delete request" on a **pre-payment** one-off request: hard-deletes the `specialist_revenue_requests` row **and** its `specialist_revenue_lines` (explicit delete; the FK is `ON DELETE CASCADE` anyway). **Three guards, all before any delete:** `recurring_plan_id` non-null → 400 (recurring has its own `specialist_revenue_recurring_cancel`) · `payment_status !== 'requested'` → 400 quoting the status · `stripe_payment_intent_id \|\| payment_method_type` non-null → 400. So `pending` (house-account — it carries `payment_method_type='bank_transfer'` from creation, and "Mark payment received" owns it) and `failed` (kept as the record of a dead payment) are **deliberately NOT deletable**. Unknown id → 404. **Touches Stripe not at all** — pre-payment there is no PI and no session; the customer minted by `send_request` is left dangling on purpose (empty, no method, no balance) and every webhook block resolving by `stripe_customer_id` is a null-tolerant `maybeSingle()`. The emailed `/specialist-revenue-pay?token=` link dies with the row (`specialist_revenue_pay_load` 404s *"Invalid or expired payment link"*). FE: `canDeleteSpecrevRequest()` + `DeleteRequestButton` in `specialistRevenueShared.jsx`, wired into `SpecialistRevenuePanel.jsx` only — **`SpecialistRevenueAutomationPanel.jsx` renders the same `RequestRow` and was deliberately NOT wired**. Gotcha **#431** (the migration's 4-value `payment_status` comment is stale; the live vocabulary is six) |

Setup email now embeds the durable `/payout-setup?token=` link (2026-07-23, gotcha #268), not a raw Stripe link. |

Plan survives a missing template (returns `email_skipped`) |

| `specialist_revenue_recurring_checkout` | `actions/specialist-revenue/recurring-checkout.ts` | PUBLIC | `setup_token` → Stripe Checkout `mode=subscription`, **ACH-only at par**, inline `price_data` recurring monthly. First charge deferred via `subscription_data[billing_cycle_anchor]` + `proration_behavior=none` (NOT `trial_end`, which renders "31 days free" — gotcha #197) to the next charge-day occurrence at 17:00 UTC ≥2h out, capped by Stripe's next-natural-billing-date limit (00:05 UTC fallback + clamp); session `expires_at` 1h; `custom_text[submit][message]` no-charge-today note |
