| `specialist_revenue_recurring_confirmationemail` | `actions/specialist-revenue/recurring-confirmation-email.ts` | PUBLIC (chained) | One-time setup-confirmation email `SPECREV_recurring_setup_confirmation` (template id 189) to the specialist when the plan activates; idempotent via `confirmation_email_sent_at` |
| `specialist_revenue_ert_transfer` | `actions/specialist-revenue/ert-transfer.ts` | **AUTH + SUPERADMIN (self-gates `auth.isSuperadmin` → 403) + in `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`** — and in **NO `TAB_ACTIONS` list** (#338) | **NEW 2026-09-11.** One-off “Send funds to ERT” catch-up: a hand-typed `POST /v1/transfers` from the **VFO Services platform balance** to **ERT’s connected account** (destination read from `ERT_CONNECT_ACCOUNT_ID[_SANDBOX]` via `ertConnectAccountEnvName` — the same secret the payout engine uses, so this tool can never send somewhere the automated leg would not). Body `{amount, memo, client_ref}`: `amount` > 0 and **≤ 50000** (a typo guard, not a policy), `memo` required and ≤ 200 chars, `client_ref` 8-64 chars of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`. Mode follows the shared `pipeline_sandbox_config` “MAP 1” toggle. **Deliberately DB-FREE** — no row written, no email drafted, no bell raised; Stripe’s own transfer list is the ledger and the handler returns `transfer_id` to the operator. Double-click safety without a DB: idempotency key **`specrev-ert-manual-<client_ref>`**, where the frontend mints ONE `client_ref` per form and re-mints it only **after a success** — so every retry of one submission collapses onto the same transfer, while a second deliberate send of the same amount is genuinely a second transfer. `description` = `Manual transfer to ERT - <memo> - by <email>`; metadata `pipeline=VFO_SPECIALIST_REVENUE`, `kind=manual_ert_catch_up`, `memo`, `created_by`. It exists because money collected **before** the ERT share existed has no line, no request and no `ert_payout_status` for the nightly engine to retry. **CHANGED 2026-09-11:** it now **probes `connectTransfersActive` before calling Stripe** and returns **502** when ERT's account cannot yet receive transfers — without the probe a refusal would be cached against this submission's `client_ref` for 24h (gotcha #489) — and **after a success it stamps the destination payment** on ERT's side via `stampErtDestinationPayment`, returning **`stamped: boolean`** next to `transfer_id` |

Payout engine per line (`utils/specialist-revenue-payout.ts`; the re-entry set is the **exported `PAYABLE_STATUSES`** = `pending` · `awaiting_connect` · `failed` · `held_member_suspended` · `held_member_paused`, which the nightly sweep's Pass-1 query now imports rather than restating): Money Mapping → no transfer + notice email; **MEMBER recipient who is suspended / paused → NOTHING is sent to them and nothing is minted: `payout_status='held_member_*'` + one internal held-notice draft, deduped on the line's prior status (2026-08-24 — the branch sits ABOVE Connect resolution, account creation, the setup email and the transfer, so a suspended member is sent nothing and has no account minted for them; **Money Mapping outranks it by branch order and a $0 line escapes it by the condition itself — `recipient_type === 'member' && member_share > 0` — since there is no payout to withhold**, and specialist/expert lines are never held)**; Revenue Share + active Connect acct → `/v1/transfers` (member_share) + confirmation; Revenue Share + **EXPERT** with missing/unfinished acct (`capabilities.transfers !== 'active'`) → create an Express account + mint a fresh onboarding link + setup email + `awaiting_connect`. **CHANGED 2026-08-24 — a MEMBER line with NO `stripe_account_id` no longer mints an Express account and sends no `SPECREV_connect_setup` email** (no valid link exists for them: the durable `/payout-setup` token is issued only by `actions/members/stripe-connect-request.ts`, and `connect-setup-link.ts` 404s for a member with no account). It parks `awaiting_connect` **without** stamping `email_drafted_at` and raises the action-required bell **`SPECREV_member_share_held`** (title via `memberShareHeldTitle("Specialist Revenue", name, member#)`, so it self-clears byte-identically when the transfer lands — and on the $0-line close, that line's only chance to clear one). A member who HAS a half-built account still gets the fresh-link email, because that link works. Webhook: independent `VFO_SPECIALIST_REVENUE` branch in `router/webhooks.ts` + a branch in `utils/resolve-stripe-failure.ts`. **`checkout.session.completed` branches on the fetched PaymentIntent's own `status` (2026-08-11, gotcha #370 — UNDEPLOYED at time of writing):** card or an already-`succeeded` ACH → `received`, chains invoice+payout (no confirmation — card policy #287); `requires_action` (hand-keyed bank → micro-deposit verification) → **`awaiting_verification`** (new status), chains `specialist_revenue_confirmationemail`, which selects template **218** `SPECREV_payment_verify_bank` off the row status; any other status → `processing`, chains the same handler on template 168. Then `payment_intent.processing` → `processing`, and **`payment_intent.succeeded` → `received` accepting BOTH `processing` and `awaiting_verification`** (a `processing`-only guard strands the new state — gotcha #371) chaining invoice+payout. **Terminal failures:** `payment_intent.canceled` (new block — was handled nowhere), `payment_intent.payment_failed` and `checkout.session.async_payment_failed` all route through the shared `markSpecialistRevenueFailed` helper → `failed` + `SPECREV_payment_failed_bell`, no-op on rows already `received`/`failed`. All SPECREV blocks resolve by `stripe_customer_id` + `.maybeSingle()` — one-customer-per-request is an **unenforced** invariant (#370). **House-account (pending) path (2026-07-07):** a NEW shared table `specialist_revenue_house_account` (`sandbox boolean PK`, `stripe_customer_id`, `account_number`/`routing_number`/`bank_name`/`holder_name`, `stripe_account_id` [2026-07-09 — the house customer's Stripe account id, captured via `GET /v1/account` in `ensureHouseAccount` so the portal can deep-link to the customer page in the correct account/mode; gotcha #200], deny-all RLS) holds the ONE reused Stripe "house" customer per mode whose bank-transfer `funding_instructions` are the permanent ACH details; the `pending` status ("Awaiting bank transfer") is settled by `specialist_revenue_confirm_received` (above), NOT by any webhook. Reconciliation + period-total surfaces count only `payment_status='received'` (gotcha #190). **Recurring (subscription) webhook flow (2026-07-09):** a separate set of branches in `router/webhooks.ts`, ALL routed by the plan's DEDICATED Stripe customer (zero collision with the `SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING` license routing). `checkout.session.completed` (`mode=subscription` + `metadata.pipeline=VFO_SPECIALIST_REVENUE_RECURRING`) → plan `active` + `acct_last4` + `next_billing_date` (derived `trial_end || current_period_end || items.data[0].current_period_end || billing_cycle_anchor` — gotcha #199) and chains the confirmation email. NEW event `invoice.finalized` (now subscribed on both sandbox + live Stripe endpoints) → pre-creates the monthly `specialist_revenue_requests` row as `processing` (displayed "Pending") + copies the plan lines, skipping the $0 `subscription_create` stub invoice (gotcha #198; idempotent via the `stripe_invoice_id` unique index). `invoice.paid`/`invoice.payment_succeeded`/`invoice_payment.paid` → shared `processSpecrevRecurringInvoicePaid` (subscription-match guard + atomic `claim_specrev_recurring_invoice` RPC + find-or-create row by `stripe_invoice_id`) flips the row to `received` ("Completed") and chains the EXISTING `specialist_revenue_invoicereceipt` + `specialist_revenue_payout` untouched. `invoice.payment_failed` → row `failed` + dismissible `SPECREV_recurring_payment_failed_bell` (Jake + Tracy), plan STAYS active (Stripe retries). `customer.subscription.updated/deleted` terminal end → plan `canceled`. Each monthly cycle repeats the full split by design (reconciliation accumulates). **ERT share leg (2026-09-11) — a SECOND, INDEPENDENT transfer per line.** After the member loop above finishes, the engine walks its **OWN** candidate list (`ertLines`, built from the exported **`isErtLegOpen(line)`** = `ert_share > 0` AND `ert_payout_status` is NULL or in `ERT_PAYABLE_STATUSES`), filtered off the **same** single fetch of the request’s lines. Both lists are computed up front and the early return fires only when **neither** has work, because a line can be terminal on one leg and open on the other. **The ERT leg is deliberately NOT gated on the member leg**: a line settled `no_payout_due`, `money_mapping` or `held_member_*` still owes ERT its share, so none of the member branches’ `continue`s may skip it. Per open leg: `POST /v1/transfers` for `ert_share` from the **VFO Services platform balance** to **ERT’s connected account** (`ertConnectAccountEnvName(isSandbox)` → `ERT_CONNECT_ACCOUNT_ID[_SANDBOX]`), idempotency key **`specrev-ert-<requestId>-line<lineId>`** — date-less and logical like the member key, so a retry of a committed-but-lost transfer reuses the same Stripe transfer and never double-pays. `description` = `Specialist Revenue ERT Share - Specialist: <name> - For: <(member#) name>` (the memo names the member the share came FROM, not ERT); metadata `pipeline` / `request_id` / `line_id` / `expert_id` / `member_number` (non-specialist lines only) / `transaction_details` (trimmed to 500 chars). **The request id is carried in metadata ONLY — it is deliberately absent from the description** (CHANGED 2026-09-11; the memo previously ended ` - Request #<id>`). **Probe BEFORE the call — and why the key rotates (2026-09-11, gotcha #489).** Stripe caches the **first** response per `Idempotency-Key` for **24 hours, error bodies included**. A transfer attempted while the destination's `transfers` capability was still inactive therefore poisoned its own key: every retry replayed the identical refusal (observed live on request 37 line 86 — the retry came back with the same `request_log_url`). Two changes fix it. (1) **`connectTransfersActive(ertDestination, STRIPE_KEY)` is probed ONCE per request**, before the ERT loop — the same helper and the same discipline as the member leg. A not-active destination parks every ERT leg of that request `failed` with the bell and makes **no Stripe call at all**, so no key is ever consumed until the money can actually move. (2) **The key rotates once the line already carries a `failed` stamp**, gaining `-r<Date.parse(updated_at)>`. That is safe precisely because `failed` is only stamped **after Stripe returned an error body** — no transfer exists behind it, so a fresh key cannot double-pay. The genuinely dangerous case is the opposite one (a network drop *after* Stripe created the transfer), and it leaves the line `pending` with `updated_at` untouched, so that retry reuses the **original** key and gets the same transfer back. The member leg's key and probe are unchanged. **Stripe does NOT copy the memo to ERT's side — so the portal stamps it (2026-09-11).** A transfer's `description` and `metadata` live on the **platform's** transfer object; the `py_…` charge Stripe creates on the connected account is born bare. On ERT's own dashboard — the one Jake actually reads — the $4,119.77 catch-up showed Description **"-"** and **"No metadata"** (`py_1UEbptA6agMWAt8dMnI7a5IP`). So after every successful ERT transfer, engine and manual action alike, `utils/ert-destination-memo.ts` **`stampErtDestinationPayment`** POSTs `/v1/charges/<transfer.destination_payment>` with the header **`Stripe-Account: <ERT acct>`**, setting the same description and metadata. Acting as ERT is permitted because ERT granted `read_write` when it connected by OAuth. **Best-effort by contract:** the money has already moved by the time it runs, so a failure is cosmetic — it logs one line, returns `false`, and must never change a payout status, raise a bell or abort a run. Live-proven on the $978.68 catch-up (`tr_1UEc2xRwdhysCa6FgnfQHEQ4`). Success → `ert_payout_status='sent'` + `ert_transfer_id` + `ert_payout_sent_at`. A Stripe failure **or a missing `ERT_CONNECT_ACCOUNT_ID[_SANDBOX]` / Stripe key** → `ert_payout_status='failed'` + the action-required bell **`SPECREV_ert_transfer_failed`**, titled **`Specialist revenue ERT transfer FAILED — request #<id>`** — ONE title per request, so it dedupes across that request’s lines. A not-configured secret is treated as an ordinary failure (stamp, bell, count, carry on) and **never throws**, because the member legs of that request are already settled above and must not be rolled back or aborted. The bell is retired via `clearJakeFailure` **only when a run sends at least one leg and fails none** (`ert_failed === 0 && ert_sent > 0`), so one line’s success can never clear another line’s failure. **Money only — the ERT leg sends NO email, mints no Connect account, resolves no recipient contact and uses no template**: ERT is a sister company, not a payout recipient being onboarded. The summary object gains its own two counters **`ert_sent` / `ert_failed`** (never folded into `sent`, which would report money as having moved to a member when it did not), and both callers spread them straight into their JSON response.

### Tax Planners + Tax Planning Groups (added 2026-07-21)

The NEW "Tax Planners" 5th key admin section — an admin-managed person-type (`tax_planners`) attached to tax engagements, plus the "companies" (`tax_planning_groups`) that receive the planner share. Mirrors Specialists (`experts`) + Strategic Member Groups (`strategic_member_groups`): admin CRUD + private vault + Stripe Connect, but **no portal login** (all AUTH + `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, NOT tab-gated). Both tables are deny-all RLS; private bucket `tax-planner-documents`. See [../flows/tax-planning.md](../flows/tax-planning.md) + [../tables/tax.md](../tables/tax.md).

| Action | File | Role | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tax_planners_load` | `actions/tax-planners/load.ts` | ADMIN | List planners (+ groups) for the section list / KPIs / allocation dropdown. |
| `save_tax_planner` | `actions/tax-planners/save.ts` | ADMIN | Create/update a planner (name, email, status, `planner_role`, `member_type` = group/partnership, `certifications` jsonb, profile fields). **2026-07-27:** accepts `planner_role` sanitized to `'Tax Planner' \| 'Team Member'`; an EDIT that omits it PRESERVES the stored role (the handler re-reads the row rather than downgrading a partial payload). Returns **400 `"Team members require a partnership"`** when the effective role is Team Member and the effective `member_type` is blank. **`website_url` was REMOVED from the write whitelist** — the column and its data survive but are never written again. Gotcha #294. |
| `delete_tax_planner` | `actions/tax-planners/delete.ts` | ADMIN | Delete a planner. |
| `tax_planner_payments_load` | `actions/tax-planners/payments-load.ts` | ADMIN | The planner's Payments tab — plans that allocated this planner + their `*_planner_paid` payout state. |
| `tax_planner_clients_load` | `actions/tax-planners/clients-load.ts` | ADMIN | **Added 2026-08-03, live v695.** The planner profile's new **Clients** tab. Body `{ planner_id }` (400 if missing) — read-only, writes nothing. Reads `client_tax_plans` (`id, client_id, program_id, tax_planner_id`) `.eq('tax_planner_id', planner_id)`, then resolves `clients` (`first_name, last_name, member_number, client_ref`) and `members` (name by `member_number`, falling back to the `client_ref` prefix before the `-`). Returns `{ clients: [{client_id, client_ref, client_name, member_name, member_number, program_id, tax_plan_id}] }` sorted by `client_ref`. **One row per PLAN** — the FE dedupes to one row per client and merges the program labels, so a client holding both a Holistic and a VFO Tax Planning plan appears once. **Admin mirror of `tax_planner_portal_clients`, and the difference matters:** the portal action scopes to the caller's group via `auth`, this one names the planner in the body and therefore does **not** take `auth` — which is exactly why it must stay in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`. |
| `tax_planner_vault_list` / `_upload_url` / `_download` / `_delete` | `actions/tax-planners/vault.ts` | ADMIN | The Tax Planner Vault against `tax-planner-documents` (namespaced by `tax_planners.id`; signed URLs). |
| `tax_allocate_planner` | `actions/tax/allocate-planner.ts` | **ADMIN + tax-planner portal** (in BOTH `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` and, again since 2026-08-10, `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` — the #273 dual-listing pattern; fenced by `denyIfNotPlannerPlan` on the plan plus the pre-existing check that the TARGET planner is inside the caller's group, #257) | Sets/clears `client_tax_plans.tax_planner_id` from the **"Allocate Team Member / Tax Planner"** step (renamed from "Allocate to Advanced Tax Planner" on 2026-08-10; null/empty clears; 404 if the planner id is unknown); also writes the task's `client_tax_progress` status to the plain planner name. Gates the tax Yes-path (gotcha #254). **AMENDED 2026-08-10 (v717): the `planner_role === "Team Member"` 400 is DELETED — Team Members ARE allocatable** and are the deliberate first holder of a plan (#294 as amended). The role is still read, now to branch the **hand-off notification chain**. On any genuine change (set OR clear) the handler first sweeps this client's unread action-required bells across FOUR prefixes — `Allocate a Tax Planner for%`, `Enter the tax planning opportunities for%`, `Review the tax returns for%`, `Complete the tax planner review for%` — and then, **only when the allocation is SET**, also clears `Allocate a team member for%` (`clearAllocateTeamMemberBells`; a clear-to-nobody must leave that one standing, since nothing would re-fire it). It then fires by the NEW allocatee's role: **Team Member** → always `TAX_team_member_allocate_planner` ("please allocate a tax planner") — **the old roiConfirmed / not-yet split is GONE and the Team-Member `TAX_planner_allocated` FYI fire is deleted**; **Tax Planner** with the review step unanswered → `TAX_planner_review_needed` ("Review the tax returns for…"); **Tax Planner** with the review answered Proceed and `assess_form_submitted_at` null → `TAX_planner_assess_needed`; otherwise → the original dismissible `TAX_planner_allocated` FYI. The whole block is one try/catch — a bell can never fail the allocation. Titles are matched by prefix and are therefore load-bearing; the map lives in `utils/tax-review-bell.ts` (gotcha **#357**). **As of 2026-07-25 it also RELEASES a withheld planner share**: on a non-null allocation it calls `transferPlannerShare` for every kind whose leg is non-terminal, whose receipt number is set and whose member leg already resolved — paying instantly rather than waiting for the 02:30 sweep. Returns `released: [{kind, status, amount}]`. Payout errors are caught and never fail the allocation (gotcha #284). |
| `tax_update_split` | `actions/tax/update-split.ts` | ADMIN + **SUPERADMIN_ONLY** | Corrects a tax plan's revenue split AFTER pricing has closed — the only surface that can, since `automation_TAX_pricing` refuses once `agreement_sent='Yes'` (true for every migrated row and every organic client past Tax 3). Body `{ tax_plan_id, split_type, member_share, tax_planner_share, vfos_share }`, all shares in the stored **dollars-of-TOTAL** convention (#252); validates they sum to `total_fee` ±0.01. Refuses (409) once `implementation_charge_status` is `succeeded`/`processing` or an implementation leg is terminal. Stores `tax_planner_share` NULL when 0 (the sweep filters `.not(…,is,null)`). Returns the per-payment `payout` breakdown plus `warnings` (no planner allocated → share will be withheld; retainer already paid → change affects the implementation only). **KNOWN GAP — it knows THREE legs and never reads or writes `strategic_partner_share`, so its sum check can never pass on a Strategic Partner plan** (plan 91: `$13,500` against a `$15,000` total) and the only way to green it is to hand VFO the partner's dollars, over-allocating the engagement. `tax/PricingSplitCard.jsx` therefore **hides its Edit split button** on such a plan (2026-09-02) rather than offer a form that cannot save; the split on plans **91** and **103** is DB-only until this handler learns the fourth leg. Added 2026-07-25, live v660. |
| `save_tax_planning_group` (uses auth) | `actions/tax-planners/group-save.ts` | ADMIN | Create/rename a Tax Planning Group (case-insensitive unique name → 23505 friendly dup); a RENAME cascades every planner's `member_type`. |
| `delete_tax_planning_group` | `actions/tax-planners/group-delete.ts` | ADMIN | Delete a group — GUARDED when any planner still references it. |
| `tax_planning_group_stripe_connect_request` (uses auth) | `actions/tax-planners/group-stripe-connect-request.ts` | ADMIN | Group-level Stripe Connect Express account + `contact_email` setup email (mirrors `strategic_group_stripe_connect_request`); the resulting `stripe_account_id` is where the planner share transfers. Setup email now embeds the durable `/payout-setup?token=` link (2026-07-23, gotcha #268), not a raw Stripe link. |

The **Tax Planner Share** payout leg (no dedicated action — chained inside `automation_TAX_revshare`) runs `utils/tax-planner-payout.ts transferPlannerShare`: proportional `(tax_planner_share / total_fee) × payment` to the planner's GROUP Connect account (`tax_planners.member_type` → `tax_planning_groups.name` → `stripe_account_id`); statuses `Yes`/`Money Mapping`/`N/A — No Share Due`/`Failed` on `{retainer,implementation}_planner_paid`; `FAILURE_tax_planner_share` bell + daily-sweep retry on failure; confirmation emails `TAX_planner_revshare|retainer`/`|implementation` (ids 198/199, Draft). Gotcha #253.

### Tax Planner PORTAL (5th portal / 6th login type — added 2026-07-22)

The NEW `tax_planner` caller role + portal (login `tax_planner_login`, above). A planner is **deny-by-default**: it may ONLY call `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` (`constants/role-gates.ts` — read the array, never a count written here, #402) — it SKIPS the tab gate and the ADMIN_ONLY gate, and every planner-callable tax handler ALSO carries an in-handler `denyIfNotPlannerClient`/`denyIfNotPlannerPlan` whole-GROUP-scope guard (`utils/tax-planner-ownership.ts`). Gotcha #257. Beyond the login, the portal adds 4 dispatched actions. See [../flows/tax-planning.md](../flows/tax-planning.md) + [04-auth-and-sessions.md](04-auth-and-sessions.md).

| Action | File | Role | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tax_planner_update_login` | `actions/login-setup/tax-planner-update.ts` | AUTH (**tax_planner**; self-service) | Self-service passcode change scoped to `auth.callerTaxPlannerId` (never a body id). On the planner Settings tab (`ChangePasswordCard`). In `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`. |
| `tax_planner_portal_clients` | `actions/tax-planners/portal-clients.ts` | AUTH (**tax_planner**-only) | The portal's client roster: every client whose `client_tax_plans` row is allocated to any planner in the caller's Tax Planning Group (by `tax_planners.member_type`). Body `planner_ids` is intersected with the group roster server-side. Returns `{clients[{client_id,client_ref,client_name,member_name,member_number,program_id,tax_plan_id,planner_id,planner_name}], group[{id,name,first_name,last_name,member_type,status,**planner_role**,certifications}], self_id, self_role}`. **AMENDED 2026-08-10 (v717, #294 as amended):** `group` now lists the **WHOLE partnership roster — planners AND Team Members** (Team Members are allocatable, so hiding them would hide a legitimate pick), and each entry carries **`planner_role`** so the FE can suffix the label `— Team Member`; `planner_ids` is intersected against that whole roster. `self_role` still tells the FE which caller it is. A **Tax Planner** caller is unchanged — **SELF is ALWAYS included** and the default view is their own clients. A **Team Member** caller defaults to the **whole roster** (self is never prepended — a coordinator holds few or no plans of their own, so forcing self in would show an almost-empty list), and an EMPTY intersection of `planner_ids` falls back to all. Group scope itself is unchanged — a team member sees only their own partnership. |
| `tax_planner_portal_experts` | `actions/tax-planners/portal-experts.ts` | AUTH (**tax_planner**-only) | Feeds the Tax 5 "+ Add Specialist" picker in planner mode (planners never receive `load_data`). 2026-08-17: returns `{id, name, tax_bio}` only — Active experts **scoped to the Tax Planning ecosystem** (`vfo_ecosystem_assignments.name = 'Tax Planning'`), `tax_bio` = the Tax Planning `ecosystem_content` entries' `short_bio` joined `" / "` (legacy rows fall back to flat `experts.short_bio`), sorted by the displayed label `"<tax_bio> - <name>"`. Raw `ecosystem_content` is deliberately NOT returned (D&B/audit content). |
| `tax_save_assess_form` | `actions/tax/save-assess-form.ts` | AUTH (**ADMIN_ONLY + TAX_PLANNER** allowlist) | The "Assess tax planning opportunities (and enter presentation details)" form-step (task ids 89/123). **FOUR FLAT TOTALS + OPTIONAL YEAR 2 AND NOTES as of 2026-08-18 (v759)** (four flat totals 2026-08-17; structured strategy list 2026-07-29 v677 → replaced): validates the engagement-level `fee` FIRST, then the year-1 group `taxes_without_plan` / `taxes_with_plan` / `cash_outlay`, then — **only when at least one of its three fields is non-blank** — the same three under `year2`, then the notes length. Each amount required, non-negative, 2 dp, with the per-group cross-field `taxes_with_plan < taxes_without_plan` (strict). Stores `{ fee, taxes_without_plan, taxes_with_plan, cash_outlay, year2?: { taxes_without_plan, taxes_with_plan, cash_outlay }, notes?: string }` into `client_tax_plans.assess_form` jsonb, writing the two optional keys **only when they have content**. **`year2` has THREE fields and NO fee — deliberate:** the fee is one engagement-level figure attributed entirely to year 1 by the deck. **The year-1 keys are UNCHANGED and FLAT, which is what made the v759 deploy WINDOWLESS** — an old deployed frontend's 4-key payload still validates and still stores, and every pre-existing row is already a valid single-year row. **Ten 400 messages, mirrored VERBATIM in the FE (U+2014 em dash) in this order:** *"VFO Services Tax Planning Fee is required"* (suffix-FREE — it reverted to no year suffix when the fee became engagement-level), then *"Total Tax without the Plan — Year 1 is required"*, *"Total Tax with the Plan — Year 1 is required"*, *"Total Cash Outlay for the Strategies — Year 1 is required"*, *"Total Tax with the Plan must be less than Total Tax without the Plan — Year 1"*, then the identical four with **`— Year 2`**, then *"Notes must be 5000 characters or fewer"*. An all-blank year-2 group is stored ABSENT and never blocks the save. The `strategies` array and its four old messages are GONE from new submissions; derived numbers are computed at render, never stored (gotcha #306). **STILL three historical shapes, not four** — the flat 4-key row IS the current single-year shape — and they still render read-only (current / strategies / legacy `{question_1}`). The handler rebuilds the whole jsonb, so a re-save is the only upgrade path for a legacy row **and the only way to DROP a `year2` group or `notes`** — there is no backfill (#336). Stamps `assess_form_submitted_at` + `assess_form_submitted_by` (session email). **NO chains / emails.** **NOTIFICATIONS, fail-soft:** it reads the plan BEFORE the update (the prior stamp is what separates a first submission from an edit), then clears this client's unread action-required bells titled `Enter the tax planning opportunities for%` and — **on the FIRST submission only** (prior stamp null; a later edit stays silent, #314) — fires `TAX_assess_completed_pf` to the assigned PF's **portal LOGIN email** (`taxPfLoginEmail` from `utils/tax-notify.ts`, **not** the `pf-emails.ts` correspondence map). **That bell is ACTION-REQUIRED as of 2026-08-11**, titled `Download the ROI presentation for <client>`, its message naming the allocated planner who assessed, and it is retired only when `automation_TAX_presentation_sweep` stamps `presentation_email_sent_at`. Gotcha **#357**. `denyIfNotPlannerPlan` group-scope guard. Migration `20260722120000` added the 3 columns; `tax_load_plans` uses `select("*")` so no loader change. **It has always accepted a RE-SAVE (there was never a submitted-once guard) and overwrites the whole `assess_form` object — no merge, so a legacy `{question_1}` row is permanently replaced.** The frontend exposed that as an **Edit** button on the submitted row from 2026-07-31; gotcha #314. |
| `tax_generate_presentation` | `actions/tax/generate-presentation.ts` | AUTH (**`ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`** — deliberately **NOT** in `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`) | **NEW 2026-08-06 (v706); TWO-YEAR v2 rewrite 2026-08-07 (v713); v3 + SINGLE-YEAR MODE 2026-08-12 (v729); four-total inputs + always-single-year 27-slide deck 2026-08-17; TWO-YEAR MODE RESTORED — 27 OR 29 slides, selected by `assess_form.year2`, on template v4 2026-08-18 (v759); TEMPLATE v5 + the 22nd token — planner notes into slide 23's SPEAKER notes, the FIRST token outside `ppt/slides/` — 2026-08-19 (v761); TEMPLATE v6 + THE REVISED TAX FEE PROCESS — 22 → **31 tokens**, `{{FEE_HALF}}` RETIRED, a SECOND independent mode (2-payment vs 3-payment) deciding the three fee rows on slides 24/28 and the three chips on slide 27 — 2026-08-27 (v795); **THE FEE MODE NOW IMPORTS THE SPLIT ITSELF — `splitFeeCents(feeCents)`, three bands, `fee >= $31,000` for 3-payment — 2026-09-01 (v805)**, template UNCHANGED at v6; **TEMPLATE v7 — header wording *"VFO Specialists"* -> *"VFO Tax Specialists"* on slides 24/28/29 — 2026-09-09 (v820); TEMPLATE v8 — that header widened + `wrap="none"`, and each fee row's LABEL and VALUE merged into ONE shape, taking `SHIFT_EXPECTED_2PAY` 12 -> 9 and `SHIFT_EXPECTED_3PAY` 14 -> 10 — 2026-09-09 (v821)**.** The "Generate and download presentation" step (`program_client_tasks` **173** prog 1 / **174** prog 4, sentinel `tax_generate_presentation`, `task_order=2` in "Tax 2 - Deeper Dive"). Reads the plan + `assess_form` + the plan-level risk grade (`client_tax_progress` row with `tax_specialist_id IS NULL` for "Client risk profile complete", parsed `/Risk\s*([1-5])/`), pulls **`ROI-template-master-v8.pptx`** from the private **`presentation-templates`** bucket (service-role only — no `storage.objects` policies; the name is **versioned**, so a token/slide-list change needs a new object name + a lockstep deploy, #342 — v5 is retained as the rollback; **v6 both ADDED nine token sites and RETIRED one**), substitutes **30 `{{TOKENS}}`** (plus `{{PLANNER_NOTES}}`, the 31st, handled separately) across the **NINE** parts of **`TOKEN_PARTS`** = `SLIDE_PARTS` **1/20/23/24/25/27/28/29** + `NOTES_PART` **`ppt/notesSlides/notesSlide23.xml`** (values XML-escaped — **note `slide28`/`slide29` DISPLAY at positions 25/26**; parts are addressed by filename, never "correct" the list to display numbers; **the patch loop, the missing-part 500 and the leftover-token guard all key off `TOKEN_PARTS`, not `SLIDE_PARTS`**), **writes `assess_form.notes` into slide 23's SPEAKER notes as `TAX PLANNER NOTES: …` via `substitutePlannerNotes()` — the ONE token that bypasses the generic loop because its replacement is XML (multiline → runs joined by `<a:br>` with the run's own `rPr` cloned; empty → one invisible run and no label), located by INDEX WALK rather than regex (#420), and dropped in NEITHER deck mode**, **then runs TWO INDEPENDENT MODES, and the ORDER IS LOAD-BEARING. (1) FEE MODE FIRST — `splitFeeCents(feeCents)` IMPORTED WHOLE from `constants/tax-fee-process.ts` (2026-09-01 / v805; it previously imported the two constants and re-derived the arithmetic locally), which is the same core `deriveFeeSplit()` prices with, so the deck and the money cannot disagree. THREE bands, strict at both edges: `≤ $30,000` plain 2-payment, `$30,000.01–$30,999.99` the BUFFER BAND (2-payment, retainer the flat $15,000, implementation `fee − 15,000`), `≥ $31,000` 3-payment. `splitFeeCents()` carries NO range check — deliberately, because `deriveFeeSplit()` throws outside $0–$60,000 and a legacy plan with an out-of-band assess fee must still generate a deck.** 3-payment fills all three fee rows (Initial Retainer $15,000 flat / Final Retainer `round(fee/2) − 15,000` / Implementation the remainder); 2-payment — **buffer band included** — calls `dropFeeRow3()` on **both** fee slides (24 and 28), deleting row 3 — **ONE shape carrying both `{{FEE_ROW3_*}}` tokens since v8, two shapes carrying one each on v6/v7** — and on slide 24 **only** un-shifts the below-section back up 317400 EMU (slide 28 was never shifted in the template and passes `shiftBack=false`). The sum identity (`initial + final + implementation === fee`, whole cents) is asserted in code. **The deck ALWAYS quotes the flat $15,000; the custom-initial-retainer allowlist is deliberately NOT wired in (user decision).** **(2) YEAR MODE SECOND — `assess_form.year2`: ABSENT → single-year, which deletes slide 24's `{{INVEST_YEARS}}` `<p:sp>` and shifts the left-column shapes below it up 539650 EMU (`dropInvestYearsHeader()`, both counts asserted, constants COUPLED to the template version, v4 through v8) and drops both appendix slides → 27 slides; PRESENT → two-year, drops nothing and keeps the header → 29 slides.** The whole drop block is guarded by one `if (!twoYear)`. **The shift count depends on the FEE mode** — `SHIFT_EXPECTED_2PAY = 9` / `SHIFT_EXPECTED_3PAY = 10` (12 / 14 on v6 and v7) — precisely *because* the fee surgery ran first and has already removed the row-3 pair in 2-payment mode; it is PASSED IN, not derived, so the two surgeries' assertions stay independent and neither can mask the other's failure. Run the other way round, the fee un-shift's absolute-y threshold would silently match nothing. Repositions the slide-20 `upArrow` per risk grade off a hardcoded EMU table, then uploads to Google Drive **multipart with metadata `mimeType='application/vnd.google-apps.presentation'`** (server-side conversion to a real Google Slides file) into an "ROI Presentations" folder under `GOOGLE_DRIVE_FOLDER_ID`, sets sharing **role=writer type=anyone** (deliberate, user-approved), reads `webViewLink`, stamps `client_tax_plans.generated_presentation_{drive_id,url,at,by}` and returns `{success, url, generated_at}`. Zip work via **fflate** (`https://esm.sh/fflate@0.8.2`, the first zip lib in the codebase) — untouched entries re-stored **level 0**, only the edited XMLs deflated (the 8 slide parts **plus `notesSlide23.xml`** since v5, plus the three shared parts in single-year mode). **VALUES, per-year as of 2026-08-18, still INVERTED at the top:** year 1 from the flat `W1`/`T1`/`C1` plus the engagement-level `F`, year 2 (when present) from `year2`'s `W2`/`T2`/`C2` — `taxes_with` is **ENTERED** and `gross = W − T` is **DERIVED**. `GROSS_Yn = Wn − Tn`, `INVEST_Yn = Cn`, and **the whole fee lands on year 1**: `NET_Y1 = GROSS_Y1 − INVEST_Y1 − F`, `NET_Y2 = GROSS_Y2 − INVEST_Y2` (which is why slide 29's fee value is a literal `$0` in the template, not a token — there is no `PLANNING_FEE_Y2`; v6 renamed the LABEL above it to *"Total Fee:"* and changed nothing else). **Every combined figure is the SUM of the per-year ones**, computed from them so they cannot drift: `TAXES_WITHOUT = W1+W2`, `TAXES_WITH = T1+T2`, `GROSS_BENEFIT`/`CASH_OUTLAY`/`NET_BENEFIT` likewise, `outlay_plus_fee = CASH_OUTLAY + F`, `fee_half = F/2`, `pct = round(GROSS_BENEFIT/(W1+W2)×100)`. **Identities that hold by construction:** `NET_Y1+NET_Y2 === NET_BENEFIT`, `GROSS_Y1+GROSS_Y2 === GROSS_BENEFIT`, `INVEST_Y1+INVEST_Y2 === CASH_OUTLAY`. In single-year mode the six appendix tokens still substitute (`_Y1` = the whole-plan figures, `_Y2` = `$0`) purely so all 21 slide-borne tokens resolve before their slides are dropped; `INVEST_YEARS` is `"<year> + <year2>"` in two-year mode and `String(year)` in single-year, where its shape is deleted anyway. **400s:** no `assess_form` / non-finite `fee` (*"Submit the Assess tax planning opportunities form first"*); **old shape or any of the three newer totals missing** (*"Re-save the Assess form — it uses the old strategy format; re-enter the four totals"* — strict new-shape-only, nothing is derived from legacy strategy rows); `taxes_without_plan <= 0` (*"Total Tax without the Plan must be greater than zero"*); `taxes_with_plan < 0` or `>= taxes_without_plan`; **a `year2` group that is PRESENT but incomplete** (*"Re-save the Assess form — the Year 2 totals are incomplete"* — an ABSENT group is a valid one-year plan, never an error; the generator re-validates a present group rather than trusting it, because the save handler only ever writes it once all three passed); no risk grade. **SINGLE-YEAR MODE (`year2` absent) — 27 slides:** **BOTH** `slide28.xml` (year 1) and `slide29.xml` (year 2) are removed from the package: per slide, the `<p:sldId>` from `ppt/presentation.xml`, the `<Relationship>` from `ppt/_rels/presentation.xml.rels` (rId resolved dynamically by `Target="slides/slideN.xml"`, never hardcoded), the `<Override>` from `[Content_Types].xml`, and the part + its rels from the zip; each patch asserts **exactly one match** and 500s loudly otherwise. **The two removals touch the SAME three shared parts, so each is read once and the patched string is threaded through both iterations.** Single-year mode ALSO performs the slide-24 header surgery described above. **TWO-YEAR MODE (`year2` present) — 29 slides: the entire drop block is skipped (`if (!twoYear)`), nothing is removed and slide 24 keeps its "Your investment: 2026 + 2027" header.** **So the generated deck is 27 OR 29 slides and `assess_form.year2` is the only thing that decides (#374)** — the template always has 29. `SLIDE_PARTS` still lists 28/29 so their tokens substitute before the parts are dropped, keeping the leftover-token guard uniform across all 9 `TOKEN_PARTS`. **`TEMPLATE_OBJECT` is `ROI-template-master-v8.pptx` as of 2026-09-09 / v821** (v7 from v820, v6 from v795, v5 from v761, v4 from v759, v3 from v729; **v7 changed one HEADER'S WORDING on slides 24/28/29 — *"Your investment in VFO Tax Specialists:"* — and v8 widened that header, forbade it wrapping and merged every fee row's value shape into its label, which is why both `SHIFT_EXPECTED_*` counts fell by four; #480**); the 2026-08-17 four-total change took neither versioned path, but v4 and v5 each added a token site and so took the versioned one. **Writes NO progress row** — `generated_presentation_at` IS the step's done-state. Regenerate creates a NEW Drive file. `src/lib/api.js` gives it a **90s** timeout via `LONG_TIMEOUT_ACTIONS` (writes still never retry, #30). **NO chains / emails / notifications — and it deliberately does NOT clear the PF's "Download the ROI presentation" bell** (building the deck is not taking it, and it is often built by someone else; see `tax_presentation_downloaded` below). Gotchas **#336** + **#374**. |
| `tax_presentation_downloaded` | `actions/tax/presentation-downloaded.ts` | AUTH (**`ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`** — same gate as the generator it sits beside, deliberately **NOT** in `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`: the planner portal locks that whole step via `plannerMode`) | **NEW 2026-08-19 (v760), +1 action (463 → 464).** Body `{tax_plan_id}` → `{success:true}`. **Writes nothing** — no column, no email, no step status. Its only effect is `clearPresentationDownloadBells(client_id)`, retiring the PF's action-required `Download the ROI presentation for%` bell (rule `TAX_assess_completed_pf`), because *download the deck* is literally what that bell asks for. The Tax 2 **Download** button is a plain synchronous `window.open` of `generated_presentation_url`, so `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` fires this **fire-and-forget alongside it, swallowing every error** — a bell must never cost the PF their download. Guards: missing `tax_plan_id` → 400, `denyIfNotPlannerPlan`, unknown plan → 404, plan with a NULL `generated_presentation_at` → 400 (nothing could have been downloaded, so the bell is still genuinely owed). The two older clear sites (`presentation-schedule.ts` on scheduling, `presentation-sweep.ts` on `presentation_email_sent_at`) both remain and are idempotent with it. See [../flows/tax-planning.md](../flows/tax-planning.md). |

The other planner-reachable actions are EXISTING handlers now allowlisted + guarded (not new): the ~18 tax handlers in gotcha #257, the client-scoped loaders (`tax_load_plans`, `load_client_notes`, `msm_load_client_home`, `msm_load_client_progress`), the **client-notes writes** `add_client_note`/`update_client_note`/`delete_client_note` (allowlisted 2026-07-23 so the planner portal gets per-phase Notes; guarded by `denyIfNotPlannerClient`/`denyIfNotPlannerNote` — gotcha #273), the vault reads (`vault_gen_list`/`_download`, `admin_ert_list`/`_download` [entity='client' only], `vault_tax_list`/`_download` [in-group planners get `can_view=true` + a 300s signed URL]), and `load_notifications`/`mark_notification_read` (scoped to the planner's own-email rows only — gotcha #259). `automation_TAX_sendagreement` is allowlisted BECAUSE the decision/pricing/extra-meeting chains forward the planner's token into it; `automation_TAX_refund`/`_charge_implementation` are PUBLIC_HANDLERS and deliberately NOT allowlisted. Login-setup for a planner reuses the unified Feature-B flow: `send_login_setup_email` + `submit_login_setup` accept `login_type='tax_planner'` (the `login_setup_tokens` CHECK constraint was widened by migration `20260722110000` — gotcha #258).

### License Fee Continuation + Accounting reporting (added 2026-06-30)

Read-only except the two continuation actions. **As of 2026-08-24 nothing in this group is `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`** — the last two (`specialist_license_continuation_start` / `_load`) moved to `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting` with the tool itself, so the accounting grant is now the whole boundary for the group (#338). `accounting_outstanding_links_load` (added 2026-07-29) still superadmin-gates only its SPECREV half, in-handler. The reporting loaders feed the Accounting → Specialists / VFO Services / Members sub-menus; the frontend flattens per-payment cleared rows + filters by year/month client-side.

| Action | File | Role | Notes |
|--------|------|------|-------|

Re-runnable = Resend. **409 `already_active`** if ANY non-canceled `lic_subscription_id` exists on ANY of the expert's rows (JS-filtered — `.neq` drops NULLs). Row reuse restricted to `license_continuation=true` rows. |
| `specialist_license_continuation_load` | `actions/specialist-license/continuation-load.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (same move, 2026-08-24 / v780) | Current license state for one expert, now returning `charge_day` + a computed **`already_active`** (the exact condition `_start` 409s on). Feeds the Accounting-tab setup form; the profile subtab it was built for is gone. |
| `specialist_license_payments_load` | `actions/specialist-license/license-payments-load.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | The `specialist_license_payments` ledger (one row per cleared monthly invoice). **Response gained `pending[]` 2026-08-24 / v781** — continuation rows with a link sent that are not succeeded/canceled, each with `state` ∈ `setup_pending`/`scheduled`/`processing`/`failed`; **unknown values (e.g. `past_due`) pass through raw**. Additive only — the cleared-rows shape is unchanged (#395). |
| `specialist_bg_payments_load` | `actions/specialist-license/bg-payments-load.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | Background-check payments from `specialist_onboarding` (`background_check_type` → Core $350 / Max $950). |

Response shape is otherwise unchanged — extra columns only, no key renamed or removed (#395). |

No writes. **As of 2026-08-04 the panel it feeds is no longer read-only** — see `accounting_resend_first_payment_link` below. |
| `accounting_resend_first_payment_link` | `actions/migration/resend-first-payment-link.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** — the SAME gate as the loader above, deliberately: an admin who can SEE an outstanding row may re-nudge it. (The sibling continuation-row Resend button reuses `migration_send_setup_link`, which **MOVED from `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` to `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting` on 2026-08-07 (v707)** on explicit user request under the full-parity decision (#338) — the user accepted that it mints a Stripe customer + token artifact, and that the same action is also reachable from the per-client Payment Continuation tab, which is therefore widened too. Its in-handler superadmin 403 was removed with it.) | **Added 2026-08-04 (v697).** Manual re-run of the nightly first-payment reminder for ONE row, fired by the **"Resend email"** button on the Outstanding Payment Links panel's **"New Clients - First Payment Link"** section (Holistic + Tax panels only — NOT on tax implementation-links, specrev or membership-fee sections). Body `{ pipeline: 'MAP1'\|'TAX', row_id }`; the pipeline string is normalised (`MAP 1` / `map1` / `MAP1` all accepted) because the panel's section keys and the migration action's spelling differ by a space. **MAP 1** → `buttonsPay1Payment` + `sendStallReminderEmail(client_id, 'CONTRACT_payment_reminder', …, memberPaysOnBehalf)`; **TAX** → `buttonsTax3Payment` + `sendReminderEmailUnified(plan_id, 'TAX_payment_reminder', …)`. **Those four helpers are the sweeps' OWN internals, newly `export`ed with no restructuring** (`actions/pipeline/contract-revshare-sweep.ts` + `actions/tax/revshare-sweep.ts`), so the manual email is byte-identical to the nightly one — same template lookup, same member-pays variant, same planner Cc, same per-client sandbox redirect. **Do not fork them.** **Guards:** 400 once the payment is no longer outstanding (`pay1_status` / `retainer_status` non-null), 400 when the row has no `checkout_token` (rather than drafting a button-less email), 404 on a missing row. **Stamping:** `pay1_reminder_sent_at` / `payment_reminder_sent_at` is written **only when currently null** AND **only after a draft actually succeeded** — only-if-null so a manual nudge suppresses the sweep's imminent duplicate without rewriting the real first-reminder date, only-on-success so a failed draft leaves the sweep armed. **`*_pf_notified_at` is never touched** (the PF escalation ladder keeps its own clock). Gotcha **#330**. |
| `accounting_redraft_installment_link` | `actions/migration/redraft-installment-link.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** — the SAME gate as the loader and `accounting_resend_first_payment_link`, for the same reason: an admin who can SEE the outstanding row may chase it. | **Added 2026-09-04 (v808).** Manual re-draft of the MAP 1 quarterly-installment charge-failure email for ONE row, fired by the **"Resend email"** button on the Outstanding Payment Links panel's new **"Failed Installments - Retry Links"** section (Holistic panel only). Body `{ row_id }` — **no payment number**: the installment is DERIVED server-side by `resolveOpenInstallment()`, the same function the public `/pay` page runs, so the email's *"Complete Payment N"* and the page the client lands on cannot disagree, and a body parameter cannot request an installment the link would not collect. When several installments have failed the resolver returns the LOWEST, which is the only one the link can collect. Calls `utils/map1-installment-failure.ts draftMap1InstallmentFailureEmail` — the sweep's OWN sender — so the manual email is byte-identical to the nightly one (same template, recipients, sandbox redirect and `/pay` button). **Do not fork it.** **Guards, all positive tests (#431):** 404 on a missing row; 400 when the link would collect nothing (`resolveOpenInstallment` returns null); 400 when the collectable installment has NOT failed — `REOPEN_STATUSES` (`declined`/`auth_required`) is now `export`ed from `utils/map1-open-installment.ts` so this gate and the `/pay` page read ONE list; 400 when the row has no `checkout_token`. **Nothing is stamped** — unlike the first-payment link there is no reminder ladder on a failed installment, so no column would be disarmed and the button is deliberately re-clickable. Returns `{ ok, email_drafted, email_sent, row_id, payment_number }`. **WHY IT EXISTS:** `contract-chargescheduled-sweep.ts` charges only an EMPTY slot (#431/#444), so a declined installment is never re-raised and its money is open indefinitely; the one email carrying the recovery link is drafted once, at failure time, from a `send_mode=false` template. Before this action the panel covered every open-money case except that one. Gotcha **#468**. |

Also (no new action): the license `invoice.paid` webhook block uses an atomic invoice-id claim (gotcha #161); `automation_SPECIALIST_licinvoicereceipt` appends to `specialist_license_payments`; `migration_send_setup_link` reads the client setup-link copy from the `CLIENT_PAYMENT_CONTINUATION`/`setup_link` template (**2026-08-04, v697:** it gained an optional **`body.reminder`** — `true` switches the template to `setup_link_reminder` (id 209, "Friendly reminder:") and is what the panel's continuation-row **"Resend email"** button sends; the default path is byte-identical, the token minted is a fresh 7-day one, and `created_by` stays the **operator's** email rather than `'sweep'`, so a manual resend never counts toward the sweep's 3-auto-resend cap and the reminder/bell ladder restarts — gotcha **#330**. **2026-08-03, v696:** its `clients` `.select()` gained `member_number, assigned_pf` and its ctx gained `MEMBER` + `ASSIGNED_PF` — the template had Cc'd both role tokens since creation while the handler supplied neither, so the email had always gone out with an empty Cc; gotcha #324. 2026-07-15 late: body gained a `[PAYMENT_SCHEDULE]` token — handler-built block: MAP 1 = table of unpaid installments (long date + flat per-quarter amount), TAX = single implementation-fee line, omitted when nothing upcoming; the security sentence is now 12px fine print); `loadOnboardings` / `automation_load_specialist_pipelines` / the specialist sweep exclude `license_continuation=true` rows.

---

## Notes & oddities

1. **`automation_CONTRACT_stripewebhook` was removed in Phase 6 mechanical.** It was doubly-dead: real Stripe events had the `stripe-signature` header and were caught by the webhook block; the synthetic-action assignment in `index.ts` (also removed) was unreachable from dispatch because the `action` const was destructured before the mutation. The function returns 401 (no token) or 400 "Unknown action" (with token) for explicit calls.
2. **The duplicate `msm_update_client` handler was removed in Phase 6 mechanical.** Same effect achieved via Map dedupe in `router/dispatch.ts`.
3. **The embedded BoldSign webhook handler in `router/webhooks.ts::maybeHandleBoldSignWebhook` does not chain downstream**, only the standalone `boldsign-webhook` function does. Live BoldSign webhook target should be confirmed (only the standalone function will trigger `automation_CONTRACT_ceocountersign` / `_stripecustomer`).
4. **Action gating is enforced in two places**, both via `constants/role-gates.ts`:
   - `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` array — explicit allowlist of mutations admins may call. Member callers get 403.
   - `MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS` array — explicit list where `member_number` is overwritten with the caller's own.
   Actions not in either list are accessible to both roles without scoping. Notable examples: `gc_redeem`, `ciq_*`, `tax_*`, `coaching_*`. Their security relies on every payload requiring an `id` or `client_id` that the caller already owns — application-level rather than role-level. (NOTE: `add_client_note`/`update_client_note`/`delete_client_note` are NOT in this ungated set — they are in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` (member 403) and, as of 2026-07-23, ALSO in `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` with in-handler group-scope guards — gotcha #273.)
5. **`document_numbers` sequence is not strongly serialized** — `automation_CONTRACT_invoicereceipt` does `SELECT count(*) FROM document_numbers WHERE type=...` then increments. Concurrent invocations could collide. Pre-existing; not a refactor regression.
6. **Three handlers take `req` as a 4th parameter** (`automation_PIPFU_decision`, `automation_PCADMIN_pricing`, `automation_PCADMIN_extrameeting`) so the chain `fetch()` can forward `req.headers.get("Authorization")` to the chained `automation_CONTRACT_sendagreement` action. This preservation is required by the refactor safety rule "never convert server-to-server chain calls from HTTP fetches to direct function calls" — see `.refactor-resume.md` for context.


## Member Membership Fees (`actions/membership/`) — added 2026-07-13

Full flow: [../flows/membership-fees.md](../flows/membership-fees.md). AUTH actions below are superadmin-gated in-handler AND listed in `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting` — **with exactly TWO deliberate exceptions as of 2026-08-04: `membership_plans_load` and `membership_renewal_meeting_outcome` carry NO in-handler superadmin check, so the `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting` grant is their whole gate.** The loader is read-only (an accounting admin may SEE the plans and their renewal-meeting queue), and Rachael Hopson — not a superadmin — is the person who runs renewal meetings and records their outcome. Both exceptions are commented at their sites in `constants/role-gates.ts` and in `plans-load.ts`, so a future reader of #309 does not "restore" a check that was removed on purpose. Every action that MOVES MONEY or mints a Stripe artifact keeps its superadmin check (gotcha #333).

| Action | File | Auth | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| `membership_plan_save` | `actions/membership/plan-save.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | Create/edit (while setup_pending) a plan; computes the whole-dollar per-pull; validates transfer renewal (a 15th). No schedule here — that's generated at first payment. **2026-07-31 (v691):** `prior_payments_made` is **REQUIRED on monthly transfers** (400 *"Enter how many payments they already made this year (0-11)"*; still optional on annual transfers), and `remaining_payments` is **DERIVED server-side as `12 − prior`** — never client-entered, written unconditionally so an edit away from monthly/transfer clears it, and the per-pull credit now spreads over the derived remaining. Gotchas #315/#316. **2026-08-21 (v776):** also accepts an optional **`charge_day` (1–15)** — 400 *"Charge day must be a whole number between 1 and 15"* when present and invalid — written **only** for monthly transfers and **unconditionally** (so an edit to annual or away from transfer clears it). The FE makes it **required** on monthly transfers. This makes plan-save a **second writer** of a column `activate.ts` used to own, and on a monthly transfer its NULL-vs-set state selects between two billing models — gotcha **#429**. |
| `membership_plans_load` | `actions/membership/plans-load.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting` ONLY** | Reconciliation feed: plans by category + nested schedule + live member fields + the MEMBER_MEMBERSHIP sandbox config. **CHANGED 2026-08-04 (v700):** the in-handler `auth.isSuperadmin` check was **REMOVED** — this is a read-only loader and the tab grant is now the whole gate, exactly like `accounting_outstanding_links_load`. It had to move because the panel's read-only cards + the renewal-meeting queue are for accounting-tab admins (Rachael). **SUPERSEDED 2026-08-07 (v707): this is no longer an exception — the ENTIRE `membership_*` family lost its in-handler superadmin checks under the Accounting-tab full-parity decision, and every money control in `MembershipFeesPanel` is now open to an accounting-tab admin (#338).** Also now returns, per plan, `renewal_meetings` (the open/closed meeting requests) and `pauses` (the audit trail) so the panel can render both histories without extra round-trips. Gotcha #333. |
| `membership_plan_cancel` | `actions/membership/plan-cancel.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | `cancel_now` (never-paid plans) / `auto_renew_off` / `auto_renew_on`. |
| `membership_send_setup_link` | `actions/membership/send-setup-link.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | Stripe customer + setup token + drafts MEMBERSHIP_setup_link, MEMBERSHIP_transfer_setup_link, or (2026-08-10 / v714) MEMBERSHIP_transfer_setup_link|annual for an ANNUAL transfer, which collects nothing at the link — #351. **2026-07-31:** `[Remaining Payments]` now quotes the authoritative derived count via `transferLinkPulls()` (it used to be re-derived from the renewal date), and two NEW tokens feed template id 191 — `[Setup Amount]` (the REAL link total, catch-up included) and `[Setup Note]` (" — this covers N months …", empty when there is only one pull). **2026-08-21 (v776): a FOURTH template.** A monthly transfer carrying a `charge_day` gets `MEMBERSHIP_transfer_setup_link|monthly-saveonly` (id **229**, **Draft** mode — deliberately not send-mode like 191/215), because its link collects nothing and the monthly copy's "collected when you complete your setup" sentences are all false for it (same reasoning as #351). Four new tokens, all from `transferDaySchedule()` and never re-derived here: `[Charge Day]` (ordinal), `[Last Charge Date]`, `[First Charge Amount]` (`per_pull × catchUpMonths`), `[Catch Up Note]` (empty unless behind). A **missing** template row degrades gracefully — 200 + `email_skipped`, link still valid. |
| `membership_terminate` | `actions/membership/terminate.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | Charges the termination fee off-session NOW, voids scheduled rows, plan → terminated. |
| `membership_stripe_remap` | `actions/membership/stripe-remap.ts` | **AUTH + `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` — and in NO `TAB_ACTIONS` list at all** (#338: the accounting grant is the whole boundary for that tab, so nothing superadmin-only may live there). **The sole exception in the otherwise fully tab-gated `membership_*` family.** | **Added 2026-09-11 (v829; the customer-only row shape in v830). RUN FOR REAL 2026-09-11 — 37/37 plans moved, 0 left on `vfos`.** Applies Stripe's "Copy PAN data across Stripe accounts" mapping CSV (`vfos` → `ert`) to `member_payment_plans` — note the delivered file has **six** columns and is cut down to four (#486). Body `{ mappings: [{customer_id_old, source_id_old, customer_id_new, source_id_new}], dry_run }`, **max 200 per call**. **The ONLY writer in the codebase permitted to re-stamp a plan's `stripe_account`** — every charge path treats the stamp as read-only, because a Stripe customer id is valid on exactly one account. Per plan it probes ERT **before** writing anything (the payment method must exist there **and** belong to the same customer; the customer must resolve), then does a guarded update `where stripe_account is null or = 'vfos'` setting `stripe_account='ert'` + `default_payment_method_id`. **Two row shapes:** the normal `cus_x,pm_old,cus_x,pm_new` mapping, and a **customer-only** `cus_x,,cus_x,` row (v830) that moves a `setup_pending` plan with no saved method — keeping the copied ERT customer or minting a fresh one, the one case `stripe_customer_id` is rewritten, refused as `not_pending` on any other plan. Outcomes (12): `moved` / `would_move` / `already_ert` / `no_plan` / `not_pending` / `customer_id_changed` / `pm_mismatch` / `ert_pm_not_found` / `ert_pm_wrong_customer` / `ert_customer_not_found` / `race_lost` / `error`. Also sets the ERT customer's invoice default (cosmetic, logged, non-fatal). **Never touches** `stripe_customer_id`, `sandbox`, `payment_method_type`, `acct_last4` or any `member_payment_schedule` row. **No email, no bell, no charge.** Idempotent — a re-submitted CSV reports `already_ert`. Frontend: the collapsed superadmin-only "Move plans to ERT Stripe" section in `MembershipFeesPanel.jsx`, gated on `getSession()?.is_superadmin` (deliberately NOT the `isSuperadmin` prop, which is `canSeeTab('accounting')` at that mount), dry run required before Apply on the exact same text. **CHANGED 2026-09-11 (later the same day): that frontend section has been DELETED from `MembershipFeesPanel.jsx`.** The action remains registered and gated but now has **NO frontend caller** — direct API call only. It is kept because it is the only writer allowed to re-stamp `member_payment_plans.stripe_account`. Full flow: [../flows/membership-fees.md](../flows/membership-fees.md). |
| `membership_pause` | `actions/membership/pause.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | **Added 2026-08-04 (v702; a label fix in v703).** Pauses a plan's payments for **N months (1–12)**. Body `{ plan_id, months, preview }`. **A pause is schedule-shape + `renewal_date`, and NOTHING else — the plan stays `status='active'`**, so no consumer that keys off `plan.status` learns a new state. Effects: every `kind='membership'` `status='scheduled'` row shifts **+N months** (applied in **DESCENDING `due_date` order** so the partial unique `(plan_id, due_date) WHERE kind='membership'` never sees a transient collision — #315); `period_label` is regenerated **only when it still equals `periodLabel(old due)`**, so annotated catch-up / annual labels are preserved verbatim; `year_end` extends +N on **all** rows of the affected `year_start` group while **`year_start` is never touched** (#280); `renewal_date` advances +N pinned to the 15th, which is what keeps #235 (every year row strictly before renewal) true. The vacated slots get `kind='pause'` marker rows (`status='waived'`, `amount_due` 0) labelled *"Paused — August 2026"* per month, or **ONE combined row** *"Paused — Aug 2026 – Oct 2026 (3 mo)"* — a single-month window renders *"Paused — Aug 2026 (1 mo)"* (v703). With no scheduled rows left to move (an annual plan mid-year, or a fully-collected monthly year) the marker anchors at the OLD renewal date and `resume_due_date` is NULL. Writes one `member_payment_plan_pauses` audit row. **`preview: true` computes the identical result and writes NOTHING** — it is what the panel auto-calls to render "Payments continue on X / Renewal date moves X → Y". **`renewal_notice_for` is deliberately left stale** so the notice pass re-arms for the new renewal date. Partial-failure errors state exactly what was and was not applied. Gotcha **#332**. |
| `membership_renewal_meeting_outcome` | `actions/membership/renewal-meeting-outcome.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting` ONLY — deliberately NOT superadmin** (never had an in-handler check; as of 2026-08-07 / v707 that is the norm for the whole `membership_*` family rather than an exception — #338) | **Added 2026-08-04 (v700; response-shape fix v701).** Closes out a renewal-meeting request: stamps `completed_at`, `outcome` (`'continue'` \| `'cancel'`) and `recorded_by`. **`cancel`** additionally flips `auto_renew=false` and drafts `MEMBERSHIP_cancel_confirmation` (best-effort `try/catch` — a Gmail failure must not lose the recorded outcome). **BOTH outcomes clear the action-required bell** (matched on pipeline + `dismissible=false` + `read=false` + `link LIKE %member=N%`). Rachael Hopson runs these meetings, which is why the tab grant is the whole gate. **Returns `{ ok: true }`, not `{ success: true }`** — `MembershipFeesPanel`'s `run()` helper reads `res.ok`, and returning the wrong flag makes a committed write read as "action failed" in the UI (that happened live and was fixed in v701; gotcha #333). |
| `membership_next_year_save` | `actions/membership/next-year-save.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | Sets/clears the renewal-time terms (amount + credit) on an active plan. |
| `membership_send_reminder` | `actions/membership/send-reminder.ts` | **AUTH + `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`** (in-handler superadmin 403 REMOVED 2026-08-07 / v707 — #338) | On-demand re-send of the MEMBERSHIP_payment_failed email covering the plan's FULL missed/declined arrears (the sweep emails each missed month only once); stamps `reminder_sent_at`. Rejects plans whose overdue rows are all still `scheduled` (nightly sweep's job). Email-only — no Stripe call. |
| `membership_setup_load` | `actions/membership/setup-load.ts` | PUBLIC (token) | /membership-pay page data; active plans return update_mode (save-only method change). **2026-07-31:** the amount shown is `base × pullsAtLink`, computed LIVE from `transferLinkPulls()`, so a late payer always sees the then-correct figure. **2026-08-21 (v776):** `saveOnly` now ALSO covers **a monthly transfer carrying a `charge_day`** (nothing is collected at its link), and the response gains eight **additive** fields for that case — `charge_day`, `charge_day_ordinal`, `per_pull_amount`, `payments_remaining`, `first_charge_date`, `last_charge_date`, `first_charge_amount`, `first_charge_months`. **The page deliberately renders none of the figures** (see [flows/membership-fees.md](../flows/membership-fees.md) step 3) — they exist so nothing downstream has to re-derive the schedule. |
| `membership_setup_checkout` | `actions/membership/setup-checkout.ts` | PUBLIC (token) | Stripe Checkout: mode=payment charging the first pull + saving the method (mode=setup for annual transfers / $0 plans / method updates). **2026-07-31:** on a monthly transfer `mode=payment` charges `pullsAtLink × base` (the New Model card gross-up wraps the TOTAL), the memo appends " (includes N-month catch-up)", and `pull_count` is stamped on **both** the session and `payment_intent_data` — `router/webhooks.ts` reads it to keep the card-fee base and the ACH confirmation amount right (#281). **2026-08-21 (v776):** the `saveOnly` predicate gained a fourth trigger — **a monthly transfer with a `charge_day`** — so those links mint `mode=setup` and collect nothing; their whole year is `scheduled` on that day by `activate.ts`. |
| `membership_renewal_meeting_request` | `actions/membership/renewal-meeting-request.ts` | **PUBLIC (token)** | **Added 2026-08-04 (v700).** The "Request a Meeting" button in the 30-day advance renewal notice email, backing the public `/membership-meeting?token=` page. **`member_payment_plans.renewal_meeting_token` is the WHOLE credential** (partial-unique, minted by the notice pass and persisted BEFORE the email is drafted), so — exactly like `vault_tax_upload_context` (#331) — the response is a **minimal `{ success }` payload that must NEVER be widened**: an unauthenticated endpoint whose only key sits in an old email must not disclose the member, the plan or the terms. A file comment states the rule. **Idempotent** per `(plan_id, renewal_date_for)` open row, so a double-click or a re-opened email cannot queue two meetings (the page renders the `already` state instead). Inserts a `membership_renewal_meetings` row and fires `notifyByRule('MEMBERSHIP_renewal_meeting_requested')` — **action-required (`dismissible:false`)**, pipeline `MEMBER_MEMBERSHIP`, link `/admin?tab=accounting&section=<category>_membership_fees&member=<n>`, default recipient `rhopson@elitert.com`. Gotcha **#333**. |
| `automation_MEMBERSHIP_sweep` | `actions/membership/sweep.ts` | PUBLIC (service-role Bearer) | Daily engine (cron jobid 16 @12:00 UTC). **FIVE passes as of 2026-08-04 (v700)** — a NEW **pass 0, renewal notices**, runs FIRST so a plan is always warned before it is rolled over: renewal notices → renewals → waive $0 → combined due+arrears charges → auto-unsuspend. **Pass 0** selects `status='active'` + `auto_renew` plans whose `renewal_date` falls in `(today, today+30]` — **strictly future**, so a plan that actually reaches its 15th is renewed by pass 1 rather than noticed — and skips any whose `renewal_notice_for` already equals that `renewal_date` (that comparison is done in JS because PostgREST cannot compare two columns, and NULL must read as not-noticed). It mints/reuses the durable `renewal_meeting_token`, **persisting it BEFORE the draft** so the link always resolves, drafts `MEMBERSHIP_renewal_notice` (id 212, Draft mode) with `[First Name]` / `[Renewal Date]` / `[Renewal Terms]` / `[MEETING_LINK]`, and **stamps `renewal_notice_for` ONLY after the draft succeeds** — so any failure simply retries tomorrow and a plan can never be double-noticed for one renewal. `[Renewal Terms]` = `(next_year_amount ?? annual_amount) − (next_year_credit_note ?? 0)`, **byte-for-byte the arithmetic pass 1 will apply** (a mismatch between the two was found in review and fixed before deploy — if you change one, change the other). No per-plan Stripe-mode guard (the pass touches no Stripe object); email sandboxing rides `resolveTemplateRecipients` as everywhere else. Per-plan `try/catch` isolation. Summary gains `noticed`. Gotcha **#333**. **NEW 2026-08-24 — pass 4 chains:** after clearing `membership_suspended` it re-reads the member row and, only when `memberHoldReason` finds no remaining reason (the manual `suspended`/`paused` toggles hold independently, #240), calls `releaseHeldMemberPayouts` — HTTP chains into `automation_CONTRACT_revshare` / `automation_TAX_revshare` / `automation_PIP_revshare` / `specialist_revenue_payout`. Summary gains `payouts_released`. |
| `automation_MEMBERSHIP_invoicereceipt` | `actions/membership/invoice-receipt.ts` | PUBLIC (service-role Bearer) | Webhook-chained once a membership payment CLEARS. Derives from the ledger whether this is a year opener (invoice + receipt) or a later pull (receipt only), renders both PDFs, drafts the email with them attached, and files them in the `member-ert-docs` ERT vault. Idempotent on `docs_emailed_at` (gotcha #280). |

Shared helpers: `actions/membership/shared.ts` (whole-dollar rounding, charge-day/renewal-15th math, schedule builders, `cardFeeFor` + date/ordinal formatters) and `actions/membership/activate.ts` (webhook-called activation — generates the year ledger at first payment, stamps `year_start`/`year_end`, returns `activated|refreshed|failed`; method-only refresh for active plans). **`shared.ts` gained the fixed-charge-day family 2026-08-21 (v776):** `dayOccurrencesAfter()`, module-private `placeMonths()`, **`transferDaySchedule(plan, basisDate)`** — the SINGLE source every surface calls, returning **null** for anything that is not a monthly transfer with a valid 1–15 day, which is itself the legacy switch (#429) — and `buildTransferDayRows()`. Basis-date dependent like `transferLinkPulls` (#349). `activate.ts` gained `chargeDayOut` so a day-carrying plan keeps the ADMIN's day rather than re-deriving one from the pay date; its legacy path is byte-identical. Document rendering lives in `utils/membership-html-templates.ts`; the first-signup confirmation email in `utils/membership-confirmation.ts`. Webhook blocks in `router/webhooks.ts` route by `metadata.pipeline='MEMBER_MEMBERSHIP'` + `payment_kind` (gotcha #216) and chain the invoice/receipt handler from three points (gotcha #280).
